<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜あまり〜ない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜あまり〜ない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not very / not much</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">あまり + negative</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Softens a negative. ぜんぜん〜ない is the absolute version, &quot;not at all&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あまり<ruby>高<rt>たか</rt></ruby>くないです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s not very expensive.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜あります</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜あります</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">there is / exists (inanimate)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + が + ある</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">For objects, plants and abstractions. Use いる for people and animals.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>机<rt>つくえ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>上<rt>うえ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>があります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is a book on the desk.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたと<ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>考<rt>かんが</rt></ruby>えには<ruby>違<rt>ちが</rt></ruby>いがありますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Is there any difference between your idea and hers?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたの<ruby>報告<rt>ほうこく</rt></ruby><ruby>書<rt>しょ</rt></ruby>には<ruby>少<rt>すこ</rt></ruby>し<ruby>間違<rt>まちが</rt></ruby>いがあります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There are a few mistakes in your report.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたは<ruby>今<rt>いま</rt></ruby>まで<ruby>鯨<rt>くじら</rt></ruby>を<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>たことがありますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Have you ever seen a whale?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜いくら</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜いくら</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">how much (price)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">いくら + です</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">For cost. Use いくつ for counting things and 何歳 for age.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">これはいくらですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How much is this?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜いつ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜いつ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">when</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">いつ + verb</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Takes no particle before です. いつも means &quot;always&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">いつ<ruby>日本<rt>にっぽん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>来<rt>き</rt></ruby>ましたか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">When did you come to Japan?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜います</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜います</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">there is / exists (animate)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + が + いる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">For people and animals. Also forms the progressive with て-form.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>部屋<rt>へや</rt></ruby>に<ruby>猫<rt>ねこ</rt></ruby>がいます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is a cat in the room.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたのクラスには<ruby>何人<rt>なんにん</rt></ruby>の<ruby>生徒<rt>せいと</rt></ruby>がいますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How many pupils are there in your class?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">マイクはシカゴに<ruby>住<rt>す</rt></ruby>んでいる<ruby>友達<rt>ともだち</rt></ruby>がいます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Mike has a friend who lives in Chicago.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ルーシーには<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>と<ruby>同<rt>おな</rt></ruby>じぐらい<ruby>友達<rt>ともだち</rt></ruby>がいます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Lucy has as many friends as I do.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜か</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜か</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">question marker</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Sentence + か</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Turns a polite sentence into a question; no question mark is needed in Japanese.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>学生<rt>がくせい</rt></ruby>ですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Are you a student?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かった</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かった</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">was ~ (い-adjective past)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">い-adjective (drop い) + かった</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Negative past is 〜くなかった. いい becomes よかった.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>昨日<rt>きのう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>楽<rt>たの</rt></ruby>しかったです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Yesterday was fun.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">とても<ruby>良<rt>よ</rt></ruby>かったです。また<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きたいですね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It was very exciting! I&#x27;d like to go again.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>昨夜<rt>さくや</rt></ruby>のパーティーはとても<ruby>楽<rt>たの</rt></ruby>しかったです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I enjoyed myself at the party last night.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>の<ruby>眼鏡<rt>めがね</rt></ruby>をどこかで<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>かけなかったですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Have you seen my glasses anywhere?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜から (reason)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜から (reason)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">because ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / です・ます form + から</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">States a reason, often a subjective one. The reason precedes the result.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>寒<rt>さむ</rt></ruby>いから、<ruby>窓<rt>まど</rt></ruby>を<ruby>閉<rt>し</rt></ruby>めます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Because it&#x27;s cold, I&#x27;ll close the window.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あの<ruby>日<rt>ひ</rt></ruby><ruby>帰<rt>かえ</rt></ruby>ったのは<ruby>体調<rt>たいちょう</rt></ruby>が<ruby>悪<rt>わる</rt></ruby>かったからです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is because the physical condition was bad that it returned that day.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そして<ruby>何時<rt>なんじ</rt></ruby><ruby>間<rt>ま</rt></ruby>もいっしょに<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>したからです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">And we had spent hours talking together.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">なぜなら<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>偉大<rt>いだい</rt></ruby>な<ruby>音楽家<rt>おんがくか</rt></ruby>だったからです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Because he was a great musician.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜から〜まで</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜から〜まで</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">from ~ until ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + から + Noun + まで</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Works for both time and place: 九時から五時まで, 東京から大阪まで.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>九時<rt>くじ</rt></ruby>から<ruby>五時<rt>ごじ</rt></ruby>まで<ruby>働<rt>はたら</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I work from nine to five.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここから<ruby>図書館<rt>としょかん</rt></ruby>までどれくらいありますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How far is it to the library from here?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここから<ruby>学校<rt>がっこう</rt></ruby>までどのくらいの<ruby>距離<rt>きょり</rt></ruby>ですか。</div><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここから<ruby>東京<rt>とうきょう</rt></ruby>までどのくらいの<ruby>距離<rt>きょり</rt></ruby>ですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How far is it from here to Tokyo?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜が</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜が</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">subject marker</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + が</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks the grammatical subject, and the object of 好き, 嫌い, 上手, ほしい and potential verbs.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>猫<rt>ねこ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>好<rt>す</rt></ruby>きです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I like cats.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜が (but)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜が (but)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">but / however</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Sentence + が + Sentence</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Connects contrasting clauses, a little more formal than けど. <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がいちばん</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がいちばん</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">~ is the most / best</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + がいちばん + adjective</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The superlative. <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がほしい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がほしい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">want (a thing)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + がほしい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Desire for an object, not an action. <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜が上手・下手</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜が<ruby>上手<rt>じょうず</rt></ruby>・<ruby>下手<rt>へた</rt></ruby></div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">good at / bad at</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + が + 上手 / 下手</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Takes が, not を. 上手 is not normally used about oneself; prefer 得意.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>料理<rt>りょうり</rt></ruby>が<ruby>上手<rt>じょうず</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He is good at cooking.</div></div>	jlpt::N5

The listed qualities should agree in tone — do not mix praise and criticism.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>部屋<rt>へや</rt></ruby>は<ruby>広<rt>ひろ</rt></ruby>くて<ruby>明<rt>あか</rt></ruby>るいです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This room is spacious and bright.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜くない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜くない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not ~ (い-adjective negative)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">い-adjective (drop い) + くない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">いい is irregular: よくない. <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜くなる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜くなる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">become ~ (い-adjective)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">い-adjective (drop い) + くなる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks a change of state. な-adjectives and nouns take になる instead.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>寒<rt>さむ</rt></ruby>くなりました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It has gotten cold.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ぐらい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ぐらい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">about / approximately</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Quantity + ぐらい / くらい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Approximates amounts and durations. ごろ is used instead for points in time.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一時<rt>いちじ</rt></ruby><ruby>間<rt>ま</rt></ruby>ぐらい<ruby>待<rt>ま</rt></ruby>ちました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I waited about an hour.</div></div>	jlpt::N5

Polite alternatives: こちら, そちら, あちら.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここは<ruby>駅<rt>えき</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This place is the station.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことができます</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことができます</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">can ~ / be able to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + ことができる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Ability or possibility. The potential verb form (読める) is shorter and more common in speech.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>日本語<rt>にほんご</rt></ruby>を<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>すことができます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I can speak Japanese.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたは<ruby>目<rt>め</rt></ruby>を<ruby>閉<rt>と</rt></ruby>じて<ruby>歩<rt>ある</rt></ruby>くことができますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Can you walk with your eyes closed?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたは<ruby>誰<rt>だれ</rt></ruby>とでも<ruby>自由<rt>じゆう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>すことができる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You are free to talk with anyone.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">このエレベーターは<ruby>十人<rt>じゅうにん</rt></ruby><ruby>乗<rt>の</rt></ruby>ることができる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This elevator is capable of carrying ten persons.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜この・その・あの</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜この・その・あの</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">this ~ / that ~ / that ~ over there</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">この + Noun</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Always attach to a noun. Never say このは.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>は<ruby>面白<rt>おもしろ</rt></ruby>いです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This book is interesting.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜これ・それ・あれ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜これ・それ・あれ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">this one / that one / that one over there</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Standalone pronoun</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">これ is near the speaker, それ near the listener, あれ away from both. These stand alone; この etc. modify a noun.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">これは<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>ですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">What is this?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ごろ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ごろ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">around (a point in time)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Time + ごろ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">For clock times and dates. Use ぐらい for durations and quantities.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>三時<rt>さんじ</rt></ruby>ごろ<ruby>来<rt>き</rt></ruby>てください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please come around three o&#x27;clock.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜しか〜ない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜しか〜ない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">only ~ (and that&#x27;s not enough)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + しか + negative</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Always with a negative verb, carrying a sense of shortage.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">だけ is neutral; しか implies the amount falls short.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>千<rt>せん</rt></ruby><ruby>円<rt>えん</rt></ruby>しかありません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I only have a thousand yen.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一人<rt>ひとり</rt></ruby>しか<ruby>来<rt>こ</rt></ruby>なかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Only one person came.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>たちには<ruby>水<rt>みず</rt></ruby>が<ruby>少<rt>すこ</rt></ruby>ししかありませんでした。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We had little water.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">これをお<ruby>任<rt>まか</rt></ruby>せできるのは、あなたしかありません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is none other than you to whom I can leave this.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ずつ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ずつ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">each / at a time</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Quantity + ずつ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Distributes an amount evenly: 一つずつ, 少しずつ.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一人<rt>ひとり</rt></ruby>に<ruby>二<rt>ふた</rt></ruby>つずつ<ruby>配<rt>くば</rt></ruby>ります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ll hand out two to each person.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ぜんぜん〜ない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ぜんぜん〜ない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not at all</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">ぜんぜん + negative</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A complete negative. In casual speech it sometimes appears with positives, but avoid that in exams.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>全然<rt>ぜんぜん</rt></ruby><ruby>分<rt>わ</rt></ruby>かりません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I don&#x27;t understand at all.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たいです</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たいです</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">want to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + たい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Conjugates as an い-adjective. The object may take が or を. Only for the speaker&#x27;s own desire; use 〜たがっている for others.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>日本<rt>にっぽん</rt></ruby>へ<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きたいです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I want to go to Japan.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">とても<ruby>良<rt>よ</rt></ruby>かったです。また<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きたいですね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It was very exciting! I&#x27;d like to go again.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">メアリーは<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>が<ruby>好<rt>す</rt></ruby>きではないみたいですね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Mary scarcely seems to care for me, does she?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたは<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>たちの<ruby>英語<rt>えいご</rt></ruby>の<ruby>授業<rt>じゅぎょう</rt></ruby>を<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>たいですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Do you want to see our English lesson?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たことがあります</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たことがあります</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">have ~ed before (experience)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form + ことがある</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Past experience, not a simple past action. Often paired with 一度, 二回 and similar.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>日本<rt>にっぽん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>ったことがあります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have been to Japan before.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたに<ruby>以前<rt>いぜん</rt></ruby><ruby>会<rt>あ</rt></ruby>ったことがあるように<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>う。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I fancy we&#x27;ve met before.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたは<ruby>今<rt>いま</rt></ruby>まで<ruby>鯨<rt>くじら</rt></ruby>を<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>たことがありますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Have you ever seen a whale?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたは<ruby>北海道<rt>ほっかいどう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>ったことがありますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Have you been to Hokkaido?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たり〜たりします</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たり〜たりします</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">do things like ~ and ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form + り + Verb た-form + り + する</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Lists representative actions without implying order or completeness.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>日曜日<rt>にちようび</rt></ruby>は<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>読<rt>よ</rt></ruby>んだり、<ruby>映画<rt>えいが</rt></ruby>を<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>たりします。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">On Sundays I read books, watch movies and so on.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だけ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だけ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">only / just</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / Verb plain form + だけ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Limits quantity or scope neutrally. しか〜ない carries a stronger sense of insufficiency.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>水<rt>みず</rt></ruby>だけ<ruby>飲<rt>の</rt></ruby>みました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I drank only water.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だれ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だれ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">who</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">だれ + が / の / に</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">どなた is the polite form. だれか is &quot;someone&quot;, だれも with a negative is &quot;no one&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あの<ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>はだれですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Who is that person?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ちょっと</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ちょっと</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">a little / a bit</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">ちょっと + adjective / verb</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Also softens refusals: ちょっと… on its own politely declines.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここでちょっと<ruby>待<rt>ま</rt></ruby>っていてくださいますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Could you wait here for the moment?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">このあたりでちょっと<ruby>待<rt>ま</rt></ruby>っていてください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please wait around for a while.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ています</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ています</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">is ~ing / is in a state</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + いる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Two readings: an action in progress (本を読んでいる), or a resulting state (結婚している, 知っている).</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今<rt>いま</rt></ruby>、<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>読<rt>よ</rt></ruby>んでいます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I am reading a book now.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">「<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>かご<ruby>用<rt>よう</rt></ruby>は？」「ドレスを<ruby>探<rt>さが</rt></ruby>しています」</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">May I help you? &quot;Yes, I&#x27;m looking for a dress.&quot;</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたが<ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>だということを<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>っています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I know that you are a teacher.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたが<ruby>早<rt>はや</rt></ruby>くよくなるように<ruby>祈<rt>いの</rt></ruby>っています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We are hoping for your quick recovery.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ておく</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ておく</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">do ~ in advance / leave as is</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + おく</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Preparation for a later need. Contracts to 〜とく in speech.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ておく prepares; てある describes the resulting state.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>会議<rt>かいぎ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>前<rt>まえ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>資料<rt>しりょう</rt></ruby>を<ruby>読<rt>よ</rt></ruby>んでおきます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ll read the documents before the meeting.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ビールを<ruby>冷<rt>ひ</rt></ruby>やしておいた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I chilled the beer in advance.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">こちらからシーガル<ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>に<ruby>電話<rt>でんわ</rt></ruby>しておきます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We&#x27;ll call Dr. Shiegal for you.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたの<ruby>時計<rt>とけい</rt></ruby>は<ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby>までには<ruby>直<rt>なお</rt></ruby>しておきますよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I will have repaired your watch by tomorrow.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てから</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てから</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">after ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + から</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Emphasises sequence: the second action follows the first.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>宿題<rt>しゅくだい</rt></ruby>をしてから、テレビを<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>ます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">After I do my homework, I watch TV.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てください</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てください</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">please do ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + ください</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A request or instruction. Negative: 〜ないでください.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ちょっと<ruby>待<rt>ま</rt></ruby>ってください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please wait a moment.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたが<ruby>町<rt>まち</rt></ruby>に<ruby>来<rt>き</rt></ruby>たらすぐに<ruby>訪<rt>たず</rt></ruby>ねてください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please visit as soon as you get into town.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたが<ruby>落<rt>お</rt></ruby>ち<ruby>着<rt>つ</rt></ruby>いたら<ruby>電話<rt>でんわ</rt></ruby>をしてください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please phone me when you have settled down.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたの<ruby>後<rt>のち</rt></ruby>でコショウを<ruby>使<rt>つか</rt></ruby>わせてください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">After you with the pepper.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てくださいませんか</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てくださいませんか</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">could you please ~?</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + くださいませんか</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A politer request than てください.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もう<ruby>一度<rt>いちど</rt></ruby><ruby>説明<rt>せつめい</rt></ruby>してくださいませんか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Could you please explain once more?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>を<ruby>門<rt>もん</rt></ruby>まで<ruby>連<rt>つ</rt></ruby>れて<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>ってくださいませんか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Will you take me to the gate?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">「<ruby>手伝<rt>てつだ</rt></ruby>ってくださいませんか」「<ruby>喜<rt>よろこ</rt></ruby>んで」</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Will you help me? &quot;I&#x27;ll be glad to.&quot;</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたの<ruby>辞書<rt>じしょ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>貸<rt>か</rt></ruby>してくださいませんか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Will you lend me your dictionary?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てしまう</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てしまう</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">end up ~ing / do completely</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + しまう</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Completion, or regret at an unintended result. Contracts to ちゃう.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">With regret it pairs with unintentional verbs; with completion, with 全部 and similar.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>電車<rt>でんしゃ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>傘<rt>かさ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>忘<rt>わす</rt></ruby>れてしまいました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I left my umbrella on the train.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>全部<rt>ぜんぶ</rt></ruby><ruby>読<rt>よ</rt></ruby>んでしまった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I finished reading the whole book.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">このミルクは<ruby>少<rt>すこ</rt></ruby>し<ruby>古<rt>ふる</rt></ruby>くなってしまいました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The milk&#x27;s a bit off.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>娘<rt>むすめ</rt></ruby>がマッチで<ruby>指<rt>ゆび</rt></ruby>をやけどしてしまいました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Our daughter burned her finger with a match.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てはいけません</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てはいけません</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">must not ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + はいけません</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A firm prohibition, typical of rules and signs. Casual contraction: 〜ちゃいけない.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここでたばこを<ruby>吸<rt>す</rt></ruby>ってはいけません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You must not smoke here.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>客様<rt>きゃくさま</rt></ruby>を<ruby>外<rt>そと</rt></ruby>に<ruby>待<rt>ま</rt></ruby>たせておいてはいけないよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You mustn&#x27;t keep your guest waiting outside.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>問題<rt>もんだい</rt></ruby>をいいかげんに<ruby>扱<rt>あつか</rt></ruby>ってはいけない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Don&#x27;t trifle with the matter.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>授業<rt>じゅぎょう</rt></ruby><ruby>中<rt>ちゅう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>日本語<rt>にほんご</rt></ruby>を<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>してはいけません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You must not speak Japanese during the class.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てみる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てみる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">try doing ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + みる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Do it to find out what happens. Written in kana.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">てみる is trying out; 〜(よ)うとする is attempting.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>新<rt>あたら</rt></ruby>しい<ruby>店<rt>みせ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>ってみます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ll try going to the new shop.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>服<rt>ふく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>着<rt>き</rt></ruby>てみてもいいですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">May I try on these clothes?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">では<ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby>その<ruby>事<rt>こと</rt></ruby>について<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>尋<rt>たず</rt></ruby>ねてみます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I will ask him about it tomorrow, then.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたがたのうちどちらが<ruby>先<rt>さき</rt></ruby>にやってみますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Which of you will try first?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てもいいです</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てもいいです</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">may ~ / it&#x27;s all right to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + もいい(です)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Grants permission; 〜てもいいですか asks for it.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここに<ruby>座<rt>すわ</rt></ruby>ってもいいですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">May I sit here?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたの<ruby>散歩<rt>さんぽ</rt></ruby>についていってもいいですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">May I accompany you on your walk?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたもそろそろ<ruby>結婚<rt>けっこん</rt></ruby>してもいい<ruby>頃<rt>ころ</rt></ruby>ですよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is about time you married.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>建物<rt>たてもの</rt></ruby>の<ruby>中<rt>なか</rt></ruby>で<ruby>写真<rt>しゃしん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>撮<rt>と</rt></ruby>ってもいいですか？</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Is it all right to take pictures in this building?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜で (joining nouns / な-adjectives)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜で (joining nouns / な-adjectives)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">~ and</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / な-adjective + で</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The equivalent of くて for nouns and な-adjectives: 静かできれいです.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>親切<rt>しんせつ</rt></ruby>で<ruby>有名<rt>ゆうめい</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He is kind and famous.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜で (means)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜で (means)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">by / with / using</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + で</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks the tool, method or transport used: バスで, はしで, 日本語で.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">バスで<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I go by bus.</div></div><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>万<rt>まん</rt></ruby><ruby>一<rt>いち</rt></ruby><ruby>車<rt>しゃ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>調子<rt>ちょうし</rt></ruby>が<ruby>悪<rt>わる</rt></ruby>かったら、バスで<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div walk or take the bus?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜で (place)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜で (place)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">at / in (where an action happens)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Place + で + action verb</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks where an action takes place. Compare に, which marks where something exists.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>図書館<rt>としょかん</rt></ruby>で<ruby>勉強<rt>べんきょう</rt></ruby>します。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I study at the library.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜でした</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜でした</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">was / were</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / な-adjective + でした</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Polite past copula.
