<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜でしょう</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜でしょう</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">probably / right?</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + でしょう</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">With falling intonation it is a guess; with rising intonation it seeks agreement.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby>は<ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>でしょう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It will probably rain tomorrow.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あきらめた<ruby>方<rt>ほう</rt></ruby>が<ruby>良<rt>よ</rt></ruby>いのではないでしょうか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You may as well give up.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あそこに<ruby>大<rt>おお</rt></ruby>きな<ruby>白<rt>しろ</rt></ruby>いビルが<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>えるでしょう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Can you see the big white building over there?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたが<ruby>英語<rt>えいご</rt></ruby>を<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>すことは<ruby>難<rt>むずか</rt></ruby>しいでしょう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It will be hard for you to speak English.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜です</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜です</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">is / are (polite statement)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / な-adjective + です</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The polite copula. Plain form is だ. Negative: 〜ではありません / 〜じゃないです.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>学生<rt>がくせい</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I am a student.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ではありません</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ではありません</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">is not / are not</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / な-adjective + ではありません</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Polite negative copula. じゃありません and じゃないです are the everyday spoken forms.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">それは<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>の<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>ではありません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">That is not my book.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あまりピアノは<ruby>上手<rt>じょうず</rt></ruby>ではありませんでした。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She was not a very good pianist.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">それはあなたの<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>ったことではありません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">That&#x27;s none of your business.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はあまり<ruby>疲<rt>つか</rt></ruby>れているようではありません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He does not seem to be very tired.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜でも</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜でも</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">but / however (sentence-initial)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Sentence. でも + Sentence</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Begins a new sentence with a contrast. Attached to a noun it instead means &quot;even ~&quot; or &quot;~ or something&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>疲<rt>つか</rt></ruby>れました。でも、<ruby>楽<rt>たの</rt></ruby>しかったです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I was tired. But it was fun.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜と (and)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜と (and)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">and (exhaustive list of nouns)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + と + Noun</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Joins nouns only, and implies the list is complete. Use や for an incomplete list.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">パンと<ruby>牛乳<rt>ぎゅうにゅう</rt></ruby>を<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>いました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I bought bread and milk.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜と (with)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜と (with)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">together with</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Person + と + verb</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks the person you do something with. 一人で means alone.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>友達<rt>ともだち</rt></ruby>と<ruby>映画<rt>えいが</rt></ruby>を<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>ました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I watched a movie with a friend.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そして、その<ruby>日<rt>ひ</rt></ruby><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>らはイエスと<ruby>一緒<rt>いっしょ</rt></ruby>にいた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">And spent that day with Him.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もし<ruby>来<rt>き</rt></ruby>たければ、<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>たちと<ruby>一緒<rt>いっしょ</rt></ruby>にきなさい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If you care to, come with us.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今日<rt>きょう</rt></ruby>あなたと<ruby>一緒<rt>いっしょ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>けるといいんですが。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I wish I could go with you today.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜という</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜という</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">called / named ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + という + Noun</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Introduces a name new to the listener.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">という names; と言う quotes speech.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>田中<rt>たなか</rt></ruby>という<ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>から<ruby>電話<rt>でんわ</rt></ruby>がありました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There was a call from a person called Tanaka.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">「<ruby>花<rt>はな</rt></ruby>」という<ruby>映画<rt>えいが</rt></ruby>を<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>ました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I watched a film called &quot;Hana&quot;.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>木村<rt>きむら</rt></ruby>さんという<ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>からあなたにお<ruby>電話<rt>でんわ</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">A Mr Kimura is calling you.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>木村<rt>きむら</rt></ruby>さんという<ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>にパーティーで<ruby>会<rt>あ</rt></ruby>ったよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I met a Mr Kimura at the party.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とき</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とき</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">when ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / な-adjective な / Noun の + とき</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Non-past before とき means the main action happens first; past means it happened before.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>どものとき、よく<ruby>公園<rt>こうえん</rt></ruby>で<ruby>遊<rt>あそ</rt></ruby>びました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">When I was a child, I often played in the park.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とても</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とても</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">very</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">とても + adjective</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Neutral intensifier. あまり with a negative means &quot;not very&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>料理<rt>りょうり</rt></ruby>はとてもおいしいです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This dish is very delicious.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜どうして・なぜ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜どうして・なぜ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">why</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">どうして / なぜ + clause</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Answered with 〜から or 〜ので. なぜ is more formal.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">どうして<ruby>遅<rt>おく</rt></ruby>れましたか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Why were you late?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">どうしてこの<ruby>仕事<rt>しごと</rt></ruby>を<ruby>辞<rt>や</rt></ruby>めることができよう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How can I quit this job?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">どうしてそれを<ruby>信<rt>しん</rt></ruby>じるようになりましたか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How did you come to believe that?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">どうしてそれを<ruby>耳<rt>みみ</rt></ruby>にするようになったのか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How did you come to hear of it?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜どこ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜どこ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">where</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">どこ + に / で / へ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">どこか means &quot;somewhere&quot;; どこも with a negative means &quot;nowhere&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>駅<rt>えき</rt></ruby>はどこですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Where is the station?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜どんな</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜どんな</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">what kind of</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">どんな + Noun</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Always modifies a noun. どう asks &quot;how&quot; about a situation instead.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">どんな<ruby>音楽<rt>おんがく</rt></ruby>が<ruby>好<rt>す</rt></ruby>きですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">What kind of music do you like?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないで</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないで</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">without ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-form + で</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Describes doing one thing without doing another: 朝ご飯を食べないで出かけた.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>朝<rt>あさ</rt></ruby>ご<ruby>飯<rt>はん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べないで<ruby>学校<rt>がっこう</rt></ruby>へ<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I went to school without eating breakfast.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないでください</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないでください</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">please don&#x27;t ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-form + でください</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A polite negative request.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここで<ruby>写真<rt>しゃしん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>撮<rt>と</rt></ruby>らないでください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please don&#x27;t take photos here.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>中<rt>なか</rt></ruby>をおいでにならないでください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Don&#x27;t bother coming in this rain.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>心配<rt>しんぱい</rt></ruby>しないでください。<ruby>調<rt>しら</rt></ruby>べてみましょう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Don&#x27;t worry. I&#x27;ll look it up for you.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>にあまり<ruby>多<rt>おお</rt></ruby>くの<ruby>期待<rt>きたい</rt></ruby>をしないでください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Don&#x27;t expect too much of me.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ながら</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ながら</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">while ~ing (simultaneous)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + ながら</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Both actions belong to one person; the main action is the second clause.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ながら is two actions at once; まま is a state left unchanged.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>音楽<rt>おんがく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>聞<rt>き</rt></ruby>きながら<ruby>勉強<rt>べんきょう</rt></ruby>します。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I study while listening to music.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>歩<rt>ある</rt></ruby>きながら<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>しましょう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Let&#x27;s talk while walking.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なくてもいいです</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なくてもいいです</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">don&#x27;t have to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-stem + なくてもいい(です)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Absence of obligation, as opposed to prohibition.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div to come tomorrow.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>売<rt>う</rt></ruby>り<ruby>上<rt>あ</rt></ruby>げを<ruby>伸<rt>の</rt></ruby>ばそうと<ruby>焦<rt>あせ</rt></ruby>らなくてもいい。</div><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きたくなければ、<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>かなくてもいいよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You needn&#x27;t go there if you don&#x27;t want to go.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そんなに<ruby>緊張<rt>きんちょう</rt></ruby>しなくてもいいですよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There&#x27;s no need to be that tense.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なければなりません</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なければなりません</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">must ~ / have to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-stem + なければなりません</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Obligation. Casual equivalents: 〜なきゃ, 〜ないと.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>薬<rt>くすり</rt></ruby>を<ruby>飲<rt>の</rt></ruby>まなければなりません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have to take my medicine.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたは<ruby>夕食<rt>ゆうしょく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>作<rt>つく</rt></ruby>らなければなりませんか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Do you have to make dinner?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>年寄<rt>としよ</rt></ruby>りには<ruby>親切<rt>しんせつ</rt></ruby>にしなければなりません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You must be kind to old people.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">このレッスンを<ruby>受<rt>う</rt></ruby>けなければなりませんか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Must I take this lesson?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なに・なん</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なに・なん</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">what</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">何 + particle</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Read なん before です, counters and た/だ/な sounds; otherwise なに.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">これは<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>ですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">What is this?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">「これらは<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>ですか」「あなたの<ruby>写真<rt>しゃしん</rt></ruby>です」</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">What are these? &quot;They are your pictures.&quot;</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>にやってほしいというのは<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>ですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">What is it that you want me to do?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>英語<rt>えいご</rt></ruby>を<ruby>勉強<rt>べんきょう</rt></ruby>する<ruby>主要<rt>しゅよう</rt></ruby>な<ruby>目的<rt>もくてき</rt></ruby>は<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>ですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">What is the main purpose of your studying English?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に (destination)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に (destination)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">to (a destination)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Place + に + motion verb</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks the goal of movement. へ is interchangeable here, but に also marks the place something exists or is placed.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>学校<rt>がっこう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I go to school.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたの<ruby>代<rt>か</rt></ruby>わりに<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>が<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>会<rt>あ</rt></ruby>いに<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I will go to see him instead of you.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>休日<rt>きゅうじつ</rt></ruby>になるとよく<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>い<ruby>物<rt>もの</rt></ruby>に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She will often go shopping on holidays.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>気分<rt>きぶん</rt></ruby>のよいときには、<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>散歩<rt>さんぽ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">When I feel fine, I go for a walk.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に (time)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に (time)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">at / on / in (a point in time)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Time expression + に</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Used with specific times: 三時に, 月曜日に. Omitted with relative times such as 今日, 明日, 毎日.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>七時<rt>しちじ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>起<rt>お</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I get up at seven.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜になる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜になる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">become ~ (noun / な-adjective)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / な-adjective + になる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The counterpart of くなる for nouns and な-adjectives.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>医者<rt>いしゃ</rt></ruby>になりました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He became a doctor.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に行く</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>く</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">go in order to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem / Noun + に + 行く・来る・帰る</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Expresses purpose of movement: 買い物に行く, 食べに行く.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>映画<rt>えいが</rt></ruby>を<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;m going to see a movie.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ね</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ね</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">~, isn&#x27;t it? (seeking agreement)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Sentence + ね</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Invites the listener to agree or share a feeling. Softens the statement.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">いい<ruby>天気<rt>てんき</rt></ruby>ですね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Nice weather, isn&#x27;t it?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜の (possessive)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜の (possessive)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">&#x27;s / of</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + の + Noun</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Links two nouns, most often showing possession or attribution: 私の本, 日本の車.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">これは<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>の<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This is my book.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ので</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ので</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">because ~ (softer)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + ので / な-adjective・Noun + なので</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Presents the reason as objective, so it sounds gentler than から — common in requests and excuses.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>熱<rt>ねつ</rt></ruby>があるので、<ruby>休<rt>やす</rt></ruby>みます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have a fever, so I&#x27;ll rest.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜は</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜は</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">topic marker: as for ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + は</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks what the sentence is about, not necessarily the subject. Pronounced わ. Contrast with が, which introduces new or emphasised information.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>日本人<rt>にっぽんじん</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I am Japanese.</div></div>	jlpt::N5

style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はどうですか</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はどうですか</div><div <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜へ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜へ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">toward (direction)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Place + へ + motion verb</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Pronounced え. <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ましょうか</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ましょうか</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">shall I ~? <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ましょうか (offer)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ましょうか (offer)</div><div
