<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ます</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ます</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">polite non-past verb ending</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + ます</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Expresses present habit or future action. Negative ません, past ました, past negative ませんでした.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>毎日<rt>まいにち</rt></ruby><ruby>日本語<rt>にほんご</rt></ruby>を<ruby>勉強<rt>べんきょう</rt></ruby>します。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I study Japanese every day.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ません</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ません</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">polite negative verb ending</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + ません</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The polite negative. Past negative is ませんでした.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>酒<rt>さけ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>飲<rt>の</rt></ruby>みません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I don&#x27;t drink alcohol.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたのご<ruby>親切<rt>しんせつ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>はけっして<ruby>忘<rt>わす</rt></ruby>れません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I shall never forget your kindness.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたは<ruby>体<rt>からだ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>大事<rt>だいじ</rt></ruby>にしなければいけません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You must take care of yourself.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたは<ruby>泳<rt>およ</rt></ruby>げる。しかし、<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>泳<rt>およ</rt></ruby>げません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You can swim, but I can&#x27;t swim.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ませんか</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ませんか</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">won&#x27;t you ~? (invitation)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + ませんか</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A polite invitation. More tentative, and therefore politer, than ましょう.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一緒<rt>いっしょ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>昼<rt>ひる</rt></ruby>ご<ruby>飯<rt>はん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べませんか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Won&#x27;t you have lunch with me?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">「いっしょにきませんか」「ええ、<ruby>喜<rt>よろこ</rt></ruby>んで」</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Won&#x27;t you come with us? &quot;I&#x27;d be glad to.&quot;</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたが<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>ったものを<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>せてくれませんか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Will you show me what you bought?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたはお<ruby>金<rt>かね</rt></ruby>をいくらか<ruby>持<rt>も</rt></ruby>っていませんか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Do you have any money?</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">still / not yet</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">まだ + affirmative (still) / negative (not yet)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">まだ〜ていません is the standard way to say something has not happened yet.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">まだ<ruby>宿題<rt>しゅくだい</rt></ruby>をしていません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I haven&#x27;t done my homework yet.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まだ〜ていません</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まだ〜ていません</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">have not ~ed yet</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">まだ + Verb て-form + いません</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The standard answer to a もう〜ましたか question when the action is incomplete.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">まだ<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べていません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I haven&#x27;t eaten yet.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜も</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜も</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">also / too</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + も</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Replaces は, が and を rather than stacking with them. With a negative it means &quot;not ~ either&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>も<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I will go too.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜も (quantity)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜も (quantity)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">as many as / as much as</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Number + counter + も</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks a quantity the speaker finds surprisingly large.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ビールを<ruby>五<rt>ご</rt></ruby><ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>も<ruby>飲<rt>の</rt></ruby>みました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I drank as many as five beers.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜もう</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜もう</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">already</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">もう + verb (usually past)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">With a negative it means &quot;no longer&quot;: もう食べません.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もう<ruby>昼<rt>ひる</rt></ruby>ご<ruby>飯<rt>はん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have already eaten lunch.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜や</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜や</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">and (partial list)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + や + Noun (+ など)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Lists representative examples, implying there are others. Often closed with など.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>机<rt>つくえ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>上<rt>うえ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>やノートがあります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There are books and notebooks (among other things) on the desk.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜よ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜よ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">~, you know (informing)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Sentence + よ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Presents information the speaker believes is new to the listener. Overuse sounds pushy.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>店<rt>みせ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>安<rt>やす</rt></ruby>いですよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This shop is cheap, you know.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜よく</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜よく</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">often / well</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">よく + verb</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Means &quot;often&quot; with habitual actions and &quot;well&quot; with ability.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">よく<ruby>映画<rt>えいが</rt></ruby>を<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>ます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I often watch movies.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜より〜のほうが</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜より〜のほうが</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">~ is more ~ than ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">A より B のほうが + adjective</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">B is the item with more of the quality. The より phrase may be dropped once context is clear.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>電車<rt>でんしゃ</rt></ruby>より<ruby>車<rt>くるま</rt></ruby>のほうが<ruby>速<rt>はや</rt></ruby>いです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Cars are faster than trains.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜を</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜を</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">direct object marker</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + を</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks what the verb acts on. Also marks the place moved through with motion verbs: 公園を歩く.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">パンを<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I eat bread.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜前に</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N5</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>前<rt>まえ</rt></ruby>に</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">before ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun の + 前に</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Always takes the dictionary form, never the past, regardless of the sentence&#x27;s tense.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>寝<rt>ね</rt></ruby>る<ruby>前<rt>まえ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>歯<rt>は</rt></ruby>を<ruby>磨<rt>みが</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I brush my teeth before going to bed.</div></div>	jlpt::N5
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜(よ)う (volitional)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜(よ)う (volitional)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">let&#x27;s ~ / I&#x27;ll ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Group 1 お-row + う / Group 2 + よう / する→しよう</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The plain counterpart of ましょう.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">〜(よ)うと思う softens it into a stated intention.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一緒<rt>いっしょ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>こう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Let&#x27;s go together.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そろそろ<ruby>帰<rt>かえ</rt></ruby>ろう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Let&#x27;s head home soon.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたの<ruby>仕事<rt>しごと</rt></ruby>が<ruby>終<rt>お</rt></ruby>わった<ruby>後<rt>のち</rt></ruby>で<ruby>会<rt>あ</rt></ruby>いましょう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We will meet after you have finished your work.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あの<ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>のために<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>か<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>ってあげましょうか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Shall I buy some for him?</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜(よ)うと思う</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜(よ)うと<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>う</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">I&#x27;m thinking of ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Volitional form + と思う</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A decision made fairly recently. 〜と思っている suggests a longer-held plan.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Softer and less fixed than つもりだ.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby>から<ruby>運動<rt>うんどう</rt></ruby>しようと<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;m thinking of exercising from tomorrow.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>来年<rt>らいねん</rt></ruby><ruby>留学<rt>りゅうがく</rt></ruby>しようと<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>っている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;m thinking of studying abroad next year.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたはすぐに<ruby>良<rt>よ</rt></ruby>くなるだろうと<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I hope you&#x27;ll soon get well.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>たちはもういちど<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>会<rt>あ</rt></ruby>おうと<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I reckon we&#x27;ll see him once more.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜うちに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜うちに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">while ~ (before the state changes)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary / ている / ない-form / adjective + うちに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Do it while conditions still allow. 〜ないうちに means &quot;before ~ happens&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">うちに warns the chance will pass; 間に is neutral timing.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>熱<rt>あつ</rt></ruby>いうちに<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べてください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please eat it while it&#x27;s hot.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>暗<rt>くら</rt></ruby>くならないうちに<ruby>帰<rt>かえ</rt></ruby>りましょう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Let&#x27;s go home before it gets dark.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜お〜する</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜お〜する</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">humble: I do ~ (for you)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">お + Verb ます-stem + する</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Lowers the speaker to show respect to the listener.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">お〜する is about your own actions only.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>荷物<rt>にもつ</rt></ruby>をお<ruby>持<rt>も</rt></ruby>ちします。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ll carry your luggage.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>後<rt>あと</rt></ruby>でお<ruby>電話<rt>でんわ</rt></ruby>します。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ll call you later.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">タオルをもう１<ruby>枚<rt>まい</rt></ruby>お<ruby>持<rt>も</rt></ruby>ちします。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ll bring one more towel.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>紅茶<rt>こうちゃ</rt></ruby>はすぐにお<ruby>持<rt>も</rt></ruby>ちしますか、それとも<ruby>後<rt>ご</rt></ruby>になさいますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Would you like your tea now or later?</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜お〜になる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜お〜になる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">honorific: (someone respected) does ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">お + Verb ます-stem + になる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Raises the status of the person acting. Never use about yourself.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">お〜になる elevates others; お〜する humbles yourself.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>はもうお<ruby>帰<rt>かえ</rt></ruby>りになりました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The teacher has already gone home.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>社長<rt>しゃちょう</rt></ruby>がお<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>しになります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The president will speak.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">どのくらい<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>をお<ruby>待<rt>ま</rt></ruby>ちになっていましたか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How long have you been waiting for me?</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かどうか</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かどうか</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">whether or not ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + かどうか</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Embeds a yes/no question. With a question word, use か alone: 誰が来るか.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">かどうか for yes/no; 〜か for open questions.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>来<rt>く</rt></ruby>るかどうか<ruby>分<rt>わ</rt></ruby>かりません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I don&#x27;t know whether he will come.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>正<rt>ただ</rt></ruby>しいかどうか<ruby>確認<rt>かくにん</rt></ruby>してください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please check whether it is correct.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたがこられるかどうか<ruby>連絡<rt>れんらく</rt></ruby>して<ruby>下<rt>くだ</rt></ruby>さい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Let us know whether you can come.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>機械<rt>きかい</rt></ruby>がうまく<ruby>動<rt>うご</rt></ruby>くかどうかは<ruby>疑<rt>うたが</rt></ruby>わしい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is doubtful whether this machine works well or not.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かもしれない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かもしれない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">might / may ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + かもしれない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A low-confidence guess. Polite: かもしれません.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">かもしれない is weaker than でしょう, which is weaker than はずだ.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby>は<ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>かもしれません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It might rain tomorrow.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はもう<ruby>帰<rt>かえ</rt></ruby>ったかもしれない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He might have already gone home.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今日<rt>きょう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>寒<rt>さむ</rt></ruby>くなり、<ruby>雪<rt>ゆき</rt></ruby>が<ruby>降<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>るかもしれません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s supposed to get colder and snow later today.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今日<rt>きょう</rt></ruby><ruby>出発<rt>しゅっぱつ</rt></ruby>したほうがいいかもしれませんね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I might as well leave today.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がする</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がする</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">feel / smell / hear ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun (音・におい・味・感じ) + がする</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">For sensations perceived involuntarily.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">がする is passive perception; を感じる is active.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">いいにおいがします。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It smells good.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>隣<rt>となり</rt></ruby>から<ruby>変<rt>へん</rt></ruby>な<ruby>音<rt>おと</rt></ruby>がした。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">A strange sound came from next door.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>本当<rt>ほんとう</rt></ruby>によくなっている<ruby>感<rt>かん</rt></ruby>じがしますもの。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I do feel on the mend.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>花<rt>はな</rt></ruby>は<ruby>美<rt>うつく</rt></ruby>しいだけでなく、よい<ruby>香<rt>かお</rt></ruby>りがします。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">These flowers are not only beautiful but also fragrant.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">show signs of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Adjective stem + がる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Describes outward signs of a third party&#x27;s feelings.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">がる observes others; そのまま adjectives describe oneself.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>弟<rt>おとうと</rt></ruby>は<ruby>犬<rt>いぬ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>怖<rt>こわ</rt></ruby>がっています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">My little brother is afraid of dogs.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">みんな<ruby>新<rt>あたら</rt></ruby>しい<ruby>店<rt>みせ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>珍<rt>めずら</rt></ruby>しがっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Everyone finds the new shop novel.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜こと (nominaliser)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜こと (nominaliser)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">the act of ~ / that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + こと</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Turns a verb into a noun for abstract statements.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">こと is abstract; の is concrete and perceptible.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>の<ruby>趣味<rt>しゅみ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>音楽<rt>おんがく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>聞<rt>き</rt></ruby>くことです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">My hobby is listening to music.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>約束<rt>やくそく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>守<rt>まも</rt></ruby>ることが<ruby>大切<rt>たいせつ</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Keeping promises is important.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>互<rt>たが</rt></ruby>いに<ruby>理解<rt>りかい</rt></ruby>しあうことは<ruby>大切<rt>たいせつ</rt></ruby>なことです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">To understand each other is important.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>種<rt>たね</rt></ruby>の<ruby>経験<rt>けいけん</rt></ruby>は<ruby>誰<rt>だれ</rt></ruby>にでもよくあることです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This kind of experience is familiar to everybody.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
