<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことにする</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことにする</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">decide to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / ない-form + ことにする</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A decision the speaker makes. ことにしている marks an established rule of one&#x27;s own.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ことにする is one&#x27;s own decision; ことになる is a decision made elsewhere.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>来月<rt>らいげつ</rt></ruby><ruby>引<rt>ひ</rt></ruby>っ<ruby>越<rt>こ</rt></ruby>すことにしました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ve decided to move next month.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>酒<rt>さけ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>飲<rt>の</rt></ruby>まないことにした。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I decided not to drink alcohol.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>法律<rt>ほうりつ</rt></ruby>をやめて<ruby>美術<rt>びじゅつ</rt></ruby>をやることにしました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He decided to abandon law for art.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>毎日<rt>まいにち</rt></ruby><ruby>日記<rt>にっき</rt></ruby>を<ruby>付<rt>つ</rt></ruby>けているのに<ruby>刺激<rt>しげき</rt></ruby>されて、<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>も<ruby>英語<rt>えいご</rt></ruby>で<ruby>日記<rt>にっき</rt></ruby>を<ruby>付<rt>つ</rt></ruby>けることにしました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He writes a daily journal, and that inspired me to try doing the same thing, but in English.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことになる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことになる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">it has been decided that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / ない-form + ことになる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An outcome arranged by circumstances or by others.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ことになる is passive arrangement; ことにする is personal choice.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>来週<rt>らいしゅう</rt></ruby><ruby>出張<rt>しゅっちょう</rt></ruby>することになりました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It has been decided that I&#x27;ll go on a business trip next week.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>試験<rt>しけん</rt></ruby>は<ruby>中止<rt>ちゅうし</rt></ruby>になることになった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It was decided that the exam would be cancelled.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>友人<rt>ゆうじん</rt></ruby>の<ruby>結婚式<rt>けっこんしき</rt></ruby>にアカペラで<ruby>歌<rt>うた</rt></ruby>うことになりました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;m going to be singing a cappella at a friend&#x27;s wedding ceremony.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>の<ruby>日本<rt>にっぽん</rt></ruby>の<ruby>級友<rt>きゅうゆう</rt></ruby><ruby>達<rt>たち</rt></ruby>は、いつも<ruby>一緒<rt>いっしょ</rt></ruby>にいるので、お<ruby>互<rt>たが</rt></ruby>いに<ruby>大変<rt>たいへん</rt></ruby>よく<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>り<ruby>合<rt>あ</rt></ruby>うことになりました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Being together all the time, my Japanese classmates got to know each other very well.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ございます</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ございます</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">there is (very polite)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + がございます</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The polite equivalent of あります, standard in shops and formal service.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ございます is politer than あります, which is politer than ある.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>手洗<rt>てあら</rt></ruby>いは<ruby>二<rt>に</rt></ruby><ruby>階<rt>かい</rt></ruby>にございます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The restroom is on the second floor.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">こちらに<ruby>商品<rt>しょうひん</rt></ruby>がございます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The goods are over here.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">いろいろとご<ruby>親切<rt>しんせつ</rt></ruby>にありがとうございます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Thank you very much for all your kindness.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ご<ruby>親切<rt>しんせつ</rt></ruby>のほど<ruby>本当<rt>ほんとう</rt></ruby>にありがとうございます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I am so much obliged to you for your kindness.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜さ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜さ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">-ness (adjective to noun)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Adjective stem + さ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Makes a measurable noun: 高さ, 長さ, 重さ.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">さ gives an objective measure; み gives a subjective quality (暖かみ).</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>山<rt>やま</rt></ruby>の<ruby>高<rt>たか</rt></ruby>さは<ruby>三<rt>さん</rt></ruby><ruby>千<rt>せん</rt></ruby>メートルです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This mountain&#x27;s height is three thousand metres.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>部屋<rt>へや</rt></ruby>の<ruby>広<rt>ひろ</rt></ruby>さを<ruby>測<rt>はか</rt></ruby>ります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ll measure the size of the room.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜させられる (causative-passive)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜させられる (causative-passive)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">be made to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Causative stem + られる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The speaker was forced into an action, usually unwillingly.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">させる makes someone act; させられる is being made to act.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>に<ruby>漢字<rt>かんじ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>書<rt>か</rt></ruby>かせられました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I was made to write kanji by the teacher.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一時<rt>いちじ</rt></ruby><ruby>間<rt>かん</rt></ruby><ruby>待<rt>ま</rt></ruby>たされた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I was made to wait an hour.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>間違<rt>まちが</rt></ruby>いを<ruby>納得<rt>なっとく</rt></ruby>させられなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He couldn&#x27;t convince her of her mistake.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>機知<rt>きち</rt></ruby>にはみんなビックリさせられる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She dazzles everybody with her wit.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜させる (causative)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜させる (causative)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">make / let someone ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Group 1 あ-row + せる / Group 2 + させる / する→させる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">を marks the person made to act with intransitive verbs; に with transitive ones.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">With 〜てあげる it reads as &quot;let&quot;; alone it often reads as &quot;make&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>母<rt>はは</rt></ruby>は<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>に<ruby>野菜<rt>やさい</rt></ruby>を<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べさせました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">My mother made me eat vegetables.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>どもを<ruby>公園<rt>こうえん</rt></ruby>で<ruby>遊<rt>あそ</rt></ruby>ばせた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I let the children play in the park.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>代表<rt>だいひょう</rt></ruby><ruby>団<rt>だん</rt></ruby>は<ruby>貿易<rt>ぼうえき</rt></ruby><ruby>交渉<rt>こうしょう</rt></ruby>を<ruby>大<rt>おお</rt></ruby>きく<ruby>前進<rt>ぜんしん</rt></ruby>させました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Representatives made a major breakthrough in the trade talks.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">おじさんは、<ruby>君<rt>くん</rt></ruby>に、おじさんの<ruby>車<rt>くるま</rt></ruby>を<ruby>運転<rt>うんてん</rt></ruby>させましたか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Did your uncle let you drive his car?</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜し</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜し</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">and moreover / for one thing ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + し</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Lists reasons that build up to a conclusion, implying there are more.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">し implies further unstated reasons; から gives one reason plainly.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>店<rt>みせ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>安<rt>やす</rt></ruby>いし、おいしいです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This restaurant is cheap, and the food is good too.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>だし、<ruby>寒<rt>さむ</rt></ruby>いし、<ruby>家<rt>いえ</rt></ruby>にいます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s raining, it&#x27;s cold, so I&#x27;ll stay home.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜すぎる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜すぎる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">too ~ / excessively</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem / adjective stem + すぎる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Conjugates as a る-verb. いい becomes よすぎる, ない becomes なさすぎる.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">すぎる always carries a negative judgement; とても is neutral.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べすぎました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I ate too much.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>問題<rt>もんだい</rt></ruby>は<ruby>難<rt>むずか</rt></ruby>しすぎる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This problem is too difficult.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>問題<rt>もんだい</rt></ruby>は<ruby>解<rt>と</rt></ruby>けません。<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>には<ruby>難<rt>むずか</rt></ruby>しすぎます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I can&#x27;t solve this problem. It&#x27;s too difficult for me.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたの<ruby>答案<rt>とうあん</rt></ruby>には<ruby>間違<rt>まちが</rt></ruby>いが<ruby>多<rt>おお</rt></ruby>すぎます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Your paper contains too many mistakes.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ずに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ずに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">without ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-stem + ずに (する→せずに)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A written, formal equivalent of 〜ないで.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ずに is formal; ないで is everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>朝<rt>あさ</rt></ruby>ご<ruby>飯<rt>はん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べずに<ruby>出<rt>で</rt></ruby>かけました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I went out without eating breakfast.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>も<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>わずに<ruby>帰<rt>かえ</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He went home without saying anything.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜そうだ (appearance)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜そうだ (appearance)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">looks like / seems</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem / adjective stem + そうだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An impression from what one can see. いい becomes よさそう, ない becomes なさそう.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Attached to a stem it means &quot;looks&quot;; attached to a plain form it means &quot;I hear&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">このケーキはおいしそうです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This cake looks delicious.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>降<rt>お</rt></ruby>りそうだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It looks like it&#x27;s going to rain.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">おいしそうですね。<ruby>僕<rt>ぼく</rt></ruby>も<ruby>試<rt>ため</rt></ruby>してみようかな。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Looks delicious. Think I&#x27;ll try some.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">はい、そうです。あなたは<ruby>中野<rt>なかの</rt></ruby>さんですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Yes, I am. Are you Mr Nakano?</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜そうだ (hearsay)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜そうだ (hearsay)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">I hear that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + そうだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Reports information received. Has no negative or past form of its own.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Plain form + そうだ is hearsay; stem + そうだ is appearance.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>天気<rt>てんき</rt></ruby><ruby>予報<rt>よほう</rt></ruby>によると、<ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby>は<ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>だそうです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">According to the forecast, it will rain tomorrow.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>来<rt>こ</rt></ruby>ないそうだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I hear he isn&#x27;t coming.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">おいしそうですね。<ruby>僕<rt>ぼく</rt></ruby>も<ruby>試<rt>ため</rt></ruby>してみようかな。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Looks delicious. Think I&#x27;ll try some.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">はい、そうです。あなたは<ruby>中野<rt>なかの</rt></ruby>さんですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Yes, I am. Are you Mr Nakano?</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜そうにない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜そうにない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">unlikely to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + そうにない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The negative of appearance-そう: there is no sign of it happening.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">そうにない negates the appearance; なさそう negates the adjective.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今日<rt>きょう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>降<rt>お</rt></ruby>りそうにない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It doesn&#x27;t look like it will rain today.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>来<rt>き</rt></ruby>そうにないです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He doesn&#x27;t seem likely to come.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">すぐには<ruby>別<rt>べつ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>提案<rt>ていあん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>受<rt>う</rt></ruby>けられそうにない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We are not likely to accept another offer any time soon.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">とても<ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby>までにこの<ruby>仕事<rt>しごと</rt></ruby>はすみそうにない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We can never begin to finish this work by tomorrow.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たがる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たがる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">(someone else) wants to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + たがる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">For third parties&#x27; desires, since Japanese avoids asserting others&#x27; feelings directly.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">たい is for oneself; たがる is for others.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>どもが<ruby>外<rt>そと</rt></ruby>で<ruby>遊<rt>あそ</rt></ruby>びたがっています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The child wants to play outside.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>日本<rt>にっぽん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きたがっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He wants to go to Japan.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたが<ruby>話<rt>はなし</rt></ruby>をしたがっていたのは<ruby>誰<rt>だれ</rt></ruby>ですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Who is it that you want to talk to?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ほとんどの<ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>は<ruby>幸福<rt>こうふく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>味<rt>あじ</rt></ruby>わいたがっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Most people want to experience happiness.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たところ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たところ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">just finished ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form + ところ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks the exact moment an action ended. ているところ is mid-action, るところ is about to start.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">たところ is objectively just now; たばかり can be days ago.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今<rt>いま</rt></ruby>、<ruby>駅<rt>えき</rt></ruby>に<ruby>着<rt>つ</rt></ruby>いたところです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ve just arrived at the station.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>食事<rt>しょくじ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>終<rt>お</rt></ruby>わったところだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The meal has just finished.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ちょうどその<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>読<rt>よ</rt></ruby>み<ruby>終<rt>お</rt></ruby>わったところだよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have just finished reading the book.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>小包<rt>こづつみ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>出<rt>だ</rt></ruby>しに<ruby>郵便<rt>ゆうびん</rt></ruby><ruby>局<rt>きょく</rt></ruby>へ<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>って<ruby>来<rt>き</rt></ruby>たところだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have just been to the post office to send a parcel.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たばかり</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たばかり</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">just ~ed</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form + ばかり</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Subjective: the speaker feels it was recent, however long ago it was.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">〜たばかり is felt recency; 〜たところ is objective immediacy.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>日本<rt>にっぽん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>来<rt>き</rt></ruby>たばかりです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have just come to Japan.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ご<ruby>飯<rt>はん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べたばかりだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ve just eaten.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたばかりでなく<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>もまちがっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Not only you but also I am in the wrong.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そこに<ruby>羽<rt>はね</rt></ruby>の<ruby>生<rt>は</rt></ruby>えたばかりの<ruby>小鳥<rt>ことり</rt></ruby>がありました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There was a fledgling bird there.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ために (purpose)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ために (purpose)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">in order to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun の + ために</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Both clauses normally share a subject who acts deliberately.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ために needs a volitional verb; ように is used with potential and negative forms.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>日本語<rt>にほんご</rt></ruby>を<ruby>勉強<rt>べんきょう</rt></ruby>するために、<ruby>日本<rt>にっぽん</rt></ruby>へ<ruby>来<rt>き</rt></ruby>ました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I came to Japan in order to study Japanese.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>家族<rt>かぞく</rt></ruby>のために<ruby>働<rt>はたら</rt></ruby>いています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I work for my family&#x27;s sake.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たら</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たら</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">if / when ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb・adjective た-form + ら / Noun + だったら</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The most flexible conditional. Works for hypothetical, temporal and discovered-fact situations.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">たら allows a request or command in the second clause; と does not.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>降<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>ったら、<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If it rains, I won&#x27;t go.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>家<rt>いえ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>着<rt>つ</rt></ruby>いたら、<ruby>電話<rt>でんわ</rt></ruby>してください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">When you get home, please call me.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たらどうですか</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たらどうですか</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">why don&#x27;t you ~?</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form + らどうですか</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A suggestion. Can sound pushy if overused with superiors.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Softer than なさい, more direct than ほうがいい.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>少<rt>すこ</rt></ruby>し<ruby>休<rt>やす</rt></ruby>んだらどうですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Why don&#x27;t you rest a little?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>医者<rt>いしゃ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>相談<rt>そうだん</rt></ruby>したらどう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Why not consult a doctor?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もう<ruby>少<rt>すこ</rt></ruby>し<ruby>待<rt>ま</rt></ruby>ったらどう。すぐにはれるから。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You may as well wait for a while. It&#x27;ll clear up soon.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もし<ruby>万一<rt>まんいち</rt></ruby><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>失敗<rt>しっぱい</rt></ruby>したらどうなるだろうか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">What if he should fail?</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たり (single)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たり (single)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">do things like ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form + り + する</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">One listed action implies others of the same kind.</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The buses ran back and forth almost empty.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜つもりだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜つもりだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">intend to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / ない-form + つもりだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A settled personal intention.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">つもり is the speaker&#x27;s own plan; 予定 is a scheduled arrangement.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>来年<rt>らいねん</rt></ruby><ruby>日本<rt>にっぽん</rt></ruby>へ<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>くつもりです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I intend to go to Japan next year.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今日<rt>きょう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>もしないつもりだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I intend to do nothing today.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あさって<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>にここへ<ruby>来<rt>き</rt></ruby>てもらうつもりです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I will have him come here the day after tomorrow.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたはいつタバコを<ruby>止<rt>と</rt></ruby>めるつもりですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">When are you going to quit smoking?</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てあげる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てあげる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">do ~ for someone</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + あげる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The speaker does a favour for someone else. Saying it directly to a superior sounds condescending.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">あげる gives outward, くれる comes inward, もらう receives.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>友達<rt>ともだち</rt></ruby>に<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>貸<rt>か</rt></ruby>してあげました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I lent my friend a book.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>妹<rt>いもうと</rt></ruby>に<ruby>宿題<rt>しゅくだい</rt></ruby>を<ruby>教<rt>おし</rt></ruby>えてあげた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I helped my little sister with her homework.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">おいしい<ruby>温<rt>あたた</rt></ruby>かい<ruby>夕食<rt>ゆうしょく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>作<rt>つく</rt></ruby>ってあげましたよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ve cooked you a nice hot dinner.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ボーイフレンドに<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>を<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>ってあげましたか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">What did you buy for your boyfriend?</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てある</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てある</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">has been ~ed (state from an action)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Transitive verb て-form + ある</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Something was done on purpose and the result remains. The object usually takes が.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">てある implies a deliberate agent; ている with an intransitive verb just describes a state.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>壁<rt>かべ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>絵<rt>え</rt></ruby>が<ruby>掛<rt>か</rt></ruby>けてあります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">A picture has been hung on the wall.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>窓<rt>まど</rt></ruby>が<ruby>開<rt>あ</rt></ruby>けてある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The window has been left open.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>両親<rt>りょうしん</rt></ruby>にあなたの<ruby>計画<rt>けいかく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>らせてありますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Have you acquainted your parents with your plans?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>の<ruby>名前<rt>なまえ</rt></ruby>と<ruby>住所<rt>じゅうしょ</rt></ruby>はこれに<ruby>書<rt>か</rt></ruby>いてあります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">My name and address are on it.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ていく</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ていく</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">go on ~ing / ~ away from now</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + いく</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Change moving away from the present, or an action done while leaving.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ていく moves away from now; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てくる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てくる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">come to ~ / ~ up to now</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + くる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Change arriving at the present, or going and coming back.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">てくる approaches the present; ていく moves away.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>降<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>ってきました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It has started to rain.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">パンを<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>ってきます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ll go buy some bread (and come back).</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">さらに<ruby>悪<rt>わる</rt></ruby>いことには、<ruby>雪<rt>ゆき</rt></ruby>が<ruby>降<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>ってきました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">To make matters worse, it began snowing.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">アメリカの<ruby>生活<rt>せいかつ</rt></ruby>にだんだん<ruby>慣<rt>な</rt></ruby>れてきました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I was getting used to living in America.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てくれる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てくれる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">someone does ~ for me</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + くれる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The benefit flows toward the speaker or the speaker&#x27;s group.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">くれる: they act for me. もらう: I receive their action.</div><div <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ても</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ても</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even if ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + も / い-adjective くても / Noun でも</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Concedes a condition that does not change the outcome.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">〜ても concedes; 〜たら simply conditions.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>降<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>っても、<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even if it rains, I&#x27;ll go.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>高<rt>たか</rt></ruby>くても<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even if it&#x27;s expensive, I&#x27;ll buy it.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てもらう</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てもらう</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">have someone do ~ for me</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + もらう</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The doer is marked with に. Humble form: 〜ていただく.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">もらう takes the receiver as subject; くれる takes the giver.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>友達<rt>ともだち</rt></ruby>に<ruby>写真<rt>しゃしん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>撮<rt>と</rt></ruby>ってもらいました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I had my friend take a photo.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>に<ruby>手紙<rt>てがみ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>直<rt>なお</rt></ruby>してもらった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I had my teacher correct my letter.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>髪<rt>かみ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>毛<rt>け</rt></ruby>を<ruby>短<rt>みじか</rt></ruby>く<ruby>切<rt>き</rt></ruby>ってもらいました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She had her hair cut short.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>の<ruby>犬<rt>いぬ</rt></ruby>はルーシーに<ruby>世話<rt>せわ</rt></ruby>をしてもらいました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">My dog was taken care of by Lucy.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜でも (even)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜でも (even)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even ~ / ~ or something</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + でも</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Presents an extreme case, or offers a casual suggestion: お茶でも飲みませんか.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Sentence-initial でも means &quot;but&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>どもでも<ruby>分<rt>わ</rt></ruby>かります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even a child can understand it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>茶<rt>ちゃ</rt></ruby>でも<ruby>飲<rt>の</rt></ruby>みませんか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Would you like some tea or something?</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜と (conditional)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜と (conditional)</div><div 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / ない-form + と</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">For automatic, natural or repeated consequences.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">The clause after と cannot be a request, invitation or command.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div
