style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">このボタンを<ruby>押<rt>お</rt></ruby>すとドアが<ruby>開<rt>ひら</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If you push this button, the door will open.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ということ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ということ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">the fact that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + ということ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Packages a whole clause into a noun.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ということ nominalises a statement; こと nominalises an action.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>留学<rt>りゅうがく</rt></ruby>するということを<ruby>聞<rt>き</rt></ruby>きました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I heard that he is going to study abroad.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>約束<rt>やくそく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>守<rt>まも</rt></ruby>るということは<ruby>大切<rt>たいせつ</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Keeping promises is important.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたが<ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>だということを<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>っています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I know that you are a teacher.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>年<rt>とし</rt></ruby>を<ruby>取<rt>と</rt></ruby>り<ruby>過<rt>す</rt></ruby>ぎて<ruby>学<rt>まな</rt></ruby>べないということはない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">One is never too old to learn.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ところだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ところだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">about to / in the middle of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary / ている / た-form + ところだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Three stages: るところ about to, ているところ in progress, たところ just finished.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Contrast the three forms by the verb that precedes ところ.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今<rt>いま</rt></ruby>から<ruby>出<rt>で</rt></ruby>かけるところです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;m just about to go out.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今<rt>いま</rt></ruby>、ご<ruby>飯<rt>はん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>作<rt>つく</rt></ruby>っているところだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;m in the middle of cooking.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここから<ruby>歩<rt>ある</rt></ruby>いてせいぜい<ruby>十分<rt>じゅっぷん</rt></ruby>のところです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is not more than ten minutes&#x27; walk from here.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>今<rt>こん</rt></ruby><ruby>歯<rt>は</rt></ruby>が<ruby>生<rt>は</rt></ruby>えかけているところです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This child is teething now.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜と思う</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜と<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>う</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">I think that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + と思う</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Always takes the plain form before と, never です・ます.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">〜と言う reports someone&#x27;s words; 〜と思う reports an opinion.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>来<rt>こ</rt></ruby>ないと<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I think he won&#x27;t come.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>映画<rt>えいが</rt></ruby>は<ruby>面白<rt>おもしろ</rt></ruby>いと<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>う。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I think this movie is interesting.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたが<ruby>説明<rt>せつめい</rt></ruby>する<ruby>必要<rt>ひつよう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>無<rt>な</rt></ruby>いと<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>いますよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I don&#x27;t think there is any need for you to explain.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたはすぐに<ruby>良<rt>よ</rt></ruby>くなるだろうと<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I hope you&#x27;ll soon get well.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜と言う</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜と<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>う</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">say that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / quoted speech + と言う</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Direct quotes take 「」; indirect quotes take the plain form.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">〜そうだ (hearsay) reports without naming a source; 〜と言っていた names one.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>くと<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>いました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He said he would go.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>母<rt>はは</rt></ruby>は「<ruby>早<rt>はや</rt></ruby>く<ruby>帰<rt>かえ</rt></ruby>りなさい」と<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">My mother said, &quot;Come home early.&quot;</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>に<ruby>読書<rt>どくしょ</rt></ruby>する<ruby>時間<rt>じかん</rt></ruby>がないと<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>いました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He told me that he had no time to read books.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>母<rt>かあ</rt></ruby>さんは<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>に<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>とつき<ruby>合<rt>あ</rt></ruby>うなと<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>いました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Mother told me not to keep company with him.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないと</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないと</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">have to ~ (casual)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-form + と</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A shortened, conversational obligation; the consequence is left unsaid.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Casual equivalent of なければならない.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もう<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>かないと。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have to go now.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>早<rt>はや</rt></ruby>く<ruby>寝<rt>ね</rt></ruby>ないと、<ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby><ruby>起<rt>お</rt></ruby>きられないよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If you don&#x27;t sleep soon, you won&#x27;t be able to get up tomorrow.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ながらも</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ながらも</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even though / while still</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem / adjective / Noun + ながらも</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A concessive use of ながら, more written than spoken.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Plain ながら is simultaneous action; ながらも is concession.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>っていながらも、<ruby>教<rt>おし</rt></ruby>えてくれなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even though he knew, he didn&#x27;t tell me.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>狭<rt>せま</rt></ruby>いながらも、いい<ruby>部屋<rt>へや</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Though small, it&#x27;s a nice room.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>幼<rt>おさな</rt></ruby>いながらも、その<ruby>子供<rt>こども</rt></ruby>は<ruby>勇敢<rt>ゆうかん</rt></ruby>だった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Child as he was, he was brave.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>女<rt>おんな</rt></ruby>ながらも<ruby>勇敢<rt>ゆうかん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>戦<rt>たたか</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Woman as she was, she fought bravely.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なくちゃ・〜なきゃ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なくちゃ・〜なきゃ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">have to ~ (very casual)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-stem + なくちゃ / なきゃ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Spoken contractions of なくては and なければ.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Same meaning as なければならない, far more casual.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>宿題<rt>しゅくだい</rt></ruby>をしなくちゃ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have to do my homework.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もう<ruby>帰<rt>かえ</rt></ruby>らなきゃ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have to go home now.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なくなる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なくなる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">come not to ~ / stop ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-stem + なくなる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A change into a negative state.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ないようになる stresses deliberate change; なくなる is neutral.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>最近<rt>さいきん</rt></ruby>テレビを<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>なくなりました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ve stopped watching TV recently.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>痛<rt>いた</rt></ruby>みがなくなった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The pain has gone.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>作家<rt>さっか</rt></ruby>はどの<ruby>位<rt>くらい</rt></ruby><ruby>前<rt>まえ</rt></ruby>になくなりましたか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How long ago did the author die?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もう<ruby>二度<rt>にど</rt></ruby>と<ruby>町<rt>まち</rt></ruby>のことは<ruby>気<rt>き</rt></ruby>にしなくなりました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Never again would she be curious about the city.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なさい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なさい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">do ~ (gentle command)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + なさい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Used by parents and teachers to those below them. Never to a superior.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">なさい is softer than the plain imperative 〜ろ but still a command.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>早<rt>はや</rt></ruby>く<ruby>寝<rt>ね</rt></ruby>なさい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Go to bed early.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここに<ruby>名前<rt>なまえ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>書<rt>か</rt></ruby>きなさい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Write your name here.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なら</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なら</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">if it&#x27;s the case that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + なら</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Picks up something the speaker has just heard and responds to it.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">なら reacts to given information; たら simply sets a condition.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>日本<rt>にっぽん</rt></ruby>へ<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>くなら、<ruby>京都<rt>きょうと</rt></ruby>がいいですよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If you&#x27;re going to Japan, Kyoto is good.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>安<rt>やす</rt></ruby>いなら<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If it&#x27;s cheap (as you say), I&#x27;ll buy it.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にくい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にくい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">hard to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + にくい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Physical or practical difficulty. づらい stresses discomfort instead.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Opposite of 〜やすい.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>字<rt>じ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>読<rt>よ</rt></ruby>みにくいです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This handwriting is hard to read.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>道<rt>みち</rt></ruby>は<ruby>歩<rt>ある</rt></ruby>きにくい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This road is hard to walk on.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のが好きだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のが<ruby>好<rt>す</rt></ruby>きだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">like doing ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + のが + 好きだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">の turns the verb phrase into a noun so it can take が.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">の is preferred for concrete, perceptible actions; こと for abstractions.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>読<rt>よ</rt></ruby>むのが<ruby>好<rt>す</rt></ruby>きです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I like reading books.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>歌<rt>うた</rt></ruby>を<ruby>歌<rt>うた</rt></ruby>うのが<ruby>好<rt>す</rt></ruby>きだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I like singing songs.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">たいていの<ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>はテレビを<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>るのが<ruby>好<rt>す</rt></ruby>きです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Most people like watching TV.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ジョンは<ruby>友達<rt>ともだち</rt></ruby>と<ruby>切手<rt>きって</rt></ruby>を<ruby>交換<rt>こうかん</rt></ruby>するのが<ruby>好<rt>す</rt></ruby>きだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">John likes to trade stamps with his friends.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のに (despite)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のに (despite)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even though / although</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / な-adjective な / Noun な + のに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Carries surprise, dissatisfaction or regret.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">のに adds emotion; が and けど are neutral contrasts.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>薬<rt>くすり</rt></ruby>を<ruby>飲<rt>の</rt></ruby>んだのに、まだ<ruby>熱<rt>ねつ</rt></ruby>があります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even though I took medicine, I still have a fever.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>約束<rt>やくそく</rt></ruby>したのに、<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>来<rt>こ</rt></ruby>なかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even though he promised, he didn&#x27;t come.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のに (in order to)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のに (in order to)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">for the purpose of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + のに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Used with 使う, 必要だ, 便利だ and similar.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Distinguish from concessive のに by the main clause: here it evaluates usefulness.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>道具<rt>どうぐ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>魚<rt>さかな</rt></ruby>を<ruby>切<rt>き</rt></ruby>るのに<ruby>使<rt>つか</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This tool is used for cutting fish.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>駅<rt>えき</rt></ruby>に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>くのに<ruby>三<rt>さん</rt></ruby><ruby>十分<rt>じゅっぷん</rt></ruby>かかります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It takes thirty minutes to get to the station.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のは〜だ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のは〜だ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">the one who / thing that ~ is ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Clause + のは + Noun + だ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Focuses attention on the element after のは.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">のは focuses; ことは topicalises an abstract idea.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>が<ruby>生<rt>う</rt></ruby>まれたのは<ruby>北海道<rt>ほっかいどう</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The place I was born is Hokkaido.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一番<rt>いちばん</rt></ruby><ruby>難<rt>むずか</rt></ruby>しいのは<ruby>発音<rt>はつおん</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The hardest thing is pronunciation.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のように・〜のような</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のように・〜のような</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">like ~ / such as ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + のように (adverb) / のような (adjective)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">ように modifies verbs, ような modifies nouns.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">みたい is the casual spoken equivalent.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>鳥<rt>とり</rt></ruby>のように<ruby>歌<rt>うた</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He sings like a bird.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>氷<rt>こおり</rt></ruby>のような<ruby>手<rt>て</rt></ruby>ですね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Your hands are like ice.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">このようにして、<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はその<ruby>大学<rt>だいがく</rt></ruby>に<ruby>入学<rt>にゅうがく</rt></ruby>した。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This was how he entered the university.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">このようにしてその<ruby>事件<rt>じけん</rt></ruby>は<ruby>起<rt>お</rt></ruby>こったのです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This is how the accident happened.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はずだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はずだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">ought to / is supposed to be</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / な-adjective な / Noun の + はずだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Expectation grounded in evidence or reasoning.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">はず is a logical expectation; べき is a moral obligation.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>今日<rt>きょう</rt></ruby><ruby>来<rt>く</rt></ruby>るはずです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He is supposed to come today.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>鍵<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>はここにあるはずだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The key ought to be here.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は１<ruby>時間<rt>じかん</rt></ruby>のうちに<ruby>事務所<rt>じむしょ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>着<rt>つ</rt></ruby>くはずです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He should get to the office in an hour.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>列車<rt>れっしゃ</rt></ruby>は１０<ruby>時<rt>じ</rt></ruby>までに<ruby>大阪<rt>おおさか</rt></ruby>につくはずです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The train should arrive at Osaka by ten.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ば</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ば</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">if ~ (conditional)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb え-row + ば / い-adjective (drop い) + ければ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Emphasises the condition itself. Common in proverbs and general truths.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ば resists commands in the main clause when the subject is the same; たら is safer there.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>安<rt>やす</rt></ruby>ければ<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If it&#x27;s cheap, I&#x27;ll buy it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>時間<rt>じかん</rt></ruby>があれば、<ruby>手伝<rt>てつだ</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If I have time, I&#x27;ll help.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ば〜ほど</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ば〜ほど</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">the more ~, the more ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ば-form + Verb dictionary form + ほど</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Both slots take the same word.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Fixed correlative pattern; ほど alone means &quot;to the extent of&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>練習<rt>れんしゅう</rt></ruby>すればするほど<ruby>上手<rt>じょうず</rt></ruby>になります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The more you practise, the better you get.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>安<rt>やす</rt></ruby>ければ<ruby>安<rt>やす</rt></ruby>いほどいい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The cheaper, the better.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ばかり</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ばかり</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">nothing but / only</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + ばかり / Verb て-form + ばかりいる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Suggests the speaker finds the repetition excessive.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">だけ is a neutral limit; ばかり implies too much of it.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はゲームばかりしています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He does nothing but play games.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>甘<rt>あま</rt></ruby>い<ruby>物<rt>もの</rt></ruby>ばかり<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べてはいけません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You shouldn&#x27;t eat only sweets.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ほうがいい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ほうがいい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">had better ~ / it&#x27;s better to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form / ない-form + ほうがいい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Advice. The past form is standard for the affirmative.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Advice, unlike なければならない, which is obligation.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>早<rt>はや</rt></ruby>く<ruby>寝<rt>ね</rt></ruby>たほうがいいですよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You had better go to bed early.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>無理<rt>むり</rt></ruby>をしないほうがいい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You had better not overdo it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたには<ruby>真実<rt>しんじつ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>したほうがいいですね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;d better tell you the truth.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そういう<ruby>四<rt>よん</rt></ruby><ruby>文字<rt>もじ</rt></ruby><ruby>言葉<rt>ことば</rt></ruby>は<ruby>使<rt>つか</rt></ruby>わないほうがいい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You had better not use those four-letter words.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まだ〜ていない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まだ〜ていない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">have not yet ~ed</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">まだ + Verb て-form + いない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The action is still expected to happen.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Never answer もう〜ましたか with まだ〜ませんでした.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">まだ<ruby>昼<rt>ひる</rt></ruby>ご<ruby>飯<rt>はん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べていません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I haven&#x27;t had lunch yet.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>宿題<rt>しゅくだい</rt></ruby>はまだ<ruby>終<rt>お</rt></ruby>わっていない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The homework isn&#x27;t finished yet.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まま</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まま</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">as it is / leaving it unchanged</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form / Noun の / adjective + まま</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A state left untouched, often when it should have been changed.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">まま is a state left as-is; ながら is two simultaneous actions.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>窓<rt>まど</rt></ruby>を<ruby>開<rt>あ</rt></ruby>けたまま<ruby>寝<rt>ね</rt></ruby>てしまいました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I fell asleep with the window left open.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>靴<rt>くつ</rt></ruby>のまま<ruby>入<rt>はい</rt></ruby>らないでください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please don&#x27;t come in with your shoes on.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜みたい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜みたい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">like / seems (casual)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / plain form + みたい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The conversational counterpart of ようだ. Behaves as a な-adjective.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">みたい is casual; ようだ is neutral or formal.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>忙<rt>いそが</rt></ruby>しいみたいです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He seems busy.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>どもみたいに<ruby>笑<rt>わら</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He laughed like a child.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">メアリーは<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>が<ruby>好<rt>す</rt></ruby>きではないみたいですね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Mary scarcely seems to care for me, does she?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今日<rt>きょう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>病気<rt>びょうき</rt></ruby>なので、<ruby>会社<rt>かいしゃ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>休<rt>やす</rt></ruby>みたいです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Because I am sick today, I want to absent myself from the company.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜やすい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜やすい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">easy to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + やすい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Conjugates as an い-adjective.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Opposite of 〜にくい.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">このペンは<ruby>書<rt>か</rt></ruby>きやすいです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This pen is easy to write with.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>の<ruby>説明<rt>せつめい</rt></ruby>は<ruby>分<rt>わ</rt></ruby>かりやすい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The teacher&#x27;s explanation is easy to understand.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">it seems / it&#x27;s like</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / な-adjective な / Noun の + ようだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A judgement from the speaker&#x27;s own observation. Also forms similes.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ようだ rests on personal observation; らしい on outside information.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>忙<rt>いそが</rt></ruby>しいようです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He seems to be busy.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あの<ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>は<ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>のようだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">That person seems to be a teacher.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あまり<ruby>楽<rt>たの</rt></ruby>しくされておられないようですね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It seems that you are not having a good time here.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>昨日<rt>きのう</rt></ruby><ruby>東京<rt>とうきょう</rt></ruby>へ<ruby>向<rt>む</rt></ruby>けて<ruby>出発<rt>しゅっぱつ</rt></ruby>したようです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She seems to have left for Tokyo yesterday.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ように (purpose)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ように (purpose)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">so that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary / ない / potential form + ように</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The clause before ように is a state to be reached, usually with a non-volitional verb.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ように takes non-volitional verbs; ために takes volitional ones.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">よく<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>えるように、<ruby>前<rt>まえ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>座<rt>すわ</rt></ruby>りました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I sat at the front so that I could see well.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>忘<rt>わす</rt></ruby>れないようにメモします。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ll take a note so I don&#x27;t forget.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">やってもらいたいように、<ruby>他人<rt>たにん</rt></ruby>にしなさい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Do to others as you would be done by.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>講義<rt>こうぎ</rt></ruby>はいつものように、ためになった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">His lecture was very instructive as usual.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようにする</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようにする</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">try to / make an effort to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / ない-form + ようにする</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Deliberate, ongoing effort. ようにしている marks it as a habit.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ようにする is effort; ことにする is a one-off decision.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>毎日<rt>まいにち</rt></ruby><ruby>運動<rt>うんどう</rt></ruby>するようにしています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I make an effort to exercise every day.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>甘<rt>あま</rt></ruby>い<ruby>物<rt>もの</rt></ruby>を<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べないようにしている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;m trying not to eat sweets.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>宅<rt>たく</rt></ruby>の<ruby>暖房<rt>だんぼう</rt></ruby>はどのようにしていますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How do you heat the house?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>甘<rt>あま</rt></ruby>い<ruby>物<rt>もの</rt></ruby>を<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べないようにしています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I allow myself no sweets.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようになる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようになる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">come to ~ / reach the point of ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / potential form + ようになる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A gradual change, especially of ability or habit.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ようになる is change that happens; ようにする is effort one makes.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>日本語<rt>にほんご</rt></ruby>が<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>せるようになりました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have become able to speak Japanese.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>毎朝<rt>まいあさ</rt></ruby><ruby>走<rt>はし</rt></ruby>るようになった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ve started running every morning.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">どうしてそれを<ruby>信<rt>しん</rt></ruby>じるようになりましたか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How did you come to believe that?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">いつその<ruby>事実<rt>じじつ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>るようになりましたか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">When did you get to know the fact?</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜らしい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜らしい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">apparently / seems (from what I heard)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + らしい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Based on information from outside the speaker. Also means &quot;typical of&quot;: 男らしい.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">らしい is second-hand; ようだ is first-hand observation.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>病気<rt>びょうき</rt></ruby>らしいです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Apparently he is ill.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あの<ruby>店<rt>みせ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>安<rt>やす</rt></ruby>いらしい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">That shop is apparently cheap.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここにいると<ruby>素晴<rt>すば</rt></ruby>らしいですよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s wonderful to be here.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>庭園<rt>ていえん</rt></ruby>の<ruby>芸術<rt>げいじゅつ</rt></ruby><ruby>的<rt>てき</rt></ruby>な<ruby>美<rt>うつく</rt></ruby>しさが<ruby>素晴<rt>すば</rt></ruby>らしいです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The artistic beauty of the garden is truly amazing.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜られる (passive)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜られる (passive)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">be ~ed (passive)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Group 1 あ-row + れる / Group 2 + られる / する→される</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The agent is marked by に. The &quot;suffering passive&quot; shows the subject was adversely affected.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Identical in form to the potential for Group 2 verbs; context decides.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>に<ruby>褒<rt>ほ</rt></ruby>められました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I was praised by the teacher.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>降<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>られて、<ruby>困<rt>こま</rt></ruby>りました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I was caught in the rain and troubled by it.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜られる (potential)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜られる (potential)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">can ~ / be able to</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Group 1 え-row + る / Group 2 + られる / する→できる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The object often shifts from を to が.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Identical in form to the passive for Group 2 verbs.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>刺身<rt>さしみ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べられます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I can eat sashimi.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby>は<ruby>早<rt>はや</rt></ruby>く<ruby>来<rt>こ</rt></ruby>られますか。</div><div thing.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そんなふるまいをすると<ruby>安<rt>やす</rt></ruby>く<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>られますよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You will debase yourself with such behavior.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜予定だ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>予定<rt>よてい</rt></ruby>だ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">be scheduled to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun の + 予定だ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An arrangement fixed externally, often by a timetable.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">予定 is objective scheduling; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜出す</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>出<rt>だ</rt></ruby>す</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">suddenly start ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + 出す</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An abrupt, often unexpected start.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">出す is sudden; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜命令形</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>命令形<rt>めいれいけい</rt></ruby></div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">plain imperative: do ~!</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Group 1 え-row / Group 2 + ろ / する→しろ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Blunt and forceful. Common on signs, in sport and among close male friends.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">The negative imperative is Verb dictionary form + な: 行くな.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>早<rt>はや</rt></ruby>くしろ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Hurry up!</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここに<ruby>立<rt>た</rt></ruby>つな。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Don&#x27;t stand here.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜方</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>方<rt>かた</rt></ruby></div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">the way of ~ing / how to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + 方</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Makes a noun, so it takes の rather than を: 漢字の書き方.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">〜方 is method; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜終わる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>終<rt>お</rt></ruby>わる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">finish ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + 終わる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks completion of an activity with duration.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">終わる is finishing;
