<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜続ける</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>続<rt>つづ</rt></ruby>ける</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">continue ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + 続ける</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An action carried on without stopping.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">続ける is deliberate continuation; 続く is something continuing by itself.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>三時<rt>さんじ</rt></ruby><ruby>間<rt>ま</rt></ruby><ruby>走<rt>はし</rt></ruby>り<ruby>続<rt>つづ</rt></ruby>けました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I kept running for three hours.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>し<ruby>続<rt>つづ</rt></ruby>けている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He keeps on talking.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜間に</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N4</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>間<rt>あいだ</rt></ruby>に</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">while / during ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ている / Noun の + 間に</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">With に, something happens at one point inside the period; without に, it continues throughout.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">間に is a point within; 間 is the whole span.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>母<rt>はは</rt></ruby>が<ruby>寝<rt>ね</rt></ruby>ている<ruby>間<rt>ま</rt></ruby>に、<ruby>掃除<rt>そうじ</rt></ruby>をしました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">While my mother was sleeping, I cleaned.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>夏休<rt>なつやす</rt></ruby>みの<ruby>間<rt>ま</rt></ruby>に<ruby>日本語<rt>にほんご</rt></ruby>を<ruby>勉強<rt>べんきょう</rt></ruby>します。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ll study Japanese during the summer holiday.</div></div>	jlpt::N4
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜あまり</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜あまり</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">so much that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun の / plain form + あまり</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Excess of emotion causing a result, in written style.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">あまり is a cause from excess; すぎる judges the excess itself.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>驚<rt>おどろ</rt></ruby>きのあまり、<ruby>声<rt>こえ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>出<rt>で</rt></ruby>なかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I was so surprised that I couldn&#x27;t speak.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>心配<rt>しんぱい</rt></ruby>するあまり、<ruby>眠<rt>ねむ</rt></ruby>れなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I worried so much that I couldn&#x27;t sleep.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜おかげで</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜おかげで</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">thanks to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun の + おかげで</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Credits a good outcome to a cause.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">おかげで is for good results; せいで for bad ones.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>のおかげで<ruby>合格<rt>ごうかく</rt></ruby>しました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Thanks to my teacher, I passed.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>薬<rt>くすり</rt></ruby>を<ruby>飲<rt>の</rt></ruby>んだおかげで、よくなった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Thanks to taking medicine, I got better.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたのおかげで<ruby>仕事<rt>しごと</rt></ruby>がうまく<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Thanks to you, the job went well.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>奨学<rt>しょうがく</rt></ruby><ruby>金<rt>きん</rt></ruby>のおかげで<ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>大学<rt>だいがく</rt></ruby>に<ruby>進学<rt>しんがく</rt></ruby>できた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The scholarship enable her to go to college.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜おそれがある</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜おそれがある</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">there is a risk that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun の + おそれがある</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal warning of a bad possibility, common in news.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">おそれがある is a risk; かもしれない is a neutral guess.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>台風<rt>たいふう</rt></ruby>が<ruby>上陸<rt>じょうりく</rt></ruby>するおそれがあります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is a risk the typhoon will make landfall.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>事故<rt>じこ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>起<rt>お</rt></ruby>こるおそれがある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is a danger of an accident.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そういう<ruby>事故<rt>じこ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>再発<rt>さいはつ</rt></ruby>するおそれがある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Such an accident is likely to happen again.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>小屋<rt>こや</rt></ruby>は<ruby>倒壊<rt>とうかい</rt></ruby>のおそれがある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This hut is in danger of falling down.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かける</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かける</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">start to ~ / half-do</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + かける</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An action begun but not finished.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">かける is half-done; 始める is simply beginning.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>読<rt>よ</rt></ruby>みかけの<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>が<ruby>机<rt>つくえ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>上<rt>うえ</rt></ruby>にある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">A half-read book is on the desk.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>いかけてやめた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I started to say it, then stopped.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かのようだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かのようだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">as if ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + かのようだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A simile for something contrary to fact, mostly written.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">かのようだ is literary; ようだ is plain.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はまるで<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>も<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>らないかのように<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>した。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He spoke as if he knew nothing at all.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>夢<rt>ゆめ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>ているかのようだった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It was as if I were dreaming.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>僕<rt>ぼく</rt></ruby>をうそつきであるかのように<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He made me out to be a liar.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>がうそつきであるかのように<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He made me out to be a liar.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜からといって</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜からといって</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">just because ~ doesn&#x27;t mean</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + からといって</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Rejects a hasty inference; the second clause is negative.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">からといって denies the inference; から asserts the reason.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>高<rt>たか</rt></ruby>いからといって、いいとは<ruby>限<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>らない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Just because it&#x27;s expensive doesn&#x27;t mean it&#x27;s good.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>若<rt>わか</rt></ruby>いからといって、<ruby>無理<rt>むり</rt></ruby>はできない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Just because you&#x27;re young doesn&#x27;t mean you can overdo it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>正直<rt>しょうじき</rt></ruby>だからといって<ruby>成功<rt>せいこう</rt></ruby>するとは<ruby>限<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>らない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Honesty is no guarantee of success.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>が<ruby>貧<rt>まず</rt></ruby>しいからといって<ruby>軽蔑<rt>けいべつ</rt></ruby>すべきではない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You should not despise a man because he is poor.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜からには</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜からには</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">now that / since ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + からには</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Once the premise holds, a firm resolve or duty follows.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">からには resolves; から merely explains.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>約束<rt>やくそく</rt></ruby>したからには、<ruby>必<rt>かなら</rt></ruby>ず<ruby>守<rt>まも</rt></ruby>ります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Now that I&#x27;ve promised, I will certainly keep it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">やるからには<ruby>全力<rt>ぜんりょく</rt></ruby>でやる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Since I&#x27;m doing it, I&#x27;ll do it with all my strength.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここにいるからには<ruby>風呂<rt>ふろ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>入<rt>はい</rt></ruby>ったほうがいい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">As long as you&#x27;re here, you&#x27;d better take a bath.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">どうせやるからには<ruby>上手<rt>じょうず</rt></ruby>にやるようにしなさい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If you do it at all, try to do it well.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がたい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がたい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">hard to ~ (emotionally)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + がたい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Difficult in principle rather than physically. Common: 信じがたい, 忘れがたい.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">がたい is abstract difficulty; にくい is practical.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>信<rt>しん</rt></ruby>じがたい<ruby>話<rt>はなし</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s a hard-to-believe story.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>忘<rt>わす</rt></ruby>れがたい<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>い<ruby>出<rt>で</rt></ruby>。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">An unforgettable memory.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がち</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がち</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">tend to / apt to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem / Noun + がち</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An undesirable tendency.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">がち is a tendency; っぽい is a resemblance.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>冬<rt>ふゆ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>風邪<rt>かぜ</rt></ruby>をひきがちです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I tend to catch colds in winter.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>遅<rt>おく</rt></ruby>れがちだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He tends to be late.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜きり</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜きり</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">only / ever since</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / Verb た-form + きり</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">With a past verb and a negative it means nothing has happened since.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">きり emphasises nothing following; だけ merely limits.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>とは<ruby>去年<rt>きょねん</rt></ruby><ruby>会<rt>あ</rt></ruby>ったきりです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I haven&#x27;t seen him since we met last year.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>二人<rt>ふたり</rt></ruby>きりで<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>した。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We talked just the two of us.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜きる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜きる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">do ~ completely</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + きる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Carry an action through to the end. 〜きれない means unable to finish.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">きる is completing; しまう can imply regret.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">マラソンを<ruby>走<rt>はし</rt></ruby>りきりました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I ran the whole marathon.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>多<rt>おお</rt></ruby>すぎて<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べきれない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There&#x27;s too much to finish eating.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜くせに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜くせに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even though (critical)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / な-adjective な / Noun の + くせに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Concessive with clear disapproval of the subject.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">くせに criticises; のに is merely surprised.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>っているくせに<ruby>教<rt>おし</rt></ruby>えてくれない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even though he knows, he won&#x27;t tell me.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>どものくせに<ruby>生意気<rt>なまいき</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He&#x27;s cheeky for a child.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜くらい・ぐらい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜くらい・ぐらい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">to the extent that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + くらい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Illustrates a degree by example.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">くらい shows degree; ほど can also form comparisons.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>歩<rt>ある</rt></ruby>けないくらい<ruby>疲<rt>つか</rt></ruby>れた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I was so tired I could hardly walk.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>泣<rt>な</rt></ruby>きたいくらい<ruby>悔<rt>くや</rt></ruby>しい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;m so frustrated I could cry.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことか</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことか</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">how ~ it is!</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Question word + plain form + ことか</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An exclamation of strong feeling, usually with どんなに or なんと.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ことか exclaims; ことだ advises.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">どんなに<ruby>心配<rt>しんぱい</rt></ruby>したことか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How worried I was!</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>何<rt>なん</rt></ruby><ruby>度<rt>ど</rt></ruby><ruby>注意<rt>ちゅうい</rt></ruby>したことか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How many times I warned you!</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことにしている</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことにしている</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">make a point of ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary / ない-form + ことにしている</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A personal rule kept over time.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ことにしている is self-imposed; ことになっている comes from outside.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>毎朝<rt>まいあさ</rt></ruby><ruby>走<rt>はし</rt></ruby>ることにしています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I make a point of running every morning.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>夜<rt>よる</rt></ruby>は<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べないことにしている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I make it a rule not to eat at night.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>九時<rt>くじ</rt></ruby><ruby>以降<rt>いこう</rt></ruby>はテレビを<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>ないことにしている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I make it a rule not to watch television after nine o&#x27;clock.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はいつも<ruby>夜<rt>よる</rt></ruby><ruby>更<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>かしをすることにしている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He makes it a rule to sit up late at night.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことになっている</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことになっている</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">it is the rule that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary / ない-form + ことになっている</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A standing rule, schedule or custom.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ことになっている is a rule; ことにしている is one&#x27;s own habit.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここでは<ruby>靴<rt>くつ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>脱<rt>ぬ</rt></ruby>ぐことになっています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The rule here is to take off your shoes.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>九時<rt>くじ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>集合<rt>しゅうごう</rt></ruby>することになっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We are supposed to meet at nine.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ジェーンは<ruby>来月<rt>らいげつ</rt></ruby><ruby>結婚<rt>けっこん</rt></ruby>することになっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Jane is to be married next month.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ベル<ruby>氏<rt>し</rt></ruby>が<ruby>今晩<rt>こんばん</rt></ruby>、<ruby>演説<rt>えんぜつ</rt></ruby>することになっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Mr. Bell is due to make a speech tonight.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことはない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことはない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">there&#x27;s no need to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + ことはない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Reassures that an action is unnecessary.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ことはない is no need; てはいけない is prohibition.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>心配<rt>しんぱい</rt></ruby>することはありません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There&#x27;s no need to worry.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>謝<rt>あやま</rt></ruby>ることはないよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You don&#x27;t need to apologise.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>ももっていない<ruby>者<rt>もの</rt></ruby>は<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>も<ruby>失<rt>うしな</rt></ruby>うことはない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Those who possess nothing lose nothing.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>己<rt>おのれ</rt></ruby>をしること、これほど<ruby>大切<rt>たいせつ</rt></ruby>なことはない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Nothing is more important than this: to know yourself.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜さえ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜さえ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + さえ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Presents an extreme example. さえ〜ば means &quot;if only&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">さえ is emphatic; も is neutral addition.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>どもさえ<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>っている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even a child knows it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>水<rt>みず</rt></ruby>さえ<ruby>飲<rt>の</rt></ruby>めなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I couldn&#x27;t even drink water.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜さえ〜ば</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜さえ〜ば</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">if only ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + さえ + ば-form</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A single sufficient condition.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">さえ〜ば is sole sufficiency; ば is a plain condition.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>金<rt>かね</rt></ruby>さえあれば、<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>でも<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>える。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If only I had money, I could buy anything.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>さえよければ、すぐ<ruby>始<rt>はじ</rt></ruby>めよう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If only you&#x27;re all right with it, let&#x27;s start now.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜させてください</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜させてください</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">please let me ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Causative て-form + ください</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A polite request for permission to act.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">させてください asks permission; てください asks the other to act.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>少<rt>すこ</rt></ruby>し<ruby>考<rt>かんが</rt></ruby>えさせてください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please let me think a little.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby><ruby>休<rt>やす</rt></ruby>ませてください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please let me take tomorrow off.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もう<ruby>一度<rt>いちど</rt></ruby>、その<ruby>計算<rt>けいさん</rt></ruby>をやり<ruby>直<rt>なお</rt></ruby>させてください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Let me go over the accounts again.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ウッドさん、<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>仕事<rt>しごと</rt></ruby>をさせてください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please give him a job, Mr Wood.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ずにはいられない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ずにはいられない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">can&#x27;t help but ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-stem + ずにはいられない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An impulse that cannot be resisted.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ずにはいられない is an urge to act; てならない is a feeling.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あの<ruby>話<rt>はなし</rt></ruby>を<ruby>聞<rt>き</rt></ruby>くと、<ruby>笑<rt>わら</rt></ruby>わずにはいられない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">When I hear that story, I can&#x27;t help laughing.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>心配<rt>しんぱい</rt></ruby>せずにはいられなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I couldn&#x27;t help worrying.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>間違<rt>まちが</rt></ruby>っていると<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>わずにはいられない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I can&#x27;t help but think you&#x27;re making a mistake.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は、<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>才能<rt>さいのう</rt></ruby>を<ruby>賞賛<rt>しょうさん</rt></ruby>せずにはいられない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I can&#x27;t help admiring his talent.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜せいで</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜せいで</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">because of ~ (blame)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun の + せいで</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Assigns blame for an undesirable outcome.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">せいで blames; おかげで credits; ために is neutral.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>のせいで<ruby>試合<rt>しあい</rt></ruby>が<ruby>中止<rt>ちゅうし</rt></ruby>になった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Because of the rain, the match was cancelled.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>寝坊<rt>ねぼう</rt></ruby>したせいで<ruby>遅刻<rt>ちこく</rt></ruby>しました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I was late because I overslept.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜せる・させる (permission)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜せる・させる (permission)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">let someone ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Causative + てあげる / てくれる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The causative plus a giving verb reads as permission rather than force.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">させてあげる is letting; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たとたん</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たとたん</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">the moment ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form + とたん</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Something unexpected happens the instant the first action ends.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">とたん is unplanned and sudden; と is a regular consequence.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>家<rt>いえ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>出<rt>で</rt></ruby>たとたん、<ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>降<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>り<ruby>出<rt>だ</rt></ruby>した。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The moment I left the house, it started raining.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>座<rt>すわ</rt></ruby>ったとたんに<ruby>電話<rt>でんわ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>鳴<rt>な</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The instant I sat down, the phone rang.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たびに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たびに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">every time ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun の + たびに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Each occurrence brings the same result.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">たびに is every single time; ときは is merely when.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>会<rt>あ</rt></ruby>うたびに<ruby>元気<rt>げんき</rt></ruby>をもらう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Every time I meet him, I feel encouraged.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>旅行<rt>りょこう</rt></ruby>のたびに<ruby>写真<rt>しゃしん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>撮<rt>と</rt></ruby>ります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Every time I travel, I take photos.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たらいい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たらいい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">it would be good if ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form + らいい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Advice or a wish. たらいいのに expresses regret.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">たらいい suggests; ほうがいい advises more firmly.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>早<rt>はや</rt></ruby>く<ruby>帰<rt>かえ</rt></ruby>ったらいいですよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It would be good if you went home early.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>止<rt>や</rt></ruby>んだらいいのに。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I wish the rain would stop.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">いつ<ruby>行<rt>おこな</rt></ruby>ったらいいのか<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>に<ruby>教<rt>おし</rt></ruby>えてください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please tell me when to go.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>金<rt>かね</rt></ruby>をもっと<ruby>持<rt>も</rt></ruby>っていたらいいのにと<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>う。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I wish I had more money with me.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だけでなく</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だけでなく</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not only ~ but also</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / plain form + だけでなく</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Often paired with も in the second clause.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">だけでなく is neutral; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だらけ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だらけ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">covered in / full of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + だらけ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An unpleasant abundance, often of something physical.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">だらけ is negative; いっぱい is neutral.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>部屋<rt>へや</rt></ruby>がほこりだらけだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The room is covered in dust.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>間違<rt>まちが</rt></ruby>いだらけの<ruby>作文<rt>さくぶん</rt></ruby>。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">A composition full of mistakes.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だらけ・〜まみれ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だらけ・〜まみれ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">covered in ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + だらけ / まみれ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">まみれ is for substances physically clinging: 泥まみれ, 汗まみれ.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">まみれ is physical coating; だらけ can be abstract (間違いだらけ).</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>どもたちは<ruby>泥<rt>どろ</rt></ruby>まみれになった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The children got covered in mud.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>汗<rt>あせ</rt></ruby>まみれで<ruby>帰<rt>かえ</rt></ruby>ってきた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He came home drenched in sweat.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜っぽい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜っぽい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">-ish / tends to be ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / Verb ます-stem / adjective stem + っぽい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Colloquial, often mildly negative.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">っぽい is casual; らしい means befitting.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>忘<rt>わす</rt></ruby>れっぽい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He is forgetful.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>色<rt>いろ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>少<rt>すこ</rt></ruby>し<ruby>赤<rt>あか</rt></ruby>っぽいですね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This colour is a little reddish.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ついでに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ついでに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">while you&#x27;re at it / on the same occasion</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary / た-form / Noun の + ついでに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A secondary action taken advantage of the main one.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ついでに is incidental; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜つつある</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜つつある</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">be in the process of ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + つつある</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A gradual change under way, in formal or written style.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">つつある is written; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てしかたがない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てしかたがない</div><div 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form / adjective くて + しかたがない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A feeling beyond the speaker&#x27;s control. しょうがない is the <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てたまらない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てたまらない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">unbearably ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form / adjective くて + たまらない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A physical or emotional state too strong to endure.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">てたまらない is spontaneous; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てはじめて</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てはじめて</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">only after ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + はじめて</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Realisation that came only through the experience.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">てはじめて marks realisation; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てほしい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てほしい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">want someone to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + ほしい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The person asked takes に. <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ということだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ということだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">I hear that / it means that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + ということだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Reports information, or restates a conclusion.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ということだ reports; わけだ concludes.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>会議<rt>かいぎ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>中止<rt>ちゅうし</rt></ruby>ということです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I hear the meeting has been cancelled.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">つまり、<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>来<rt>こ</rt></ruby>ないということだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In other words, it means he isn&#x27;t coming.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">それでは、あなたは<ruby>来<rt>こ</rt></ruby>ないということですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Does that mean you won&#x27;t come?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もうこの<ruby>案<rt>あん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>決<rt>き</rt></ruby>まったということですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Do you mean that you have already decided on this plan?</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とおりに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とおりに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">just as ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary / た-form / Noun の + とおりに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Exactly following an instruction or model.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">とおりに follows exactly; ように is looser.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>説明<rt>せつめい</rt></ruby><ruby>書<rt>しょ</rt></ruby>のとおりに<ruby>組<rt>く</rt></ruby>み<ruby>立<rt>た</rt></ruby>てました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I assembled it just as the manual said.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>が<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>ったとおりにやってください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please do it exactly as the teacher said.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>の<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>うとおりにしなくてはいけない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You are to do as I tell you.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>の<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>うとおりにしなければならない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You must do as I tell you.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜として</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜として</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">as / in the capacity of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + として</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">States a role, status or category.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">として is a role; にとって is a viewpoint.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>医者<rt>いしゃ</rt></ruby>として<ruby>働<rt>はたら</rt></ruby>いています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He works as a doctor.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>留学生<rt>りゅうがくせい</rt></ruby>として<ruby>日本<rt>にっぽん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>来<rt>き</rt></ruby>ました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I came to Japan as an exchange student.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜として〜ない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not a single ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">一 + style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Not a single person objected.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一日<rt>いちにち</rt></ruby>として<ruby>忘<rt>わす</rt></ruby>れたことはない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have not style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Now he is recognized as one of the most promising writers.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とは限らない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とは<ruby>限<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>らない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not necessarily ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + とは限らない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Denies that something always holds.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">とは限らない denies universality;
