<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜どころか</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜どころか</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">far from ~ / let alone</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + どころか</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Denies the first item and asserts something stronger.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">どころか overturns; だけでなく merely adds.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>漢字<rt>かんじ</rt></ruby>どころか、ひらがなも<ruby>読<rt>よ</rt></ruby>めない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Far from kanji, he can&#x27;t even read hiragana.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>安<rt>やす</rt></ruby>いどころか、とても<ruby>高<rt>たか</rt></ruby>かった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Far from cheap, it was very expensive.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">やむどころか、<ruby>嵐<rt>あらし</rt></ruby>はいっそう<ruby>激<rt>はげ</rt></ruby>しくなった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Far from stopping, the storm became much more intense.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>我々<rt>われわれ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>交渉<rt>こうしょう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>失敗<rt>しっぱい</rt></ruby>どころか、<ruby>大<rt>だい</rt></ruby><ruby>成功<rt>せいこう</rt></ruby>だった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Far from being a failure, our negotiation was a great success.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないわけにはいかない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないわけにはいかない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">cannot avoid ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜において</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜において</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">in / at / regarding (formal)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + において</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal marker of setting, era or field. <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にしては</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にしては</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">considering that it&#x27;s ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + にしては</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The result does not match what the standard would predict.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にしては judges against a standard; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にしても</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にしても</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even if / even considering ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + にしても</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Concedes a point but maintains the main claim.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にしても concedes; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜について</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜について</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">about / concerning ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + について</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks the topic of speaking, writing or thinking.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">について is the topic; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にとって</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にとって</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">for / from the standpoint of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + にとって</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Introduces whose judgement is being given.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にとって is a viewpoint; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + によって</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Three uses: variation, method, and the agent in a passive sentence.</div><div <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜によると</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜によると</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">according to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + によると</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Names the source of hearsay, usually paired with 〜そうだ or 〜らしい.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">によると gives the source; によって gives the means or agent.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>天気<rt>てんき</rt></ruby><ruby>予報<rt>よほう</rt></ruby>によると、<ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby>は<ruby>晴<rt>は</rt></ruby>れるそうです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">According to the forecast, it will be sunny tomorrow.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>新聞<rt>しんぶん</rt></ruby>によると、<ruby>事故<rt>じこ</rt></ruby>があったらしい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">According to the paper, there was an accident.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今日<rt>きょう</rt></ruby>の<ruby>天気<rt>てんき</rt></ruby><ruby>予報<rt>よほう</rt></ruby>によると<ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby>は<ruby>晴<rt>は</rt></ruby>れらしい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Today&#x27;s weather forecast says that it is likely to be fine tomorrow.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>新聞<rt>しんぶん</rt></ruby>によると<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>今日<rt>きょう</rt></ruby>はここに<ruby>来<rt>く</rt></ruby>るらしい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">According to the newspaper, he will be here today.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にわたって</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にわたって</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">over / spanning ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + にわたって</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Covers a whole range of time, space or field.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にわたって is extent; を通じて is a route or medium.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>三日<rt>みっか</rt></ruby><ruby>間<rt>ま</rt></ruby>にわたって<ruby>会議<rt>かいぎ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>行<rt>おこな</rt></ruby>われた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The conference was held over three days.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>広<rt>ひろ</rt></ruby>い<ruby>範囲<rt>はんい</rt></ruby>にわたって<ruby>被害<rt>ひがい</rt></ruby>が<ruby>出<rt>で</rt></ruby>た。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Damage occurred over a wide area.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>男<rt>おとこ</rt></ruby>は５０<ruby>年<rt>ねん</rt></ruby>にわたってその<ruby>国<rt>くに</rt></ruby>を<ruby>支配<rt>しはい</rt></ruby>した。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The man controlled the country for fifty years.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">インドは<ruby>長年<rt>ながねん</rt></ruby>にわたって<ruby>英国<rt>えいこく</rt></ruby>に<ruby>支配<rt>しはい</rt></ruby>されていた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">India was governed by Great Britain for many years.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に加えて</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>加<rt>くわ</rt></ruby>えて</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">in addition to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + に加えて</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Adds a further element, usually in writing.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に加えて is formal; だけでなく is everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>加<rt>くわ</rt></ruby>えて<ruby>風<rt>かぜ</rt></ruby>も<ruby>強<rt>つよ</rt></ruby>くなった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In addition to the rain, the wind grew strong.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>英語<rt>えいご</rt></ruby>に<ruby>加<rt>くわ</rt></ruby>えて<ruby>中国<rt>ちゅうごく</rt></ruby><ruby>語<rt>ご</rt></ruby>も<ruby>学<rt>まな</rt></ruby>んでいる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In addition to English, I&#x27;m learning Chinese.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>事<rt>こと</rt></ruby>に<ruby>加<rt>くわ</rt></ruby>えて、ほかにもまだ<ruby>理由<rt>りゆう</rt></ruby>がある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In addition to this, there are other reasons.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>英語<rt>えいご</rt></ruby>に<ruby>加<rt>くわ</rt></ruby>えて、ドイツ<ruby>語<rt>ご</rt></ruby>も<ruby>勉強<rt>べんきょう</rt></ruby>したい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I want to study German in addition to English.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に基づいて</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>基<rt>もと</rt></ruby>づいて</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">based on ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + に基づいて</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Grounded in data, rules or evidence.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に基づいて is a basis; によって is a means.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>事実<rt>じじつ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>基<rt>もと</rt></ruby>づいて<ruby>書<rt>か</rt></ruby>かれた<ruby>小説<rt>しょうせつ</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is a novel based on fact.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>規則<rt>きそく</rt></ruby>に<ruby>基<rt>もと</rt></ruby>づいて<ruby>処理<rt>しょり</rt></ruby>します。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We will handle it according to the rules.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>報告<rt>ほうこく</rt></ruby>は<ruby>事実<rt>じじつ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>基<rt>もと</rt></ruby>づいていなければならない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The report must be based on the facts.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>物語<rt>ものがたり</rt></ruby>は<ruby>実際<rt>じっさい</rt></ruby>の<ruby>出来事<rt>できごと</rt></ruby>に<ruby>基<rt>もと</rt></ruby>づいている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This story is based on actual events.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に対して</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>対<rt>たい</rt></ruby>して</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">toward / in contrast to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + に対して</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks the target of an attitude, or sets up a contrast.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に対して faces something; について is merely the subject matter.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>に<ruby>対<rt>たい</rt></ruby>して<ruby>失礼<rt>しつれい</rt></ruby>なことを<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I said something rude to the teacher.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>兄<rt>あに</rt></ruby>は<ruby>静<rt>しず</rt></ruby>かなのに<ruby>対<rt>たい</rt></ruby>して、<ruby>弟<rt>おとうと</rt></ruby>はよく<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>す。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In contrast to the quiet older brother, the younger one talks a lot.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ほとんどの<ruby>作家<rt>さっか</rt></ruby>は<ruby>批評<rt>ひひょう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>対<rt>たい</rt></ruby>して<ruby>敏感<rt>びんかん</rt></ruby>である。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Most writers are sensitive to criticism.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>自分<rt>じぶん</rt></ruby>の<ruby>行動<rt>こうどう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>対<rt>たい</rt></ruby>して<ruby>責任<rt>せきにん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>取<rt>と</rt></ruby>りなさい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You&#x27;ll have to answer for your behavior.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に比べて</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>比<rt>くら</rt></ruby>べて</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">compared with ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + に比べて</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Sets a baseline for comparison.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に比べて compares; に対して contrasts or targets.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>去年<rt>きょねん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>比<rt>くら</rt></ruby>べて、<ruby>今年<rt>ことし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>暖<rt>あたた</rt></ruby>かい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Compared with last year, this year is warm.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>都会<rt>とかい</rt></ruby>に<ruby>比<rt>くら</rt></ruby>べて<ruby>田舎<rt>いなか</rt></ruby>は<ruby>静<rt>しず</rt></ruby>かだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Compared with the city, the countryside is quiet.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>作品<rt>さくひん</rt></ruby>は<ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>前<rt>ぜん</rt></ruby>のに<ruby>比<rt>くら</rt></ruby>べて<ruby>進歩<rt>しんぽ</rt></ruby>している。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This work is an improvement on your last.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>はご<ruby>主人<rt>しゅじん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>比<rt>くら</rt></ruby>べてとても<ruby>若<rt>わか</rt></ruby>く<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>えます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She looks very young as against her husband.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に決まっている</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>決<rt>き</rt></ruby>まっている</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">is bound to be ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + に決まっている</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Emphatic and colloquial certainty.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に決まっている is emotional; に違いない is reasoned.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そんな<ruby>話<rt>はなし</rt></ruby>、<ruby>嘘<rt>うそ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>決<rt>き</rt></ruby>まっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">A story like that is bound to be a lie.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby>も<ruby>暑<rt>あつ</rt></ruby>いに<ruby>決<rt>き</rt></ruby>まっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s certain to be hot tomorrow too.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そんな<ruby>計画<rt>けいかく</rt></ruby>は<ruby>失敗<rt>しっぱい</rt></ruby>するに<ruby>決<rt>き</rt></ruby>まっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Such a plan is bound to fail.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">うちの<ruby>母<rt>はは</rt></ruby>はノーと<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>うに<ruby>決<rt>き</rt></ruby>まっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">My mother is certain to say no.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に違いない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>違<rt>ちが</rt></ruby>いない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">must be / surely</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + に違いない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Strong conviction from evidence.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に違いない reasons; に決まっている is emotional.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>字<rt>じ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>書<rt>か</rt></ruby>いたに<ruby>違<rt>ちが</rt></ruby>いない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This handwriting must be his.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>っているに<ruby>違<rt>ちが</rt></ruby>いない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She must know.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そのことはお<ruby>金<rt>かね</rt></ruby>に<ruby>関係<rt>かんけい</rt></ruby>があったに<ruby>違<rt>ちが</rt></ruby>いない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It must have something to do with money.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>機械<rt>きかい</rt></ruby>はどこか<ruby>故障<rt>こしょう</rt></ruby>しているに<ruby>違<rt>ちが</rt></ruby>いない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There must be something wrong with the machine.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に違って</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>違<rt>ちが</rt></ruby>って</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">unlike ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + と違って</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Sets one thing against another.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">と違って contrasts two items directly.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>兄<rt>あに</rt></ruby>と<ruby>違<rt>ちが</rt></ruby>って、<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>運動<rt>うんどう</rt></ruby>が<ruby>苦手<rt>にがて</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Unlike my brother, I&#x27;m bad at sports.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>去年<rt>きょねん</rt></ruby>と<ruby>違<rt>ちが</rt></ruby>って、<ruby>今年<rt>ことし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>雪<rt>ゆき</rt></ruby>が<ruby>多<rt>おお</rt></ruby>い。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Unlike last year, there&#x27;s a lot of snow this year.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ジミーの<ruby>絵<rt>え</rt></ruby>は<ruby>他人<rt>たにん</rt></ruby>のものと<ruby>違<rt>ちが</rt></ruby>っていました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Jimmy&#x27;s pictures were different from other people&#x27;s.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>と<ruby>違<rt>ちが</rt></ruby>ってその<ruby>状況<rt>じょうきょう</rt></ruby><ruby>分析<rt>ぶんせき</rt></ruby>には<ruby>賛成<rt>さんせい</rt></ruby>できないよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I beg to differ, as I disagree with your analysis of the situation.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に関して</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>関<rt>かん</rt></ruby>して</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">regarding ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + に関して</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A formal equivalent of について.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に関して is formal; について neutral.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>件<rt>けん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>関<rt>かん</rt></ruby>して<ruby>質問<rt>しつもん</rt></ruby>があります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have a question regarding that matter.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>環境<rt>かんきょう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>関<rt>かん</rt></ruby>する<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>読<rt>よ</rt></ruby>みました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I read a book concerning the environment.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>事<rt>こと</rt></ruby>に<ruby>関<rt>かん</rt></ruby>してはあなたの<ruby>意見<rt>いけん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>賛成<rt>さんせい</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In this regard, I agree with you.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>件<rt>けん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>関<rt>かん</rt></ruby>して<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>か<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>うことはありますか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Do you have anything to say with regard to this matter?</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ぬ・〜ん</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ぬ・〜ん</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not ~ (archaic negative)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-stem + ぬ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Survives in set phrases and formal writing: 知らぬ, 見ぬ.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ぬ is archaic or literary; ない is standard.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>見知<rt>みし</rt></ruby>らぬ<ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>に<ruby>声<rt>こえ</rt></ruby>をかけられた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">A stranger spoke to me.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>わぬ<ruby>結果<rt>けっか</rt></ruby>になった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It turned out unexpectedly.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はずがない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はずがない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">cannot possibly be ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + はずがない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The speaker&#x27;s reasoning rules the possibility out.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">はずがない reasons it out; わけがない is flatly impossible.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>犯人<rt>はんにん</rt></ruby>のはずがない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He cannot possibly be the culprit.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そんなに<ruby>簡単<rt>かんたん</rt></ruby>なはずがない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It can&#x27;t possibly be that easy.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そのような<ruby>高度<rt>こうど</rt></ruby><ruby>経済<rt>けいざい</rt></ruby><ruby>成長<rt>せいちょう</rt></ruby>が<ruby>続<rt>つづ</rt></ruby>くはずがない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Such drastic economic growth cannot be sustained.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>女<rt>おんな</rt></ruby>の<ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>がそんなことをしたはずがない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The girl can&#x27;t have done such a thing.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ば〜のに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ば〜のに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">if only ~ (regret)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">ば-form + のに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Counterfactual regret about what did not happen.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">のに here regrets; たら〜のに is similar but softer.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もっと<ruby>勉強<rt>べんきょう</rt></ruby>すればよかったのに。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If only you had studied more.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>車<rt>くるま</rt></ruby>があれば<ruby>便利<rt>べんり</rt></ruby>なのに。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If only I had a car, it would be convenient.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ばかりでなく</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ばかりでなく</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not only ~ but also</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / plain form + ばかりでなく</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Adds a further, often bigger, item.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">だけでなく is the everyday equivalent.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>英語<rt>えいご</rt></ruby>ばかりでなく、フランス<ruby>語<rt>ご</rt></ruby>も<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>せる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He speaks not only English but also French.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>ばかりでなく、<ruby>風<rt>かぜ</rt></ruby>も<ruby>強<rt>つよ</rt></ruby>かった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Not only did it rain, but the wind was strong.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなた<ruby>方<rt>かた</rt></ruby>ばかりでなく<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>らも<ruby>普通<rt>ふつう</rt></ruby>の<ruby>人々<rt>ひとびと</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">They, as well as you, are ordinary people.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>勇気<rt>ゆうき</rt></ruby>があるばかりでなく<ruby>賢明<rt>けんめい</rt></ruby>でもある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He is not only brave but wise.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ばかりに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ばかりに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">simply because ~ (bad result)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + ばかりに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">One small cause led to an unfortunate outcome.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ばかりに regrets; おかげで credits.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>寝坊<rt>ねぼう</rt></ruby>したばかりに、<ruby>試験<rt>しけん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>受<rt>う</rt></ruby>けられなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Simply because I overslept, I couldn&#x27;t sit the exam.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一言<rt>ひとこと</rt></ruby><ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>ったばかりに、けんかになった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Just because I said one word, we ended up quarrelling.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>部屋<rt>へや</rt></ruby>は<ruby>引<rt>ひ</rt></ruby>っ<ruby>越<rt>こ</rt></ruby>せるばかりになっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The room is ready for us to move into.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>同意<rt>どうい</rt></ruby>すると<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>わんばかりにうなずいた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He nodded as much as to say, I agree.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ばよかった</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ばよかった</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">should have ~ed</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ば-form + よかった</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Regret over a past choice. Negative: 〜なければよかった.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ばよかった regrets; ほうがよかった compares options.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もっと<ruby>早<rt>はや</rt></ruby>く<ruby>来<rt>く</rt></ruby>ればよかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I should have come earlier.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あんなことを<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>わなければよかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I shouldn&#x27;t have said such a thing.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>別<rt>べつ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>男性<rt>だんせい</rt></ruby>と<ruby>結婚<rt>けっこん</rt></ruby>しさえすればよかったのに。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If only I had married another man.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>はお<ruby>父<rt>とう</rt></ruby>さんの<ruby>忠告<rt>ちゅうこく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>聞<rt>き</rt></ruby>けばよかったのだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You ought to have taken your father&#x27;s advice.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜べきだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜べきだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">should / ought to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + べきだ (する→すべき)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Moral or social obligation. Not used for laws or rules of fact.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">べき is moral duty; はず is logical expectation.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>約束<rt>やくそく</rt></ruby>は<ruby>守<rt>まも</rt></ruby>るべきだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You should keep your promises.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">もっと<ruby>考<rt>かんが</rt></ruby>えるべきでした。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I should have thought more.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたの<ruby>喫煙<rt>きつえん</rt></ruby><ruby>習慣<rt>しゅうかん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>断<rt>た</rt></ruby>つ<ruby>努力<rt>どりょく</rt></ruby>をすべきです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You should try to conquer your smoking habit.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたは<ruby>自分<rt>じぶん</rt></ruby><ruby>自身<rt>じしん</rt></ruby>の<ruby>計画<rt>けいかく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>実行<rt>じっこう</rt></ruby>すべきです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You should carry out your own plan.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ほど</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ほど</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">to the extent of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / plain form + ほど</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks degree. With a negative it forms a comparison: 〜ほど〜ない.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ほど〜ない says A is less than B; より says A exceeds B.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>泣<rt>な</rt></ruby>きたいほど<ruby>嬉<rt>うれ</rt></ruby>しかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I was so happy I could have cried.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今日<rt>きょう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>昨日<rt>きのう</rt></ruby>ほど<ruby>寒<rt>さむ</rt></ruby>くない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Today isn&#x27;t as cold as yesterday.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">will not / probably not</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + まい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Literary negative volition or conjecture.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">まい is literary; ないつもり everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>二度<rt>にど</rt></ruby>と<ruby>同<rt>おな</rt></ruby>じ<ruby>失敗<rt>しっぱい</rt></ruby>はするまい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I will not make the same mistake twice.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>来<rt>く</rt></ruby>るまいと<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I thought he probably wouldn&#x27;t come.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜みたいだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜みたいだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">seems / looks like (casual)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / plain form + みたいだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The spoken counterpart of ようだ; inflects as a な-adjective.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">みたい is casual; ようだ neutral; らしい second-hand.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>疲<rt>つか</rt></ruby>れているみたいです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He seems tired.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>夢<rt>ゆめ</rt></ruby>みたいな<ruby>話<rt>はなし</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s a story like a dream.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">メアリーは<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>が<ruby>好<rt>す</rt></ruby>きではないみたいですね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Mary scarcely seems to care for me, does she?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今日<rt>きょう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>病気<rt>びょうき</rt></ruby>なので、<ruby>会社<rt>かいしゃ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>休<rt>やす</rt></ruby>みたいです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Because I am sick today, I want to absent myself from the company.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ものだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ものだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">used to / it is the nature of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + ものだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">General truths, nostalgic recollection, or gentle admonition.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ものだ is general; ことだ is advice for a case.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>どものころ、よくここで<ruby>遊<rt>あそ</rt></ruby>んだものだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">As a child, I used to play here a lot.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>時間<rt>じかん</rt></ruby>はあっという<ruby>間<rt>ま</rt></ruby>に<ruby>過<rt>す</rt></ruby>ぎるものだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Time passes in a flash, as it does.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">「これは<ruby>誰<rt>だれ</rt></ruby>のイスですか」「<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>のものです」</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Whose chair is this? &quot;It is mine.&quot;</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">いつかまたあなたにお<ruby>会<rt>あ</rt></ruby>いしたいものです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I would like to see you again some day.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ものだから</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ものだから</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">because ~ (explanation)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + ものだから</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Explains, often defensively, why something happened.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ものだから excuses; ので states neutrally.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>電車<rt>でんしゃ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>遅<rt>おく</rt></ruby>れたものだから、<ruby>間<rt>ま</rt></ruby>に<ruby>合<rt>あ</rt></ruby>わなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Because the train was late, I didn&#x27;t make it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>忙<rt>いそが</rt></ruby>しかったものですから、<ruby>連絡<rt>れんらく</rt></ruby>できませんでした。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Because I was busy, I couldn&#x27;t get in touch.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あわてものだから<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はたぶん<ruby>早合点<rt>はやがてん</rt></ruby>するだろう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Being a hasty person, he is likely to jump to conclusions.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>契約<rt>けいやく</rt></ruby>は<ruby>成立<rt>せいりつ</rt></ruby>したようなものだから<ruby>外<rt>そと</rt></ruby>へ<ruby>出<rt>で</rt></ruby>かけてお<ruby>祝<rt>いわ</rt></ruby>いしよう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The contract is in the bag, so let&#x27;s go out and celebrate.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようがない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようがない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">there&#x27;s no way to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + ようがない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">No means exists, regardless of willingness.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ようがない is no method; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようとする</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようとする</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">try to / be about to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Volitional form + とする</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An attempt, or the instant before an action begins.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ようとする is attempting; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようなら</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようなら</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">if it seems that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + ようなら</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A condition based on an observed sign.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ようなら follows observation; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようになっている</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようになっている</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">be designed / set up so that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary / potential form + ようになっている</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Describes how a mechanism or system is arranged.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ようになっている is a design;
