<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜わけがない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜わけがない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">there&#x27;s no way that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + わけがない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Strong denial of possibility.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">わけがない is impossibility; はずがない is confident disbelief.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>がそんなことを<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>うわけがない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There&#x27;s no way he would say such a thing.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">こんな<ruby>簡単<rt>かんたん</rt></ruby>な<ruby>問題<rt>もんだい</rt></ruby>が<ruby>解<rt>と</rt></ruby>けないわけがない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There&#x27;s no way I can&#x27;t solve such an easy problem.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>神<rt>かみ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>った。「<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>に<ruby>勝<rt>か</rt></ruby>てるわけがないだろう。<ruby>有名<rt>ゆうめい</rt></ruby><ruby>選手<rt>せんしゅ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>皆<rt>みな</rt></ruby><ruby>天国<rt>てんごく</rt></ruby>にいるのだ」</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">How can you win, Satan? asked God. &quot;All the famous ballplayers are up here.&quot;</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>には<ruby>日常<rt>にちじょう</rt></ruby><ruby>必需<rt>ひつじゅ</rt></ruby><ruby>品<rt>ひん</rt></ruby>すらない、まして<ruby>贅沢<rt>ぜいたく</rt></ruby><ruby>品<rt>ひん</rt></ruby>はあるわけがない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He has no daily necessities, much less luxuries.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜わけだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜わけだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">that&#x27;s why / no wonder</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + わけだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Draws a natural conclusion from what was just said.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">わけだ concludes; はずだ expects in advance.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>窓<rt>まど</rt></ruby>が<ruby>開<rt>ひら</rt></ruby>いている。<ruby>寒<rt>さむ</rt></ruby>いわけだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The window is open. No wonder it&#x27;s cold.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>十<rt>じゅう</rt></ruby><ruby>年<rt>ねん</rt></ruby><ruby>住<rt>す</rt></ruby>んでいるから、<ruby>日本語<rt>にほんご</rt></ruby>が<ruby>上手<rt>じょうず</rt></ruby>なわけだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He&#x27;s lived here ten years, so of course his Japanese is good.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>偶然<rt>ぐうぜん</rt></ruby>その<ruby>単語<rt>たんご</rt></ruby>にであったわけです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I just came up with the word.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">これらの<ruby>中<rt>なか</rt></ruby>から<ruby>選<rt>えら</rt></ruby>ぶわけですか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Do I take choice among these?</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜わけではない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜わけではない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">it doesn&#x27;t mean that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + わけではない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Partial denial: it corrects an over-general conclusion.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">わけではない denies partly; わけがない denies entirely.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>嫌<rt>きら</rt></ruby>いなわけではないが、あまり<ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s not that I dislike it, but I don&#x27;t eat it much.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>全部<rt>ぜんぶ</rt></ruby><ruby>分<rt>わ</rt></ruby>かったわけではありません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s not that I understood everything.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>仕事<rt>しごと</rt></ruby>は<ruby>誰<rt>だれ</rt></ruby>でも<ruby>良<rt>よ</rt></ruby>いというわけではない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I do not want anyone for this job.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">これらの<ruby>卵<rt>たまご</rt></ruby>が<ruby>全部<rt>ぜんぶ</rt></ruby><ruby>新鮮<rt>しんせん</rt></ruby>というわけではない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">All these eggs are not fresh.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜わけにはいかない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜わけにはいかない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">cannot (for social reasons)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + わけにはいかない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Circumstances forbid it, though it is physically possible.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">わけにはいかない is social; られない is ability.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>大事<rt>だいじ</rt></ruby>な<ruby>会議<rt>かいぎ</rt></ruby>だから、<ruby>休<rt>やす</rt></ruby>むわけにはいかない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s an important meeting, so I can&#x27;t take the day off.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>秘密<rt>ひみつ</rt></ruby>だから、<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>すわけにはいかない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s a secret, so I can&#x27;t talk about it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>がそうすることを<ruby>許<rt>ゆる</rt></ruby>すわけにはいかない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I can&#x27;t allow you to do that.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そんな<ruby>話<rt>はなし</rt></ruby>を<ruby>聞<rt>き</rt></ruby>くと<ruby>笑<rt>わら</rt></ruby>わないわけにはいかない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I cannot help laughing to hear such a story.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をこめて</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をこめて</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">with (feeling) put into it</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + をこめて</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Pours an emotion into an action: 心をこめて, 愛をこめて.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">をこめて is emotional investment; で is a plain means.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>心<rt>こころ</rt></ruby>をこめて<ruby>手紙<rt>てがみ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>書<rt>か</rt></ruby>きました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I wrote the letter with all my heart.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>感謝<rt>かんしゃ</rt></ruby>をこめて<ruby>贈<rt>おく</rt></ruby>ります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I give this with gratitude.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>憎<rt>にく</rt></ruby>しみをこめて<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>た。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She looked at him with hatred.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>心<rt>こころ</rt></ruby>をこめて<ruby>美<rt>うつく</rt></ruby>しい<ruby>歌<rt>うた</rt></ruby>を<ruby>歌<rt>うた</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She sang her sweet song with feeling.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をはじめ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をはじめ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">starting with / including ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + をはじめ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Names the most representative member of a group.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">をはじめ highlights a leading example; など merely lists.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>東京<rt>とうきょう</rt></ruby>をはじめ、<ruby>多<rt>おお</rt></ruby>くの<ruby>都市<rt>とし</rt></ruby>で<ruby>開催<rt>かいさい</rt></ruby>されます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It will be held in many cities, starting with Tokyo.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>社長<rt>しゃちょう</rt></ruby>をはじめ、<ruby>社員<rt>しゃいん</rt></ruby><ruby>全員<rt>ぜんいん</rt></ruby>が<ruby>参加<rt>さんか</rt></ruby>した。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Everyone from the president down took part.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">いつその<ruby>島<rt>しま</rt></ruby>をはじめて<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>たか<ruby>教<rt>おし</rt></ruby>えて<ruby>下<rt>くだ</rt></ruby>さい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Tell me when you first saw the island.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>を<ruby>待<rt>ま</rt></ruby>たないで<ruby>夕食<rt>ゆうしょく</rt></ruby>をはじめてください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Don&#x27;t wait dinner for me.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜を通じて</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜を<ruby>通<rt>つう</rt></ruby>じて</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">through / throughout ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + を通じて</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A medium, or a span covered entirely.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">を通じて is a route or span; によって is an agent.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>友人<rt>ゆうじん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>通<rt>つう</rt></ruby>じて<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>りました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I came to know him through a friend.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一<rt>いち</rt></ruby><ruby>年<rt>ねん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>通<rt>つう</rt></ruby>じて<ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>多<rt>おお</rt></ruby>い。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It rains a lot throughout the year.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>読書<rt>どくしょ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>通<rt>つう</rt></ruby>じて<ruby>多<rt>おお</rt></ruby>くの<ruby>言葉<rt>ことば</rt></ruby>が<ruby>習得<rt>しゅうとく</rt></ruby>される。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Many words are acquired through reading.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">トムとマイクはお<ruby>互<rt>たが</rt></ruby>いの<ruby>友人<rt>ゆうじん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>通<rt>つう</rt></ruby>じて<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>り<ruby>合<rt>あ</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Tom and Mike became acquainted through their mutual friends.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜一方で</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>一方<rt>いっぽう</rt></ruby>で</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">on the other hand / while</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + 一方で</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Sets two coexisting aspects side by side.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">一方で balances; に対して contrasts more sharply.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>仕事<rt>しごと</rt></ruby>は<ruby>忙<rt>いそが</rt></ruby>しい<ruby>一方<rt>いっぽう</rt></ruby>で、やりがいがある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The work is busy, but on the other hand it&#x27;s rewarding.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>物価<rt>ぶっか</rt></ruby>が<ruby>上<rt>あ</rt></ruby>がる<ruby>一方<rt>いっぽう</rt></ruby>で、<ruby>給料<rt>きゅうりょう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>変<rt>か</rt></ruby>わらない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">While prices rise, wages stay the same.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜向き</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>向<rt>む</rt></ruby>き</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">suited to / facing ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + 向き</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Naturally suitable, or physically facing a direction.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">向き is suitability; 向け is deliberate targeting.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>部屋<rt>へや</rt></ruby>は<ruby>南<rt>みなみ</rt></ruby><ruby>向<rt>む</rt></ruby>きです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This room faces south.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>初心者<rt>しょしんしゃ</rt></ruby><ruby>向<rt>む</rt></ruby>きの<ruby>教科書<rt>きょうかしょ</rt></ruby>。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">A textbook suited to beginners.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜向け</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>向<rt>む</rt></ruby>け</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">aimed at ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + 向け</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Made for a particular audience.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">向け is intended for; 向き is suited to.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">これは<ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>ども<ruby>向<rt>む</rt></ruby>けの<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This is a book aimed at children.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>海外<rt>かいがい</rt></ruby><ruby>向<rt>む</rt></ruby>けに<ruby>輸出<rt>ゆしゅつ</rt></ruby>しています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We export it for overseas markets.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜得る</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>得<rt>う</rt></ruby>る</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">can / be possible to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + 得る</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal possibility. Read える or うる in the dictionary form, えない when negative.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">得る is formal possibility; られる is ordinary ability.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">それはあり<ruby>得<rt>え</rt></ruby>ない<ruby>話<rt>はなし</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">That is an impossible story.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>誰<rt>だれ</rt></ruby>にでも<ruby>起<rt>お</rt></ruby>こり<ruby>得<rt>え</rt></ruby>ることです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is something that can happen to anyone.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜最中に</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>最中<rt>さいちゅう</rt></ruby>に</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">right in the middle of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ている / Noun の + 最中に</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An interruption at the peak of an activity.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">最中に is the very middle; 間に is anywhere within.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>食事<rt>しょくじ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>最中<rt>さいちゅう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>電話<rt>でんわ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>鳴<rt>な</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The phone rang right in the middle of the meal.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>会議<rt>かいぎ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>最中<rt>さいちゅう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>地震<rt>じしん</rt></ruby>があった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There was an earthquake in the middle of the meeting.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜次第</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>次第<rt>しだい</rt></ruby></div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">as soon as ~ / depending on ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + 次第 / Noun + 次第だ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal. After a stem it means &quot;as soon as&quot;; after a noun, &quot;depends on&quot;.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">次第 is formal; たらすぐ is everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>到着<rt>とうちゃく</rt></ruby>し<ruby>次第<rt>しだい</rt></ruby>、<ruby>連絡<rt>れんらく</rt></ruby>します。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I will contact you as soon as I arrive.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>天気<rt>てんき</rt></ruby><ruby>次第<rt>しだい</rt></ruby>で<ruby>予定<rt>よてい</rt></ruby>が<ruby>変<rt>か</rt></ruby>わります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The plan changes depending on the weather.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜気味</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N3</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>気味<rt>ぎみ</rt></ruby></div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">a touch of / slightly</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem / Noun + 気味</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A slight, usually unwelcome, tendency.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">気味 is slight; がち is habitual.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>少<rt>すこ</rt></ruby>し<ruby>風邪<rt>かぜ</rt></ruby><ruby>気味<rt>ぎみ</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I have a slight cold.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>最近<rt>さいきん</rt></ruby><ruby>疲<rt>つか</rt></ruby>れ<ruby>気味<rt>ぎみ</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ve been a bit tired lately.</div></div>	jlpt::N3
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜あげく</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜あげく</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">after all that ~, in the end</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form / Noun の + あげく</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A long process ending badly.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">あげく ends badly; 末に is neutral.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">さんざん<ruby>迷<rt>まよ</rt></ruby>ったあげく、<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>わなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">After hesitating a great deal, I didn&#x27;t buy it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>口論<rt>こうろん</rt></ruby>のあげく、<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>出<rt>で</rt></ruby>て<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">After the argument, he left.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かぎり</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かぎり</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">as long as / as far as ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary / ている-form + かぎり</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Sets a boundary of time, knowledge or condition.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ないかぎり means unless.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>が<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>るかぎり、<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>正直<rt>しょうじき</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">As far as I know, he is honest.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>体<rt>からだ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>健康<rt>けんこう</rt></ruby>なかぎり、<ruby>働<rt>はたら</rt></ruby>きたい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">As long as I&#x27;m healthy, I want to work.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かねない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かねない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">might well ~ (something bad)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + かねない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Warns of an undesirable possibility.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">かねない is a bad possibility; かねる is inability.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そんな<ruby>運転<rt>うんてん</rt></ruby>では<ruby>事故<rt>じこ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>起<rt>お</rt></ruby>こしかねない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Driving like that might well cause an accident.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>放<rt>はな</rt></ruby>っておくと、<ruby>大変<rt>たいへん</rt></ruby>なことになりかねない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If left alone, it could well become serious.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>手<rt>て</rt></ruby>の<ruby>雑誌<rt>ざっし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>若者<rt>わかもの</rt></ruby>に<ruby>害<rt>がい</rt></ruby>を<ruby>与<rt>あた</rt></ruby>えかねない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This kind of magazine can do harm to young people.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はどんな<ruby>悪事<rt>あくじ</rt></ruby>でもやりかねない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He&#x27;s capable of wickedness of any kind.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かねる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かねる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">cannot (bring oneself to) ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + かねる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A polite refusal in business language.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">かねる declines politely; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かのように</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かのように</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">as if ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + かのように</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A simile for something not actually so.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">かのように is literary; ように is plain.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>も<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>らないかのように<ruby>振<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>る<ruby>舞<rt>ま</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He behaved as if he knew nothing.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>時<rt>とき</rt></ruby>が<ruby>止<rt>と</rt></ruby>まったかのようだった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It was as if time had stopped.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>僕<rt>ぼく</rt></ruby>をうそつきであるかのように<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He made me out to be a liar.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>がうそつきであるかのように<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He made me out to be a liar.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜からいうと</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜からいうと</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">from the standpoint of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + からいうと</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">States which angle the judgement comes from.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">からいうと is a standpoint; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜からこそ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜からこそ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">precisely because ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + からこそ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Singles out the real reason among possible ones.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">からこそ emphasises the reason; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜からすると</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜からすると</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">judging from ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + からすると</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Draws an inference from evidence.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">からすると infers; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かわりに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かわりに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">instead of / in exchange for ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun の + かわりに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Substitution, or a trade-off between two things.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">かわりに substitutes; ではなく simply negates.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>父<rt>ちち</rt></ruby>のかわりに<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>が<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>きます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I will go instead of my father.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>手伝<rt>てつだ</rt></ruby>うかわりに、<ruby>昼<rt>ひる</rt></ruby>ご<ruby>飯<rt>はん</rt></ruby>をおごって。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In exchange for helping, buy me lunch.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>砂糖<rt>さとう</rt></ruby>のかわりに<ruby>塩<rt>しお</rt></ruby>をあげたら、<ruby>母<rt>はは</rt></ruby>は<ruby>怒<rt>おこ</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I got my mother&#x27;s goat when I gave her the salt instead of the sugar.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">２<ruby>階<rt>かい</rt></ruby>に<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>のかわりにもっていってください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Would you mind carrying it up the stairs for me?</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜きっての</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜きっての</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">the most ~ in</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + きっての + Noun</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Superlative within a group, in written style.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">きっての is literary; で一番 everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>当代<rt>とうだい</rt></ruby>きっての<ruby>名優<rt>めいゆう</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He is the finest actor of the age.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>町<rt>まち</rt></ruby>きっての<ruby>金持<rt>かねも</rt></ruby>ちです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He is the richest man in town.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>田中<rt>たなか</rt></ruby>くんは<ruby>省内<rt>しょうない</rt></ruby>きっての<ruby>馬鹿<rt>ばか</rt></ruby><ruby>者<rt>しゃ</rt></ruby>だよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Young Tanaka is the stupidest person in the department.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">チームきっての<ruby>名<rt>めい</rt></ruby><ruby>選手<rt>せんしゅ</rt></ruby>がけがしなかったら、うちのチームが<ruby>簡単<rt>かんたん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>勝<rt>か</rt></ruby>っていただろう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Our team could easily have brought home the bacon, if it weren&#x27;t for the team&#x27;s best man being injured.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜こそ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜こそ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">precisely / it is ~ that</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + こそ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Emphasises the preceding element.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">こそ emphasises; は merely topicalises.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今<rt>いま</rt></ruby>こそ<ruby>行動<rt>こうどう</rt></ruby>するときだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Now is precisely the time to act.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">こちらこそありがとう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Thank you too (it is I who should thank you).</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことから</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことから</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">from the fact that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + ことから</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Gives the evidence a conclusion or name rests on.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ことから gives grounds; ことだから reasons from character.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>窓<rt>まど</rt></ruby>が<ruby>割<rt>わ</rt></ruby>れていることから、<ruby>泥棒<rt>どろぼう</rt></ruby>が<ruby>入<rt>はい</rt></ruby>ったと<ruby>分<rt>わ</rt></ruby>かった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">From the fact that the window was broken, we knew a thief had entered.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>形<rt>かたち</rt></ruby>が<ruby>似<rt>に</rt></ruby>ていることから、この<ruby>名<rt>な</rt></ruby>がついた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It got this name from the fact that the shape is similar.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>次<rt>つぎ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>質問<rt>しつもん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>立<rt>た</rt></ruby>てることから<ruby>分析<rt>ぶんせき</rt></ruby>を<ruby>始<rt>はじ</rt></ruby>めよう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Let us begin our analysis by positing the following question.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はまず<ruby>朝食<rt>ちょうしょく</rt></ruby>をたっぷりとることから<ruby>始<rt>はじ</rt></ruby>めた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He started off with a good breakfast.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">you should ~ (advice)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary / ない-form + ことだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Direct advice for a particular situation.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ことだ advises a case; ものだ states a general truth.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>風邪<rt>かぜ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>治<rt>なお</rt></ruby>すには、よく<ruby>休<rt>やす</rt></ruby>むことだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">To cure a cold, you should rest well.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>無理<rt>むり</rt></ruby>をしないことだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You shouldn&#x27;t overdo it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>互<rt>たが</rt></ruby>いに<ruby>理解<rt>りかい</rt></ruby>しあうことは<ruby>大切<rt>たいせつ</rt></ruby>なことです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">To understand each other is important.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>種<rt>たね</rt></ruby>の<ruby>経験<rt>けいけん</rt></ruby>は<ruby>誰<rt>だれ</rt></ruby>にでもよくあることです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This kind of experience is familiar to everybody.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことだから</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことだから</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">knowing ~ (as they are)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun の + ことだから</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Predicts behaviour from someone&#x27;s known character.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ことだから uses character; ことから uses evidence.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>のことだから、また<ruby>遅<rt>おく</rt></ruby>れるだろう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Knowing him, he&#x27;ll be late again.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>真面目<rt>まじめ</rt></ruby>な<ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>のことだから、きっとやり<ruby>遂<rt>と</rt></ruby>げる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Being the serious person she is, she&#x27;ll surely see it through.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>急<rt>きゅう</rt></ruby>を<ruby>要<rt>よう</rt></ruby>することだから<ruby>急<rt>いそ</rt></ruby>いでください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please hurry, it&#x27;s urgent.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>自分<rt>じぶん</rt></ruby>でやったことだから<ruby>仕方<rt>しかた</rt></ruby>がないね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You brought it on yourself.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことなく</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ことなく</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">without ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + ことなく</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal written equivalent of ないで.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ことなく is literary; ずに is formal but shorter.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>休<rt>やす</rt></ruby>むことなく<ruby>働<rt>はたら</rt></ruby>き<ruby>続<rt>つづ</rt></ruby>けた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He kept working without resting.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>変<rt>か</rt></ruby>わることなく<ruby>愛<rt>あい</rt></ruby>し<ruby>続<rt>つづ</rt></ruby>けた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He kept loving her without change.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>止<rt>と</rt></ruby>めることなく<ruby>何時<rt>なんじ</rt></ruby><ruby>間<rt>ま</rt></ruby>も<ruby>泣<rt>な</rt></ruby>き<ruby>続<rt>つづ</rt></ruby>けた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She cried for hours without ceasing.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はためらうことなく<ruby>妻<rt>つま</rt></ruby>に<ruby>真実<rt>しんじつ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>語<rt>かた</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He didn&#x27;t hesitate to tell his wife the truth.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ざるを得ない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ざるを<ruby>得<rt>え</rt></ruby>ない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">have no choice but to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-stem + ざるを得ない (する→せざるを得ない)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal, reluctant compulsion.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ざるを得ない is reluctant; なければならない is neutral duty.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>のため、<ruby>中止<rt>ちゅうし</rt></ruby>せざるを<ruby>得<rt>え</rt></ruby>ない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Because of the rain, we have no choice but to cancel.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>認<rt>みと</rt></ruby>めざるを<ruby>得<rt>え</rt></ruby>ない<ruby>事実<rt>じじつ</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is a fact we cannot but acknowledge.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>兄<rt>あに</rt></ruby>を<ruby>死<rt>し</rt></ruby>んだものとあきらめざるを<ruby>得<rt>え</rt></ruby>ない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I cannot but give up my brother for lost.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby><ruby>達<rt>たち</rt></ruby>は<ruby>生活<rt>せいかつ</rt></ruby><ruby>費<rt>ひ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>切<rt>き</rt></ruby>り<ruby>詰<rt>つ</rt></ruby>めざるを<ruby>得<rt>え</rt></ruby>ない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We cannot avoid cutting down our living expenses.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ずじまい</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ずじまい</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">ended up never ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-stem + ずじまい</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A regret that something never got done.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ずじまい regrets omission; なかった is a plain negative.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>結局<rt>けっきょく</rt></ruby>、<ruby>会<rt>あ</rt></ruby>わずじまいだった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In the end, we never did meet.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>わずじまいで<ruby>後悔<rt>こうかい</rt></ruby>している。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I regret having ended up never saying it.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だけあって</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だけあって</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">as one would expect from ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + だけあって</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The quality matches the reputation or background, admiringly.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">だけあって praises; わりに notes a mismatch.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>高<rt>たか</rt></ruby>いだけあって、<ruby>品質<rt>ひんしつ</rt></ruby>がいい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">As you&#x27;d expect from the price, the quality is good.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>十<rt>じゅう</rt></ruby><ruby>年<rt>ねん</rt></ruby><ruby>住<rt>す</rt></ruby>んだだけあって、<ruby>日本語<rt>にほんご</rt></ruby>が<ruby>上手<rt>じょうず</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Having lived here ten years, his Japanese is good, as expected.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>専門<rt>せんもん</rt></ruby><ruby>家<rt>か</rt></ruby>だけあって<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はその<ruby>分野<rt>ぶんや</rt></ruby>に<ruby>詳<rt>くわ</rt></ruby>しい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">As may be expected of an expert, he&#x27;s well versed in the field.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">さすがに<ruby>偉大<rt>いだい</rt></ruby>な<ruby>学者<rt>がくしゃ</rt></ruby>だけあって、<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はその<ruby>問<rt>と</rt></ruby>いに<ruby>容易<rt>ようい</rt></ruby>に<ruby>答<rt>こた</rt></ruby>えた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Like the great scholar that he was, he answered the question easily.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だけに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だけに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">precisely because ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + だけに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The cause makes the effect especially strong.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">だけに intensifies; から states plainly.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>期待<rt>きたい</rt></ruby>していただけに、<ruby>残念<rt>ざんねん</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Precisely because I had hopes, it&#x27;s disappointing.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>努力<rt>どりょく</rt></ruby>しただけに、<ruby>喜<rt>よろこ</rt></ruby>びも<ruby>大<rt>おお</rt></ruby>きい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Because he worked hard, the joy is all the greater.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜っこない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜っこない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">there&#x27;s no way ~ (casual)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + っこない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Emphatic colloquial denial of possibility.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">っこない is casual; わけがない is neutral.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そんな<ruby>話<rt>はなし</rt></ruby>、<ruby>信<rt>しん</rt></ruby>じられっこない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There&#x27;s no way anyone could believe such a story.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一日<rt>いちにち</rt></ruby>でできっこないよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There&#x27;s no way it can be done in a day.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>がどんな<ruby>目<rt>め</rt></ruby>にあったか<ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>にはわかりっこない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You will never realize what I went through.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">それは<ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>だってまちがいっこない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You can&#x27;t mistake it.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜っぱなし</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜っぱなし</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">leave ~ on / keep ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + っぱなし</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Something left in a state, usually carelessly.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">っぱなし is careless neglect; ておく is deliberate.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>電気<rt>でんき</rt></ruby>をつけっぱなしにした。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I left the light on.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>立<rt>た</rt></ruby>ちっぱなしで<ruby>疲<rt>つか</rt></ruby>れた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I got tired from standing the whole time.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一日<rt>いちにち</rt></ruby><ruby>中<rt>ちゅう</rt></ruby><ruby>立<rt>た</rt></ruby>ちっぱなしだったのでくたくただ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;m worn out, because I&#x27;ve been standing all day.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はエンジンをかけっぱなしにしておいた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He left the motor running.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜つつ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜つつ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">while ~ / although</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + つつ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Written equivalent of ながら. つつも adds concession.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">つつ is literary; ながら everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>悪<rt>わる</rt></ruby>いと<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>りつつ、やめられない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Although I know it&#x27;s wrong, I can&#x27;t stop.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>景色<rt>けしき</rt></ruby>を<ruby>眺<rt>なが</rt></ruby>めつつ、<ruby>歩<rt>ある</rt></ruby>いた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I walked while gazing at the scenery.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てからでないと</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てからでないと</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">unless you ~ first</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + からでないと</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The second clause is negative or impossible without the first.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">てからでないと blocks; てから merely sequences.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>許可<rt>きょか</rt></ruby>を<ruby>取<rt>と</rt></ruby>ってからでないと、<ruby>入<rt>い</rt></ruby>れません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You can&#x27;t enter unless you get permission first.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>食<rt>た</rt></ruby>べてからでないと、<ruby>薬<rt>くすり</rt></ruby>は<ruby>飲<rt>の</rt></ruby>めない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You can&#x27;t take the medicine unless you eat first.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てしょうがない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てしょうがない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">extremely / unbearably ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form / adjective くて + しょうがない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Casual spoken form of てしかたがない.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">しょうがない is casual;
