<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てならない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てならない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">can&#x27;t help feeling ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form / adjective くて + ならない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A spontaneous, uncontrollable feeling, in written style.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">てならない is literary; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜というものだ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜というものだ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">that&#x27;s what you call ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + というものだ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Delivers the speaker&#x27;s judgement as a general truth.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">というものだ judges; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜というより</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜というより</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">rather than ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + というより</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Corrects a description with a better one.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">というより replaces; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とともに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とともに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">together with / as ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / Verb dictionary form + とともに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Two things change or occur in parallel.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">とともに is parallel change; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜どころではない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜どころではない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">this is no time for ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / Verb dictionary form + どころではない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Circumstances make the activity out of the question.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">どころではない dismisses; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないかぎり</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないかぎり</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">unless ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-form + かぎり</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The result holds unless the condition changes.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ないかぎり is unless; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないことには</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないことには</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">unless ~, nothing can happen</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-form + ことには</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The first condition is indispensable.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ないことには is a prerequisite; なければ is a plain condition.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>実物<rt>じつぶつ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>ないことには、<ruby>判断<rt>はんだん</rt></ruby>できない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Unless I see the real thing, I can&#x27;t judge.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>始<rt>はじ</rt></ruby>めないことには、<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>も<ruby>変<rt>か</rt></ruby>わらない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Unless you start, nothing will change.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>自分<rt>じぶん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>関<rt>かか</rt></ruby>わりのないことには<ruby>口<rt>くち</rt></ruby>を<ruby>出<rt>だ</rt></ruby>すな。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Don&#x27;t interfere with matters that do not concern you!</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にあたって</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にあたって</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">on the occasion of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun + にあたって</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks an important occasion, in formal speech and writing.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にあたって is a significant moment; ときに is neutral.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>卒業<rt>そつぎょう</rt></ruby>にあたって、<ruby>一言<rt>ひとこと</rt></ruby><ruby>申<rt>もう</rt></ruby>し<ruby>上<rt>あ</rt></ruby>げます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">On the occasion of graduation, I would like to say a word.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>開店<rt>かいてん</rt></ruby>にあたって、<ruby>準備<rt>じゅんび</rt></ruby>を<ruby>進<rt>すす</rt></ruby>めています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In preparation for the opening, we are making arrangements.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>金<rt>かね</rt></ruby>のことはジェーンにあたってみるよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I&#x27;ll try Jane for some money.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>たちが<ruby>到着<rt>とうちゃく</rt></ruby>する<ruby>日<rt>ひ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>日曜日<rt>にちようび</rt></ruby>にあたっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The day when we will arrive falls on Sunday.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にかかわらず</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にかかわらず</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">regardless of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / plain form + にかかわらず</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The condition does not affect the outcome.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にかかわらず ignores conditions; にもかかわらず concedes a fact.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>天候<rt>てんこう</rt></ruby>にかかわらず、<ruby>決行<rt>けっこう</rt></ruby>します。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We will go ahead regardless of the weather.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>経験<rt>けいけん</rt></ruby>の<ruby>有無<rt>うむ</rt></ruby>にかかわらず<ruby>応募<rt>おうぼ</rt></ruby>できます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You may apply regardless of experience.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>結婚式<rt>けっこんしき</rt></ruby>は<ruby>天候<rt>てんこう</rt></ruby>にかかわらず<ruby>催<rt>もよお</rt></ruby>されるだろう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The wedding ceremony will be held regardless of the weather.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>年齢<rt>ねんれい</rt></ruby>にかかわらず<ruby>全<rt>すべ</rt></ruby>ての<ruby>人々<rt>ひとびと</rt></ruby>が<ruby>許可<rt>きょか</rt></ruby>されます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Every person will be admitted regardless of his or her age.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にこたえて</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にこたえて</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">in response to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + にこたえて</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Answers a request, expectation or demand.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にこたえて answers expectations; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にしたがって</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にしたがって</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">in accordance with / as ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun + にしたがって</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Following a change, an instruction or a rule.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にしたがって can mean obey; につれて is only change.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>指示<rt>しじ</rt></ruby>にしたがって<ruby>行動<rt>こうどう</rt></ruby>してください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please act in accordance with the instructions.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>年<rt>とし</rt></ruby>をとるにしたがって、<ruby>丸<rt>まる</rt></ruby>くなった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">As he aged, he mellowed.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>命令<rt>めいれい</rt></ruby>にしたがって<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>はボートを<ruby>売<rt>う</rt></ruby>ろう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I will sell the boat in accordance with your orders.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>信念<rt>しんねん</rt></ruby>にしたがって<ruby>行動<rt>こうどう</rt></ruby>するのは<ruby>難<rt>むずか</rt></ruby>しい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is hard to live up to your convictions.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にすれば</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にすれば</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">from ~&#x27;s point of view</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun (person) + にすれば</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Presents how a person is likely to feel.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にすれば is a person&#x27;s view; にとって is broader.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>親<rt>おや</rt></ruby>にすれば<ruby>心配<rt>しんぱい</rt></ruby>なのだろう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">From a parent&#x27;s point of view, it must be worrying.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>にすれば<ruby>当然<rt>とうぜん</rt></ruby>の<ruby>要求<rt>ようきゅう</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">From his point of view it&#x27;s a natural demand.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>短気<rt>たんき</rt></ruby>なのを<ruby>別<rt>べつ</rt></ruby>にすれば、<ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>申<rt>もう</rt></ruby>し<ruby>分<rt>ぶん</rt></ruby>がない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Apart from her temper, she&#x27;s all right.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>の<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>う<ruby>通<rt>とお</rt></ruby>りにすれば<ruby>間違<rt>まちが</rt></ruby>いはないよ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You can&#x27;t go wrong if you are advised by me.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜につれて</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜につれて</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">as ~ progresses</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun + につれて</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Gradual, proportional change.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">につれて is gradual; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にほかならない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にほかならない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">is nothing other than ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + にほかならない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Emphatic identification of a cause or essence.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にほかならない affirms strongly; に過ぎない diminishes.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>成功<rt>せいこう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>努力<rt>どりょく</rt></ruby>の<ruby>結果<rt>けっか</rt></ruby>にほかならない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Success is nothing other than the result of effort.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">これは<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>責任<rt>せきにん</rt></ruby>にほかならない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This is nothing but his responsibility.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>世界<rt>せかい</rt></ruby>の<ruby>歴史<rt>れきし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>自由<rt>じゆう</rt></ruby><ruby>意識<rt>いしき</rt></ruby>の<ruby>進歩<rt>しんぽ</rt></ruby>にほかならない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The history of the world is none other than the progress of the consciousness of freedom.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にもかかわらず</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にもかかわらず</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">despite / in spite of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + にもかかわらず</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Strong formal concession; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に伴って</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>伴<rt>ともな</rt></ruby>って</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">accompanying / as ~ changes</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / Verb dictionary form + に伴って</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">One change brings another with it.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に伴って is a consequence; とともに is simple parallelism.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>人口<rt>じんこう</rt></ruby>の<ruby>増加<rt>ぞうか</rt></ruby>に<ruby>伴<rt>ともな</rt></ruby>って、<ruby>住宅<rt>じゅうたく</rt></ruby>が<ruby>不足<rt>ふそく</rt></ruby>している。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">With the population increase, housing is short.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>経済<rt>けいざい</rt></ruby>の<ruby>発展<rt>はってん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>伴<rt>ともな</rt></ruby>い、<ruby>生活<rt>せいかつ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>変<rt>か</rt></ruby>わった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">As the economy developed, life changed.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に応じて</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>応<rt>おう</rt></ruby>じて</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">in accordance with / depending on ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + に応じて</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Adjusts flexibly to match a changing condition.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に応じて adapts; に基づいて follows a fixed basis.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>収入<rt>しゅうにゅう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>応<rt>おう</rt></ruby>じて<ruby>税金<rt>ぜいきん</rt></ruby>が<ruby>決<rt>き</rt></ruby>まります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Tax is determined according to income.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>必要<rt>ひつよう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>応<rt>おう</rt></ruby>じて<ruby>対応<rt>たいおう</rt></ruby>します。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We will respond as necessary.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>会社<rt>かいしゃ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>の<ruby>注文<rt>ちゅうもん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>応<rt>おう</rt></ruby>じてくれなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The company couldn&#x27;t fill my order.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>能力<rt>のうりょく</rt></ruby>に<ruby>応<rt>おう</rt></ruby>じて<ruby>働<rt>はたら</rt></ruby>き、<ruby>必要<rt>ひつよう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>応<rt>おう</rt></ruby>じて<ruby>受<rt>う</rt></ruby>け<ruby>取<rt>と</rt></ruby>る。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">From each according to their ability, to each according to their needs.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に相違ない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>相違<rt>そうい</rt></ruby>ない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">there is no doubt that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + に相違ない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A very formal, written equivalent of に違いない.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に相違ない is legal or literary; に違いない everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>犯人<rt>はんにん</rt></ruby>であるに<ruby>相違<rt>そうい</rt></ruby>ない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is no doubt that he is the culprit.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>本人<rt>ほんにん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>相違<rt>そうい</rt></ruby>ありません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is no doubt it is the person himself.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に過ぎない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>過<rt>す</rt></ruby>ぎない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">is nothing more than ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + に過ぎない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Downplays the significance of something.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に過ぎない minimises; だけ merely limits.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">それは<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>い<ruby>訳<rt>わけ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>過<rt>す</rt></ruby>ぎない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">That is nothing more than an excuse.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>一<rt>いち</rt></ruby><ruby>社員<rt>しゃいん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>過<rt>す</rt></ruby>ぎません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I am merely one employee.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>男<rt>おとこ</rt></ruby>はただ<ruby>大<rt>おお</rt></ruby>きく<ruby>育<rt>そだ</rt></ruby>った<ruby>子供<rt>こども</rt></ruby>に<ruby>過<rt>す</rt></ruby>ぎない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Men are but children of a larger growth.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">それじゃ<ruby>女<rt>おんな</rt></ruby>は<ruby>大<rt>おお</rt></ruby>きく<ruby>育<rt>そだ</rt></ruby>った<ruby>子供<rt>こども</rt></ruby>に<ruby>過<rt>す</rt></ruby>ぎない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Women, then, are only children of a larger growth.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に限って</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>限<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>って</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">of all times / only when it&#x27;s ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + に限って</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Bad luck strikes precisely on that occasion.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に限って is ironic timing; だけ is neutral limitation.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>急<rt>いそ</rt></ruby>いでいる<ruby>時<rt>とき</rt></ruby>に<ruby>限<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>って、<ruby>電車<rt>でんしゃ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>遅<rt>おく</rt></ruby>れる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The train is delayed precisely when I&#x27;m in a hurry.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>傘<rt>かさ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>忘<rt>わす</rt></ruby>れた<ruby>日<rt>ひ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>限<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>って<ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>降<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>る。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It rains exactly on the day I forget my umbrella.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">「<ruby>飲<rt>の</rt></ruby>み<ruby>物<rt>もの</rt></ruby>は<ruby>無料<rt>むりょう</rt></ruby>ですか」「ご<ruby>婦人<rt>ふじん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>限<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>ってです」</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Are the drinks free? &quot;Only for ladies.&quot;</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>急<rt>いそ</rt></ruby>いでいる<ruby>時<rt>とき</rt></ruby>に<ruby>限<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>ってバスが<ruby>遅<rt>おく</rt></ruby>れる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Our bus comes late, particularly when we are in a hurry.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に限らず</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>限<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>らず</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not limited to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + に限らず</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Extends beyond the named case.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に限らず widens; に限って narrows.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>若者<rt>わかもの</rt></ruby>に<ruby>限<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>らず、<ruby>高齢<rt>こうれい</rt></ruby><ruby>者<rt>しゃ</rt></ruby>にも<ruby>人気<rt>にんき</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s popular not only with young people but with the elderly.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>週末<rt>しゅうまつ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>限<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>らず、<ruby>平日<rt>へいじつ</rt></ruby>も<ruby>混<rt>こ</rt></ruby>んでいる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s crowded on weekdays too, not just weekends.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ここ<ruby>数<rt>すう</rt></ruby><ruby>年<rt>ねん</rt></ruby>、コンピューターは<ruby>仕事<rt>しごと</rt></ruby>に<ruby>限<rt>かぎ</rt></ruby>らず<ruby>広<rt>ひろ</rt></ruby>く<ruby>利用<rt>りよう</rt></ruby>されるようになりました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Recently, the increasing diversity of computer use has extended far beyond the realms of the office.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に際して</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>際<rt>さい</rt></ruby>して</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">at the time of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun + に際して</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal, for a notable occasion or turning point.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に際して is very formal; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ぬきで</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ぬきで</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">without ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + ぬきで</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Leaves something out deliberately.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ぬきで omits; なしで simply lacks.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>冗談<rt>じょうだん</rt></ruby>ぬきで、<ruby>本当<rt>ほんとう</rt></ruby>の<ruby>話<rt>はなし</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Joking aside, it&#x27;s a true story.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>朝食<rt>ちょうしょく</rt></ruby>ぬきで<ruby>出<rt>で</rt></ruby>かけた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I went out without breakfast.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のに対して</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のに<ruby>対<rt>たい</rt></ruby>して</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">whereas ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + のに対して</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Sets two facts in direct contrast.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">のに対して contrasts two subjects; 一方で balances aspects.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>兄<rt>あに</rt></ruby>は<ruby>文系<rt>ぶんけい</rt></ruby>なのに<ruby>対<rt>たい</rt></ruby>して、<ruby>弟<rt>おとうと</rt></ruby>は<ruby>理系<rt>りけい</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Whereas the elder brother is in humanities, the younger is in science.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>都会<rt>とかい</rt></ruby>は<ruby>便利<rt>べんり</rt></ruby>なのに<ruby>対<rt>たい</rt></ruby>して、<ruby>田舎<rt>いなか</rt></ruby>は<ruby>静<rt>しず</rt></ruby>かだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Whereas the city is convenient, the countryside is quiet.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>時<rt>とき</rt></ruby><ruby>以来<rt>いらい</rt></ruby>、<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>自分<rt>じぶん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>虐待<rt>ぎゃくたい</rt></ruby>するものに<ruby>対<rt>たい</rt></ruby>して<ruby>尽<rt>つ</rt></ruby>きる<ruby>事<rt>こと</rt></ruby>のない<ruby>憎<rt>にく</rt></ruby>しみを<ruby>感<rt>かん</rt></ruby>じるようになった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">From that moment on, he felt undying hatred for his oppressors.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のみならず</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のみならず</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not only ~ but also</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + のみならず</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal written equivalent of だけでなく.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">のみならず is literary; だけでなく everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>国内<rt>こくない</rt></ruby>のみならず、<ruby>海外<rt>かいがい</rt></ruby>でも<ruby>有名<rt>ゆうめい</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He is famous not only at home but abroad.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>のみならず、<ruby>雷<rt>かみなり</rt></ruby>も<ruby>鳴<rt>な</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Not only did it rain, but it thundered.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>のみならず<ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>息子<rt>むすこ</rt></ruby><ruby>達<rt>たち</rt></ruby>も<ruby>幸<rt>しあわ</rt></ruby>せだった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Her sons as well as she were happy.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>英語<rt>えいご</rt></ruby>のみならずフランス<ruby>語<rt>ご</rt></ruby>も<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>すことができる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He can speak not only English but also French.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のもとで</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜のもとで</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">under (the guidance of) ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + のもとで</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Under someone&#x27;s supervision or a set of conditions.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">のもとで is under influence; の下に is physical.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>のもとで<ruby>研究<rt>けんきゅう</rt></ruby>しています。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I am researching under my professor.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>厳<rt>きび</rt></ruby>しい<ruby>条件<rt>じょうけん</rt></ruby>のもとで<ruby>働<rt>はたら</rt></ruby>いた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I worked under harsh conditions.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ブラウン<ruby>先生<rt>せんせい</rt></ruby>のもとで<ruby>数学<rt>すうがく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>学<rt>まな</rt></ruby>びました。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I studied mathematics under Dr. Brown.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>法<rt>ほう</rt></ruby>のもとではすべての<ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>は<ruby>平等<rt>びょうどう</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">All men are equal under the law.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はもちろん</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はもちろん</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not to mention ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + はもちろん</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The first item is obvious; the second is the notable addition.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">はもちろん assumes the obvious; だけでなく merely adds.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>漢字<rt>かんじ</rt></ruby>はもちろん、ひらがなも<ruby>書<rt>か</rt></ruby>けない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Not to mention kanji, he can&#x27;t even write hiragana.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>英語<rt>えいご</rt></ruby>はもちろん、<ruby>中国<rt>ちゅうごく</rt></ruby><ruby>語<rt>ご</rt></ruby>も<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>せる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He speaks English, of course, and Chinese too.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>たちに<ruby>着物<rt>きもの</rt></ruby>はもちろんお<ruby>金<rt>かね</rt></ruby>もくれた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He gave us not only clothes but some money.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>英語<rt>えいご</rt></ruby>はもちろんのことドイツ<ruby>語<rt>ご</rt></ruby>も<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>す。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He speaks German, not to mention English.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はんめん</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はんめん</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">on the other hand</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + 反面</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Two opposite sides of the same thing.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">反面 shows two sides; 一方で sets two things side by side.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>仕事<rt>しごと</rt></ruby>は<ruby>楽<rt>たの</rt></ruby>しい<ruby>反面<rt>はんめん</rt></ruby>、<ruby>責任<rt>せきにん</rt></ruby>も<ruby>重<rt>おも</rt></ruby>い。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This job is enjoyable, but on the other hand the responsibility is heavy.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>便利<rt>べんり</rt></ruby>な<ruby>反面<rt>はんめん</rt></ruby>、<ruby>危険<rt>きけん</rt></ruby>もある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is convenient, but on the other hand there are dangers.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ばかりか</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ばかりか</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not only ~ but even</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + ばかりか</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The second item exceeds the first in degree.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ばかりか escalates; だけでなく simply adds.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>遅刻<rt>ちこく</rt></ruby>したばかりか、<ruby>謝<rt>あやま</rt></ruby>りもしなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Not only was he late, he didn&#x27;t even apologise.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>ばかりか、<ruby>風<rt>かぜ</rt></ruby>まで<ruby>強<rt>つよ</rt></ruby>くなった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Not only did it rain, but the wind got strong too.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は１００ドルばかりかそれ<ruby>以上<rt>いじょう</rt></ruby><ruby>持<rt>も</rt></ruby>っている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He has not less than 100 dollars.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>美人<rt>びじん</rt></ruby>であるばかりか<ruby>気立<rt>きだ</rt></ruby>てもいい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She is not only beautiful but also amiable.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜べきではない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜べきではない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">should not ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + べきではない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Moral prohibition rather than a rule.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">べきではない is moral; てはいけない is a rule.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>の<ruby>悪口<rt>わるぐち</rt></ruby>を<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>うべきではない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You should not speak ill of others.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">そんな<ruby>約束<rt>やくそく</rt></ruby>をするべきではなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You should not have made such a promise.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あなたは<ruby>自分<rt>じぶん</rt></ruby>の<ruby>意見<rt>いけん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>固執<rt>こしつ</rt></ruby>すべきではない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You should not stick to your opinion.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">きみはそんなにタバコを<ruby>吸<rt>す</rt></ruby>うべきではない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">You ought not to smoke so much.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜も〜ば〜も</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜も〜ば〜も</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">both ~ and ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + も + ば-form + Noun + も</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Lists two coordinated qualities or facts.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">A parallel listing pattern, more formal than と.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div money nor time.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>高価<rt>こうか</rt></ruby>なものもあれば、<ruby>大変<rt>たいへん</rt></ruby><ruby>安<rt>やす</rt></ruby>いものもある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Some are expensive, and others are very cheap.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ものがある</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ものがある</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">there is something ~ about it</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / な-adjective な + ものがある</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The speaker senses a quality worth remarking on.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ものがある senses a quality; ことがある reports occurrence.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>演奏<rt>えんそう</rt></ruby>には<ruby>心<rt>こころ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>打<rt>う</rt></ruby>つものがある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is something moving about his performance.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>考<rt>かんが</rt></ruby>えには<ruby>納得<rt>なっとく</rt></ruby>できないものがある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is something unconvincing about that idea.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>問題<rt>もんだい</rt></ruby>の<ruby>中<rt>なか</rt></ruby>にはゴリラに<ruby>不公平<rt>ふこうへい</rt></ruby>なものがある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Some test questions are unfair to gorillas.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>性格<rt>せいかく</rt></ruby>には<ruby>単<rt>たん</rt></ruby>なる<ruby>正直<rt>しょうじき</rt></ruby><ruby>以上<rt>いじょう</rt></ruby>のものがある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is more in his character than simple honesty.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ものの</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ものの</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">although ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + ものの</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal concession; the expected consequence does not follow.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ものの is written; けれども is spoken.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>ったものの、<ruby>一度<rt>いちど</rt></ruby>も<ruby>使<rt>つか</rt></ruby>っていない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Although I bought it, I have never used it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>約束<rt>やくそく</rt></ruby>はしたものの、<ruby>自信<rt>じしん</rt></ruby>がない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I made the promise, but I have no confidence.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようでは</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようでは</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">if it&#x27;s the case that ~ (bad)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + ようでは</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A negative outcome is expected from the situation.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ようでは predicts a bad outcome; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜わりに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜わりに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">considering / for a ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun の + わりに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The result is out of proportion to what the standard suggests.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">わりに weighs proportion; にしては compares to a standard.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>値段<rt>ねだん</rt></ruby>のわりにおいしい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s tasty for the price.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>年<rt>とし</rt></ruby>のわりに<ruby>若<rt>わか</rt></ruby>く<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>える。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He looks young for his age.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をとわず</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をとわず</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">regardless of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + を問わず</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The distinction named does not matter.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">を問わず dismisses distinctions; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をめぐって</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をめぐって</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">concerning / surrounding ~ (dispute)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + をめぐって</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks a contested issue that people argue over.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">をめぐって implies dispute;
