<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をもとに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をもとに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">based on ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + をもとに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Uses something as raw material or a model.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">をもとに is source material; に基づいて is a governing basis.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>実話<rt>じつわ</rt></ruby>をもとに<ruby>作<rt>つく</rt></ruby>られた<ruby>映画<rt>えいが</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is a film made based on a true story.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">アンケートをもとに<ruby>分析<rt>ぶんせき</rt></ruby>した。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I analysed it based on the survey.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>映画<rt>えいが</rt></ruby>は<ruby>小説<rt>しょうせつ</rt></ruby>をもとにしている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This film is an adaptation of a novel.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>親譲<rt>おやゆず</rt></ruby>りの<ruby>財産<rt>ざいさん</rt></ruby>をもとにして<ruby>富<rt>とみ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>作<rt>つく</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He built on his father&#x27;s fortune.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜上で</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>上<rt>うえ</rt></ruby>で</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">after ~ing / upon</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form / Noun の + 上で</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Do the first thing, then decide or act on that basis.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">上で is a deliberate prerequisite; てから is mere sequence.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">よく<ruby>考<rt>かんが</rt></ruby>えた<ruby>上<rt>うえ</rt></ruby>で、<ruby>返事<rt>へんじ</rt></ruby>をします。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I will reply after thinking it over carefully.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>確認<rt>かくにん</rt></ruby>の<ruby>上<rt>うえ</rt></ruby>で、<ruby>手続<rt>てつづ</rt></ruby>きをしてください。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Please proceed after confirming.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜上に</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>上<rt>うえ</rt></ruby>に</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">on top of / in addition to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / な-adjective な / Noun の + 上に</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Both clauses point the same way, good or bad.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">上に adds in the same direction; のに contrasts.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">この<ruby>店<rt>みせ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>安<rt>やす</rt></ruby>い<ruby>上<rt>うえ</rt></ruby>に、おいしい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This shop is cheap, and on top of that, tasty.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>道<rt>みち</rt></ruby>に<ruby>迷<rt>まよ</rt></ruby>った<ruby>上<rt>うえ</rt></ruby>に、<ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>も<ruby>降<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>ってきた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">On top of getting lost, it started to rain.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜以上は</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>以上<rt>いじょう</rt></ruby>は</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">now that / since ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + 以上は</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Given the premise, a firm conclusion or duty follows.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">以上は is formal; からには is slightly softer.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>引<rt>ひ</rt></ruby>き<ruby>受<rt>う</rt></ruby>けた<ruby>以上<rt>いじょう</rt></ruby>は、<ruby>最後<rt>さいご</rt></ruby>までやります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Now that I&#x27;ve taken it on, I&#x27;ll see it through.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>学生<rt>がくせい</rt></ruby>である<ruby>以上<rt>いじょう</rt></ruby>、<ruby>勉強<rt>べんきょう</rt></ruby>すべきだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">As long as you are a student, you should study.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜向けに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>向<rt>む</rt></ruby>けに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">intended for ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + 向けに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Deliberately aimed at an audience or market.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">向けに targets; 向きに suits.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>ども<ruby>向<rt>む</rt></ruby>けに<ruby>書<rt>か</rt></ruby>かれた<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is a book written for children.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>海外<rt>かいがい</rt></ruby><ruby>向<rt>む</rt></ruby>けに<ruby>製品<rt>せいひん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>作<rt>つく</rt></ruby>っている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We make products for overseas markets.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜末に</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>末<rt>すえ</rt></ruby>に</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">after ~, finally</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form / Noun の + 末に</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A long effort reaching a conclusion, good or bad.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">末に is neutral; あげく is negative.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>長<rt>なが</rt></ruby>い<ruby>議論<rt>ぎろん</rt></ruby>の<ruby>末<rt>すえ</rt></ruby>に、<ruby>結論<rt>けつろん</rt></ruby>が<ruby>出<rt>で</rt></ruby>た。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">After a long discussion, a conclusion was reached.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>悩<rt>なや</rt></ruby>んだ<ruby>末<rt>すえ</rt></ruby>に、<ruby>留学<rt>りゅうがく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>決<rt>き</rt></ruby>めた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">After much worry, I decided to study abroad.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜次第だ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>次第<rt>しだい</rt></ruby>だ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">depends on ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + 次第だ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The outcome rests entirely on the named factor.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">次第だ depends; によって varies.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>成功<rt>せいこう</rt></ruby>するかどうかは<ruby>努力<rt>どりょく</rt></ruby><ruby>次第<rt>しだい</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Whether you succeed depends on your effort.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">すべては<ruby>本人<rt>ほんにん</rt></ruby><ruby>次第<rt>しだい</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Everything depends on the person themselves.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>成功<rt>せいこう</rt></ruby>するかいなかは、<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>健康<rt>けんこう</rt></ruby><ruby>次第<rt>しだい</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Whether he will succeed or not depends upon his health.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>我々<rt>われわれ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>明日<rt>あした</rt></ruby><ruby>出発<rt>しゅっぱつ</rt></ruby>するかどうかは<ruby>天候<rt>てんこう</rt></ruby><ruby>次第<rt>しだい</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Our departure tomorrow is contingent on fair weather.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜次第で</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N2</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜<ruby>次第<rt>しだい</rt></ruby>で</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">depending on ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + 次第で</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The result varies with the named factor.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">次第で is a decisive factor; によって is general variation.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">やり<ruby>方<rt>かた</rt></ruby><ruby>次第<rt>しだい</rt></ruby>で<ruby>結果<rt>けっか</rt></ruby>が<ruby>変<rt>か</rt></ruby>わる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Results change depending on how you do it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>天気<rt>てんき</rt></ruby><ruby>次第<rt>しだい</rt></ruby>で<ruby>中止<rt>ちゅうし</rt></ruby>になります。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It will be cancelled depending on the weather.</div></div>	jlpt::N2
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜あっての</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜あっての</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">only possible because of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + あっての + Noun</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Without the first, the second could not exist.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">あっての is a precondition; おかげで is gratitude.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">お<ruby>客様<rt>きゃくさま</rt></ruby>あっての<ruby>商売<rt>しょうばい</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Business exists only because of customers.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>努力<rt>どりょく</rt></ruby>あっての<ruby>成功<rt>せいこう</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Success exists only because of effort.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>命<rt>いのち</rt></ruby>あっての<ruby>物種<rt>ものだね</rt></ruby>。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">While there is life, there is hope.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">なんの<ruby>気<rt>き</rt></ruby>なしに<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>っただけで、<ruby>別<rt>べつ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>悪気<rt>わるぎ</rt></ruby>があってのことではありません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I just said so without thinking much about it. I didn&#x27;t mean anything serious.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜いかんによっては</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜いかんによっては</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">depending on ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + いかんによっては</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Very formal: the outcome hinges on the named factor.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">いかん is bureaucratic; 次第 is ordinary formal.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>結果<rt>けっか</rt></ruby>のいかんによっては、<ruby>計画<rt>けいかく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>変更<rt>へんこう</rt></ruby>する。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Depending on the results, we will change the plan.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>事情<rt>じじょう</rt></ruby>のいかんによらず、<ruby>受<rt>う</rt></ruby>け<ruby>付<rt>つ</rt></ruby>けます。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We will accept it regardless of circumstances.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜いかんによらず</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜いかんによらず</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">regardless of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + のいかんによらず</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal, common in official notices.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">いかんによらず is bureaucratic; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かたわら</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜かたわら</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">while also ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun の + かたわら</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A secondary long-term activity alongside a main one.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">かたわら is a parallel occupation; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がてら</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がてら</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">while also doing ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem / Noun + がてら</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">One outing serving two purposes.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">がてら is a combined errand; ついでに is incidental.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>散歩<rt>さんぽ</rt></ruby>がてら<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>い<ruby>物<rt>もの</rt></ruby>に<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I went shopping while taking a walk.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>見物<rt>けんぶつ</rt></ruby>がてら<ruby>訪<rt>たず</rt></ruby>ねてみた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I visited while sightseeing.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がゆえに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜がゆえに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">because of ~ (literary)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun (である) + がゆえに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal written causation.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">がゆえに is literary; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜が早いか</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜が<ruby>早<rt>はや</rt></ruby>いか</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">the instant ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + が早いか</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Two actions almost simultaneous; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜が最後</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜が<ruby>最後<rt>さいご</rt></ruby></div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">once ~ happens, that&#x27;s it</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb た-form + が最後</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">After this point the bad result is unavoidable.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">が最後 is inevitable doom; たら is a plain condition.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>寝<rt>ね</rt></ruby>たが<ruby>最後<rt>さいご</rt></ruby>、<ruby>朝<rt>あさ</rt></ruby>まで<ruby>起<rt>お</rt></ruby>きない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Once he falls asleep, he won&#x27;t wake until morning.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>ったが<ruby>最後<rt>さいご</rt></ruby>、<ruby>後戻<rt>あともど</rt></ruby>りできない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Once you say it, there&#x27;s no going back.</div></div>	jlpt::N1

<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ずにはすまない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ずにはすまない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">cannot get away without ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-stem + ずにはすまない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Social pressure makes the action unavoidable.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ずにはすまない is social duty; ざるを得ない is circumstance.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>壊<rt>こわ</rt></ruby>したのだから、<ruby>弁償<rt>べんしょう</rt></ruby>せずにはすまない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Since I broke it, I can&#x27;t get away without paying for it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>謝<rt>あやま</rt></ruby>らずにはすまないだろう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He won&#x27;t get away without apologising.</div></div>	jlpt::N1

<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たりとも</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜たりとも</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">not even a single ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">一 + counter + たりとも + negative</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Emphatic total denial, formal.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">たりとも is formal; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜だに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even just ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun + だに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Fixed literary phrases: 想像だに, 微動だにしない.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">だに is archaic; さえ is modern.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>想像<rt>そうぞう</rt></ruby>だにしなかった<ruby>出来事<rt>できごと</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It was an event I had not even imagined.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>微動<rt>びどう</rt></ruby>だにしなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He did not stir in the slightest.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てからというもの</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜てからというもの</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">ever since ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + からというもの</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A lasting change dating from one event.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">てからというもの stresses lasting change; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜であれ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜であれ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">whatever / even if it is ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / question word + であれ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Concedes any case whatsoever.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">であれ is literary; でも is everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">どんな<ruby>理由<rt>りゆう</rt></ruby>であれ、<ruby>暴力<rt>ぼうりょく</rt></ruby>は<ruby>許<rt>ゆる</rt></ruby>されない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Whatever the reason, violence is not permitted.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>誰<rt>だれ</rt></ruby>であれ、<ruby>規則<rt>きそく</rt></ruby>は<ruby>守<rt>まも</rt></ruby>るべきだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Whoever it is, the rules should be kept.</div></div>	jlpt::N1

style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">であろうと and であれ are interchangeable in most contexts.</div><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">どんな<ruby>困難<rt>こんなん</rt></ruby>であろうと<ruby>乗<rt>の</rt></ruby>り<ruby>越<rt>こ</rt></ruby>える。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Whatever the difficulty, I will overcome it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>相手<rt>あいて</rt></ruby>が<ruby>誰<rt>だれ</rt></ruby>であろうと<ruby>関係<rt>かんけい</rt></ruby>ない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It doesn&#x27;t matter who the other party is.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">きっと<ruby>事態<rt>じたい</rt></ruby>は<ruby>改善<rt>かいぜん</rt></ruby>されるであろうと<ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The situation, I am sure, will be improved.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>何事<rt>なにごと</rt></ruby>であろうとも、<ruby>忍耐<rt>にんたい</rt></ruby>しなくてはならない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Be the matter what it may, we must persevere.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ですら</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ですら</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even ~ (emphatic)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + ですら</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Emphatic even, presenting an extreme case.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ですら is emphatic; でも is neutral.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>専門<rt>せんもん</rt></ruby><ruby>家<rt>か</rt></ruby>ですら<ruby>分<rt>わ</rt></ruby>からない<ruby>問題<rt>もんだい</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is a problem even experts cannot solve.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>親<rt>おや</rt></ruby>ですら<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>らなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even his parents did not know.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とあって</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とあって</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">because it is ~ (special situation)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / plain form + とあって</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A special circumstance produces a natural result.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">とあって explains a special case; ので is general.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>連休<rt>れんきゅう</rt></ruby>とあって、<ruby>道路<rt>どうろ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>混<rt>こ</rt></ruby>んでいる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Being a long weekend, the roads are crowded.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>人気<rt>にんき</rt></ruby><ruby>歌手<rt>かしゅ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>来<rt>く</rt></ruby>るとあって、<ruby>会場<rt>かいじょう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>満員<rt>まんいん</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">With a popular singer coming, the venue is full.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>帽子<rt>ぼうし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>靴<rt>くつ</rt></ruby>とあっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Her hat matches her shoes.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>金<rt>きん</rt></ruby>がうんとあって<ruby>使<rt>つか</rt></ruby>えない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He has more money than he can use.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とあれば</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とあれば</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">if it is for ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / plain form + とあれば</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">For this special reason the speaker will do anything.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">とあれば is a special condition; なら is general.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>どものためとあれば、<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>でもする。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If it&#x27;s for my child, I&#x27;ll do anything.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>必要<rt>ひつよう</rt></ruby>とあれば、すぐ<ruby>伺<rt>うかが</rt></ruby>います。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If it is necessary, I will come at once.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>と<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>し<ruby>合<rt>あ</rt></ruby>う<ruby>時間<rt>じかん</rt></ruby>がもっとあればいいのに。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I wish I had the time to stay and talk with you.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ぜひにとあれば<ruby>仕方<rt>しかた</rt></ruby>がない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If you must, you must.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜といえども</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜といえども</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even though / even ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / plain form + といえども</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Literary concession, often with an authority or extreme case.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">といえども is archaic and formal; でも is everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>専門<rt>せんもん</rt></ruby><ruby>家<rt>か</rt></ruby>といえども、<ruby>間違<rt>まちが</rt></ruby>うことはある。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even an expert can make mistakes.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>どもといえども<ruby>許<rt>ゆる</rt></ruby>されない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even a child would not be excused.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>指摘<rt>してき</rt></ruby>、<ruby>中<rt>あた</rt></ruby>らずといえども<ruby>遠<rt>とお</rt></ruby>からずだね。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Your point may be a little off target, but it certainly is close.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>貧<rt>まず</rt></ruby>しいといえども<ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>幸<rt>しあわ</rt></ruby>せだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Though she is poor, she is happy.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜といったらない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜といったらない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">extremely / indescribably ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / adjective + といったらない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An emotional emphasis, positive or negative.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">といったらない is emotional; きわまりない is formal.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あの<ruby>時<rt>とき</rt></ruby>の<ruby>嬉<rt>うれ</rt></ruby>しさといったらなかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">My joy at that moment was indescribable.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>暑<rt>あつ</rt></ruby>いといったらない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It&#x27;s unbearably hot.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ところを</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ところを</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">although it is a time when ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun の / plain form + ところを</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Set courtesy phrase acknowledging the listener&#x27;s inconvenience.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ところを is a polite formula; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜としたところで</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜としたところで</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even if / even supposing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + としたところで</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Concedes a hypothesis while denying its usefulness.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">としたところで concedes a hypothesis; にしても is broader.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今<rt>いま</rt></ruby>から<ruby>急<rt>いそ</rt></ruby>いだところで、<ruby>間<rt>ま</rt></ruby>に<ruby>合<rt>あ</rt></ruby>わない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even if we hurry now, we won&#x27;t make it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>謝<rt>あやま</rt></ruby>ったところで<ruby>許<rt>ゆる</rt></ruby>されないだろう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Even if you apologise, you probably won&#x27;t be forgiven.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とはいうものの</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とはいうものの</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">having said that ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + とはいうものの</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Concedes then qualifies, in written style.</div><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>暖<rt>あたた</rt></ruby>かくなったとはいうものの、<ruby>朝晩<rt>あさばん</rt></ruby>は<ruby>冷<rt>ひ</rt></ruby>える。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It has warmed up, but mornings and evenings are still cold.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Convenient though it is, it&#x27;s too expensive.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とはいえ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とはいえ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">although / that said</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / Noun + とはいえ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Concedes the previous statement, then qualifies it.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">とはいえ concedes formally; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とばかりに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜とばかりに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">as if to say ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form / quotation + とばかりに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Behaviour that conveys an unspoken message.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">とばかりに shows attitude; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ともなく</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ともなく</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">without particularly ~ing</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + ともなく</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An action done absent-mindedly.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ともなく is unintentional; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ともなると</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ともなると</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">once it reaches the stage of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / Verb dictionary form + ともなると</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">At this level, things naturally differ.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ともなると marks a threshold; になると is plain.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>社長<rt>しゃちょう</rt></ruby>ともなると、<ruby>責任<rt>せきにん</rt></ruby>が<ruby>重<rt>おも</rt></ruby>い。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Once you become president, the responsibility is heavy.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>冬<rt>ふゆ</rt></ruby>ともなると、<ruby>雪<rt>ゆき</rt></ruby>が<ruby>深<rt>ふか</rt></ruby>くなる。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Once winter comes, the snow gets deep.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないではおかない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないではおかない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">will inevitably ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-form + ではおかない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Strong determination, or an effect that must follow.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ないではおかない is forceful; ずにはいられない is an urge.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>真実<rt>しんじつ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>明<rt>あき</rt></ruby>らかにしないではおかない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I will not rest until the truth is revealed.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>話<rt>はなし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>を<ruby>感動<rt>かんどう</rt></ruby>させないではおかない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">That story cannot fail to move people.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないまでも</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないまでも</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even if not ~, at least</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-form + までも</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Sets a lower acceptable standard.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ないまでも lowers the bar; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないものか</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ないものか</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">isn&#x27;t there some way to ~?</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-form + ものか</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A wish that something could be done.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ないものか wishes;
