<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ながらに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ながらに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">while remaining ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / Verb ます-stem + ながらに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Set expressions: 涙ながらに, 生まれながらに.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ながらに is fixed and literary; ながら is productive.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>涙<rt>なみだ</rt></ruby>ながらに<ruby>語<rt>かた</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She spoke through her tears.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>生<rt>う</rt></ruby>まれながらに<ruby>才能<rt>さいのう</rt></ruby>があった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He had talent from birth.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>は<ruby>生<rt>う</rt></ruby>まれながらに<ruby>平等<rt>びょうどう</rt></ruby>といえるだろうか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Is it valid to say that all men are created equal?</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">すべての<ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>は<ruby>生<rt>う</rt></ruby>まれながらに<ruby>平等<rt>びょうどう</rt></ruby>である。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">All men are created equal.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ならいざしらず</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ならいざしらず</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">if it were ~ that would be one thing, but</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / plain form + ならいざしらず</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Sets an acceptable case against the unacceptable actual one.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ならいざしらず contrasts cases; なら is a plain condition.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>子<rt>こ</rt></ruby>どもならいざしらず、<ruby>大人<rt>おとな</rt></ruby>がそんなことをするとは。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If it were a child that would be one thing, but for an adult to do that...</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>昔<rt>むかし</rt></ruby>ならいざしらず、<ruby>今<rt>いま</rt></ruby>は<ruby>通用<rt>つうよう</rt></ruby>しない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It might have worked in the past, but not now.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ならでは</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ならでは</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">unique to / only possible for ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + ならでは</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Praises something only that person or place can offer.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ならでは praises uniqueness; だけ merely limits.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">これは<ruby>京都<rt>きょうと</rt></ruby>ならではの<ruby>味<rt>あじ</rt></ruby>です。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This is a flavour unique to Kyoto.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>職人<rt>しょくにん</rt></ruby>ならではの<ruby>技<rt>わざ</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is a skill only a craftsman has.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なり</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜なり</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">the moment ~ (unexpected)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + なり</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The subject does something surprising immediately after.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">なり implies surprise; や否や is neutral immediacy.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>部屋<rt>へや</rt></ruby>に<ruby>入<rt>はい</rt></ruby>るなり、<ruby>泣<rt>な</rt></ruby>き<ruby>出<rt>だ</rt></ruby>した。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The moment he entered the room, he burst into tears.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>席<rt>せき</rt></ruby>に<ruby>着<rt>つ</rt></ruby>くなり、<ruby>話<rt>はな</rt></ruby>し<ruby>始<rt>はじ</rt></ruby>めた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">As soon as he sat down, he started talking.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にして</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にして</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">only at / even at ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + にして</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Marks a notable stage or standard: 六十にして, 一瞬にして.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にして marks a threshold; で is plain.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>六<rt>ろく</rt></ruby><ruby>十<rt>じゅう</rt></ruby>にして<ruby>新<rt>あたら</rt></ruby>しい<ruby>趣味<rt>しゅみ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>始<rt>はじ</rt></ruby>めた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">At sixty, he took up a new hobby.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一瞬<rt>いっしゅん</rt></ruby>にして<ruby>景色<rt>けしき</rt></ruby>が<ruby>変<rt>か</rt></ruby>わった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In an instant the scenery changed.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にたえない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にたえない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">unbearable to ~ / beyond words</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun + にたえない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Either too painful to watch, or an overwhelming feeling.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にたえない is literary; てたまらない is spoken.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>光景<rt>こうけい</rt></ruby>は<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>るにたえない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">That scene is unbearable to look at.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>感謝<rt>かんしゃ</rt></ruby>にたえません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I am more grateful than I can say.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜には及ばない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜には<ruby>及<rt>およ</rt></ruby>ばない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">there is no need to / not as good as</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun + には及ばない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Two senses: unnecessary, or falling short in comparison.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">には及ばない dismisses need; までもない stresses obviousness.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">わざわざ<ruby>来<rt>く</rt></ruby>るには<ruby>及<rt>およ</rt></ruby>びません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is no need for you to come all this way.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>腕前<rt>うでまえ</rt></ruby>には<ruby>及<rt>およ</rt></ruby>ばない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I am no match for his skill.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>現在<rt>げんざい</rt></ruby>における<ruby>果物<rt>くだもの</rt></ruby>の<ruby>供給<rt>きょうきゅう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>需要<rt>じゅよう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>及<rt>およ</rt></ruby>ばない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Present supplies of fruit are short of requirements.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>僕<rt>ぼく</rt></ruby>のバイクは<ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>のにはとても<ruby>及<rt>およ</rt></ruby>ばない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">My bike is not anything like yours.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にひきかえ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にひきかえ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">in contrast to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun / plain form の + にひきかえ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A sharp contrast, often with an evaluation.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にひきかえ contrasts strongly; に対して is neutral.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>兄<rt>あに</rt></ruby>にひきかえ、<ruby>弟<rt>おとうと</rt></ruby>はよく<ruby>働<rt>はたら</rt></ruby>く。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In contrast to the elder brother, the younger works hard.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>去年<rt>きょねん</rt></ruby>にひきかえ、<ruby>今年<rt>ことし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>忙<rt>いそが</rt></ruby>しい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In contrast to last year, this year is busy.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にもまして</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜にもまして</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">even more than ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + にもまして</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Exceeds an already high standard.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">にもまして surpasses; より simply compares.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>去年<rt>きょねん</rt></ruby>にもまして<ruby>今年<rt>ことし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>暑<rt>あつ</rt></ruby>い。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">This year is even hotter than last.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>にもまして<ruby>健康<rt>けんこう</rt></ruby>が<ruby>大事<rt>だいじ</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Health matters more than anything.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">トムはいまや<ruby>以前<rt>いぜん</rt></ruby>にもまして<ruby>勤勉<rt>きんべん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>働<rt>はたら</rt></ruby>いている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Tom works more diligently now.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>ものにもまして<ruby>私<rt>わたし</rt></ruby>は<ruby>健康<rt>けんこう</rt></ruby>になりたい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Above all, I want to be healthy.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に堪えない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>堪<rt>た</rt></ruby>えない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">cannot bear to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + に堪えない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Too unpleasant to continue, or overwhelming gratitude.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に堪えない is literary; たくない is plain.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>読<rt>よ</rt></ruby>むに<ruby>堪<rt>こた</rt></ruby>えない<ruby>文章<rt>ぶんしょう</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is writing one cannot bear to read.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>感謝<rt>かんしゃ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>念<rt>ねん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>堪<rt>こた</rt></ruby>えません。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I am overwhelmed with gratitude.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>動物<rt>どうぶつ</rt></ruby>がいじめられているのは<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>るに<ruby>堪<rt>こた</rt></ruby>えない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I can&#x27;t stand to see animals be teased.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に至る</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>至<rt>いた</rt></ruby>る</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">reach the point of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form / Noun + に至る</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">A long process culminating in a state.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に至る is a culmination; になる is a plain change.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>長<rt>なが</rt></ruby>い<ruby>交渉<rt>こうしょう</rt></ruby>の<ruby>末<rt>すえ</rt></ruby>、<ruby>合意<rt>ごうい</rt></ruby>に<ruby>至<rt>いた</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">After long negotiations, we reached an agreement.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>議論<rt>ぎろん</rt></ruby>はついに<ruby>結論<rt>けつろん</rt></ruby>に<ruby>至<rt>いた</rt></ruby>った。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The discussion finally reached a conclusion.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に至るまで</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜に<ruby>至<rt>いた</rt></ruby>るまで</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">right down to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + に至るまで</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Emphasises the full extent of a range.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">に至るまで stresses completeness; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜の至り</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜の<ruby>至<rt>いた</rt></ruby>り</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">the height of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + の至り</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Ceremonial: 光栄の至り, 赤面の至り.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">の至り is a peak of feeling; の極み is an extreme state.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">このような<ruby>賞<rt>しょう</rt></ruby>をいただき、<ruby>光栄<rt>こうえい</rt></ruby>の<ruby>至<rt>いた</rt></ruby>りです。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Receiving such an award is the greatest honour.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>若気<rt>わかげ</rt></ruby>の<ruby>至<rt>いた</rt></ruby>りでした。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It was the folly of youth.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はさておき</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はさておき</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">setting ~ aside</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + はさておき</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Postpones one topic to address a more pressing one.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">はさておき postpones; はともかく dismisses.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>冗談<rt>じょうだん</rt></ruby>はさておき、<ruby>本題<rt>ほんだい</rt></ruby>に<ruby>入<rt>にゅう</rt></ruby>ろう。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Joking aside, let&#x27;s get to the main point.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>結果<rt>けっか</rt></ruby>はさておき、<ruby>努力<rt>どりょく</rt></ruby>は<ruby>認<rt>みと</rt></ruby>めたい。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Setting the result aside, I want to acknowledge the effort.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>はさておきもっと<ruby>健康<rt>けんこう</rt></ruby>に<ruby>注意<rt>ちゅうい</rt></ruby>するべきだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Before everything you should take better care of your health.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>冗談<rt>じょうだん</rt></ruby>はさておき、<ruby>君<rt>きみ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>何<rt>なに</rt></ruby>をするつもりなの。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Joking aside, what do you mean to do?</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はともかく</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜はともかく</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">leaving ~ aside</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + はともかく</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Dismisses one factor as less important.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">はともかく judges it less important; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ばこそ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ばこそ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">precisely because ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">ば-form + こそ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Literary emphasis on the true reason.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ばこそ is literary; からこそ is everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>愛<rt>あい</rt></ruby>すればこそ<ruby>厳<rt>きび</rt></ruby>しく<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>うのだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is precisely because I love you that I speak strictly.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>健康<rt>けんこう</rt></ruby>であればこそ<ruby>働<rt>はたら</rt></ruby>ける。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It is precisely because I am healthy that I can work.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜べからず</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜べからず</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">must not ~ (prohibition)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + べからず</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Archaic prohibition, seen on signs and notices.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">べからず is archaic; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜べく</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜べく</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">in order to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + べく (する→すべく)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Literary purpose, common in written reports.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">べく is literary; ために is everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>問題<rt>もんだい</rt></ruby>を<ruby>解決<rt>かいけつ</rt></ruby>すべく、<ruby>会議<rt>かいぎ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>開<rt>ひら</rt></ruby>いた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We held a meeting in order to solve the problem.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>真実<rt>しんじつ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>るべく<ruby>調査<rt>ちょうさ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>始<rt>はじ</rt></ruby>めた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We began an investigation in order to learn the truth.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まじき</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まじき</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">unbecoming / must not be</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + まじき + Noun</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Archaic: 許すまじき, あるまじき. <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まで (extreme)</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まで (extreme)</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">go so far as to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb て-form + まで / Noun + まで</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">An extreme measure the speaker finds notable.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">てまで is an extreme; ても is a plain concession.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>借金<rt>しゃっきん</rt></ruby>してまで<ruby>買<rt>か</rt></ruby>う<ruby>必要<rt>ひつよう</rt></ruby>はない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There&#x27;s no need to go so far as borrowing money to buy it.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>徹夜<rt>てつや</rt></ruby>してまで<ruby>仕上<rt>しあ</rt></ruby>げた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He finished it, going so far as to stay up all night.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜までもない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜までもない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">there is no need to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + までもない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The action is too obvious or minor to bother with.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">までもない is unnecessary; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まみれ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜まみれ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">smeared with ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + まみれ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Physically coated in a substance: 泥まみれ, 血まみれ.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">まみれ is physical; だらけ can be abstract.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>作業<rt>さぎょう</rt></ruby>を<ruby>終<rt>お</rt></ruby>えて<ruby>汗<rt>あせ</rt></ruby>まみれになった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">After the work I was covered in sweat.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">ほこりまみれの<ruby>本<rt>ほん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>見<rt>み</rt></ruby>つけた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I found a dust-covered book.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜もさることながら</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜もさることながら</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">~ is true, but even more so</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + もさることながら</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Acknowledges the first, then stresses the second.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">もさることながら escalates; <div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ものか</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ものか</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">as if / absolutely not</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + ものか</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Emphatic refusal or denial in speech.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ものか refuses emphatically; ない is plain.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あんな<ruby>店<rt>みせ</rt></ruby>、<ruby>二度<rt>にど</rt></ruby>と<ruby>行<rt>い</rt></ruby>くものか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">As if I&#x27;d ever go to that shop again.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>負<rt>ま</rt></ruby>けるものか。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There&#x27;s no way I&#x27;ll lose.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ものを</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ものを</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">if only ~ (regret / reproach)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + ものを</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Regret that a better outcome was missed, with reproach.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ものを reproaches; のに merely regrets.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>早<rt>はや</rt></ruby>く<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>えばよかったものを。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If only you had said so sooner.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>相談<rt>そうだん</rt></ruby>してくれればよかったものを。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">If only you had consulted me.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜や否や</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜や<ruby>否<rt>いな</rt></ruby>や</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">as soon as ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb dictionary form + や否や</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Literary; the second event follows immediately.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">や否や is written; たとたん is spoken.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>家<rt>いえ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>着<rt>つ</rt></ruby>くや<ruby>否<rt>いな</rt></ruby>や、<ruby>寝<rt>ね</rt></ruby>てしまった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">As soon as he got home, he fell asleep.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>発表<rt>はっぴょう</rt></ruby>されるや<ruby>否<rt>いな</rt></ruby>や、<ruby>話題<rt>わだい</rt></ruby>になった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The moment it was announced, it became a topic.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ゆえに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ゆえに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">because of / therefore</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun の / plain form + ゆえに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Literary causation, common in academic writing.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ゆえに is literary; から is everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>若<rt>わか</rt></ruby>さゆえに<ruby>失敗<rt>しっぱい</rt></ruby>も<ruby>多<rt>おお</rt></ruby>い。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Because of his youth, he makes many mistakes.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>我<rt>わが</rt></ruby><ruby>思<rt>おも</rt></ruby>う、ゆえに<ruby>我<rt>わが</rt></ruby>あり。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I think, therefore I am.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようによっては</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜ようによっては</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">depending on how you ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ます-stem + ようによっては</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The interpretation shifts with the approach taken.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">ようによっては is about method; 次第で is about a factor.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>考<rt>かんが</rt></ruby>えようによっては、いい<ruby>機会<rt>きかい</rt></ruby>だ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Depending on how you look at it, it&#x27;s a good opportunity.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">やりようによっては<ruby>成功<rt>せいこう</rt></ruby>する。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Depending on how it&#x27;s done, it can succeed.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜わ〜わ</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜わ〜わ</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">what with ~ and ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Plain form + わ + Plain form + わ</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Piles up troubles or excesses, colloquially.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">わ〜わ piles up; し merely lists.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>雨<rt>あめ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>降<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>るわ、<ruby>風<rt>かぜ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>吹<rt>ふ</rt></ruby>くわで<ruby>大変<rt>たいへん</rt></ruby>だった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">What with the rain and the wind, it was terrible.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>泣<rt>な</rt></ruby>くわ<ruby>叫<rt>さけ</rt></ruby>ぶわの<ruby>大騒<rt>おおさわ</rt></ruby>ぎだった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There was a huge fuss, what with crying and shouting.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をおいて</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をおいて</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">other than ~ (there is no)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + をおいて + negative</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Nobody or nothing else qualifies. Always with a negative.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">をおいて excludes all others; 以外に merely says apart from.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>をおいて<ruby>適任<rt>てきにん</rt></ruby><ruby>者<rt>しゃ</rt></ruby>はいない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is no one suitable other than him.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>今<rt>いま</rt></ruby>をおいて<ruby>機会<rt>きかい</rt></ruby>はない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">There is no opportunity other than now.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>はペンをおいて<ruby>椅子<rt>いす</rt></ruby>にもたれた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He laid down his pen and leaned back in his chair.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>遅刻<rt>ちこく</rt></ruby>したので、われわれは<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>をおいて<ruby>出発<rt>しゅっぱつ</rt></ruby>した。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He being late, we started without him.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をかわきりに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をかわきりに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">starting with ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + を皮切りに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The first of a series that follows.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">を皮切りに starts a series; をはじめ names a representative.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>東京<rt>とうきょう</rt></ruby>を<ruby>皮切<rt>かわき</rt></ruby>りに<ruby>全国<rt>ぜんこく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>回<rt>まわ</rt></ruby>る。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Starting with Tokyo, we will tour the whole country.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>開会<rt>かいかい</rt></ruby><ruby>式<rt>しき</rt></ruby>を<ruby>皮切<rt>かわき</rt></ruby>りに<ruby>行事<rt>ぎょうじ</rt></ruby>が<ruby>続<rt>つづ</rt></ruby>く。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Beginning with the opening ceremony, events continue.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をもって</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をもって</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">with / as of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + をもって</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal: a means, or an effective date in announcements.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">をもって is formal; で is everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>本日<rt>ほんじつ</rt></ruby>をもって<ruby>閉店<rt>へいてん</rt></ruby>いたします。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We will close as of today.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>書面<rt>しょめん</rt></ruby>をもってお<ruby>知<rt>し</rt></ruby>らせします。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We will inform you in writing.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>人<rt>ひと</rt></ruby>をもっているもので<ruby>判断<rt>はんだん</rt></ruby>してはいけない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Don&#x27;t judge a man by what he has.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>一時<rt>いちじ</rt></ruby>に<ruby>二<rt>に</rt></ruby><ruby>事<rt>ごと</rt></ruby>をなす<ruby>能力<rt>のうりょく</rt></ruby>をもっている。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She has a faculty for doing two things at once.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をものともしない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をものともしない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">pay no heed to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + をものともしない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">The attributive counterpart of をものともせず.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">Same sense as をものともせず, used before a noun.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>危険<rt>きけん</rt></ruby>をものともしない<ruby>勇気<rt>ゆうき</rt></ruby>があった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He had courage that paid no heed to danger.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>批判<rt>ひはん</rt></ruby>をものともしない<ruby>姿勢<rt>しせい</rt></ruby>。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">An attitude that pays no heed to criticism.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>危険<rt>きけん</rt></ruby>をものともしない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He never shrinks from danger.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をものともせず</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をものともせず</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">undaunted by ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + をものともせず</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Praises someone who overcame a hardship.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">をものともせず admires; にもかかわらず is neutral.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>困難<rt>こんなん</rt></ruby>をものともせず、<ruby>挑戦<rt>ちょうせん</rt></ruby>を<ruby>続<rt>つづ</rt></ruby>けた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Undaunted by hardship, he kept on challenging.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>嵐<rt>あらし</rt></ruby>をものともせず<ruby>出航<rt>しゅっこう</rt></ruby>した。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">They set sail undaunted by the storm.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>一行<rt>いっこう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>危険<rt>きけん</rt></ruby>をものともせず<ruby>前進<rt>ぜんしん</rt></ruby>した。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The party went forward in the face of danger.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>氷<rt>こおり</rt></ruby>のような<ruby>水<rt>みず</rt></ruby>をものともせずに<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>川<rt>かわ</rt></ruby>へ<ruby>飛<rt>と</rt></ruby>びこんだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He jumped into the river in defiance of the icy water.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をよそに</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜をよそに</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">ignoring / in defiance of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + をよそに</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Disregards others&#x27; concern or opposition, usually with disapproval.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">をよそに criticises; をものともせず admires.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>親<rt>おや</rt></ruby>の<ruby>心配<rt>しんぱい</rt></ruby>をよそに、<ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>旅<rt>たび</rt></ruby>に<ruby>出<rt>で</rt></ruby>た。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">Ignoring his parents&#x27; worry, he set off on a journey.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>反対<rt>はんたい</rt></ruby>をよそに<ruby>計画<rt>けいかく</rt></ruby>は<ruby>進<rt>すす</rt></ruby>んだ。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">In defiance of opposition, the plan went ahead.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜を余儀なくされる</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜を<ruby>余儀<rt>よぎ</rt></ruby>なくされる</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">be forced to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun + を余儀なくされる</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal: circumstances compel an unwelcome outcome.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">を余儀なくされる is passive compulsion; ざるを得ない is the speaker&#x27;s own reluctance.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>台風<rt>たいふう</rt></ruby>で<ruby>中止<rt>ちゅうし</rt></ruby>を<ruby>余儀<rt>よぎ</rt></ruby>なくされた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">We were forced to cancel because of the typhoon.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">けがで<ruby>引退<rt>いんたい</rt></ruby>を<ruby>余儀<rt>よぎ</rt></ruby>なくされた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He was forced into retirement by injury.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>大臣<rt>だいじん</rt></ruby>は<ruby>内閣<rt>ないかく</rt></ruby>からの<ruby>辞職<rt>じしょく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>余儀<rt>よぎ</rt></ruby>なくされた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The minister was obliged to resign from the cabinet.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>首相<rt>しゅしょう</rt></ruby>は<ruby>内閣<rt>ないかく</rt></ruby>からの<ruby>辞職<rt>じしょく</rt></ruby>を<ruby>余儀<rt>よぎ</rt></ruby>なくされた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">The Prime Minister was obliged to resign from the Cabinet.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜を禁じ得ない</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜を<ruby>禁<rt>きん</rt></ruby>じ<ruby>得<rt>え</rt></ruby>ない</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">cannot suppress (a feeling)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Noun (emotion) + を禁じ得ない</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Formal and literary; used with 涙, 怒り, 同情.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">を禁じ得ない is literary; ずにはいられない is broader.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">その<ruby>話<rt>はなし</rt></ruby>には<ruby>涙<rt>なみだ</rt></ruby>を<ruby>禁<rt>きん</rt></ruby>じ<ruby>得<rt>え</rt></ruby>なかった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I could not hold back tears at that story.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>怒<rt>いか</rt></ruby>りを<ruby>禁<rt>きん</rt></ruby>じ<ruby>得<rt>え</rt></ruby>ない。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">I cannot suppress my anger.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜んがため</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜んがため</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">for the purpose of ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-stem + んがため (する→せんがため)</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">Strong literary purpose.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">んがため is archaic; ために is everyday.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>生<rt>い</rt></ruby>きんがために<ruby>働<rt>はたら</rt></ruby>く。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He works in order to live.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>勝<rt>か</rt></ruby>たんがために<ruby>努力<rt>どりょく</rt></ruby>した。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He strove in order to win.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜んばかり</div>	<div style="display:inline-block;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:#6b7280;border:1px solid #d8dde6;border-radius:99px;padding:2px 10px;margin-bottom:14px">N1</div><div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.9;margin-bottom:10px">〜んばかり</div><div style="font-size:22px;color:#14532d;margin-bottom:14px">almost as if about to ~</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#475569;background:#f1f4f9;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;display:inline-block">Verb ない-stem + んばかり</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;margin-top:14px;line-height:1.7">On the very verge, in vivid literary description.</div><div style="font-size:14px;color:#8a5a2b;background:#fdf6ec;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;margin-top:12px;line-height:1.6">んばかり is literary; そうだ is plain appearance.</div><div style="font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#a7aebb;margin:22px 0 6px">Examples</div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>泣<rt>な</rt></ruby>かんばかりの<ruby>顔<rt>かお</rt></ruby>をしていた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He had a face as if about to cry.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2">あふれんばかりの<ruby>笑顔<rt>えがお</rt></ruby>だった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">It was an overflowing smile.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼女<rt>かのじょ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>喜<rt>よろこ</rt></ruby>びのあまり<ruby>跳<rt>と</rt></ruby>び<ruby>上<rt>あ</rt></ruby>がらんばかりだった。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">She fairly jumped for joy.</div></div><div style="border-top:1px solid #e6eaf0;padding:12px 0"><div style="font-size:21px;line-height:2.2"><ruby>彼<rt>かれ</rt></ruby>は<ruby>同意<rt>どうい</rt></ruby>すると<ruby>言<rt>い</rt></ruby>わんばかりにうなずいた。</div><div style="font-size:15px;color:#5b6472;font-style:italic;margin-top:4px">He nodded as much as to say, I agree.</div></div>	jlpt::N1
